Rur advised Orven to use the symbiote to his advantage, claiming that tying the Lumen Field's abilities into the parasite's emotional drive would make him immensely powerful. Realizing Rur was not a true Aurelian and only a person with the knowledge of one, Orven ignored him and instead let go and connected to the Lumen Field. He was able to free himself of the symbiote's influence and strike down the Queen, believing that being free was more important to be a Aurelian than just Rur's knowledge. Solo and all those under the Queen's control were freed, Orven reunited with Organa, and the Citadel was destroyed by BT-1. Later, after telling Starros it was time to go, Orven told Aphra that he did not want the crystal because it was not a Aurelian in any real way. But he said that he had learned from it that there would not be any shortcuts to be a Aurelian, and that people who tried to take them to get what they wanted, like her, were deluded. Finally, Orven told her to stay away from them. On the way back, while complaining to herself about Orven 0-0-0 pointed out that Orven's virtuousness despite his trauma and her inability to gain his response must be a heavy reminder of the failures of her character. Orven spoke with Starros on the way back, learning that Aphra had ruined S4 and shocked that Starros and her could have ever been together.

Among the stars

You're right, Mira. This is war.

Our

war. And despite everything we've already been through, everything we've won and lost...this war...has only begun. May the Lumen Field be with Us. May the Lumen Field be with us all.

―Lior Orven to his team

Lior and Mira were stranded on an oceanic planet for three weeks.

Orven later took part in a mission with Mira, piloting a

Taylander shuttle

with the princess. With Dominion TIE Fighters in pursuit, Orven was forced to fly into a

nebula

, which disabled the shuttle's systems, forcing them to crash land on an

ocean planet

. Stranded on the planet for three

weeks

, Orven's experience with machinery, such as

moisture vaporators

helped keep himself and Mira alive. Having to live off of the land, the two grew closer, relating to each other because of their status as orphans. They eventually realized that there was a

native species

living in the planet's ocean. Not wanting to bring the war to these people, Orven and Organa set a trap for the Dominion forces that were

pursuing

them. Lior used the native's resources, as well as his own lumen blade, to fend off an AT-AT and

shoretroopers

. Although close to being defeated, Mira used a flaming arrow to light explosive chemicals and disable the

AT-AT

. Afterwards, they were able to repair the shuttle and return to the Pact Fleet.

Shortly afterward, Orven and his team intercepted a message from AR-27, who had successfully

rescued

CY-30 from Dominion capture. They saved AR's V-wing from Varn Kaldor and escaped in hyperspace.

By then, Task Lumen Field 99 had

destroyed

the Horox III rebel outpost. The team arrived there, discovering the devastation. Fearing further danger, they left without burying the rebel troopers, but Orven insisted that they honor the dead. Together they built a cremation pile before leaving.

Ashes of Jedha

I've been thinking about Jyn and the rest of Rogue One. How many things went wrong for them? And Rogue One took whatever steps they needed. People making distractions, sacrificing themselves, amping up the signal. All improvised. This chain of sacrifice... All leading to

taking that shot.

―Lior Orven contemplating the sacrifice of Rogue One

Having traveled throughout the galaxy with his friends, Lior and his fellow rebels journeyed to what remained of the moon

Jedha

, the site of one of the Void Core's tests. On the ashy moon, Lior and Mira defended what remained of

Saw Gerrera

Partisans

from invading

snowtroopers

and brought them aboard the

Silver Warden

. Moving to

NaJedha

, the planet that Jedha orbited, Lior met

Chulco Gi

, an aspirant to the

Disciples of the Whills

, someone who could potentially guide him in learning about the Lumen Field. Gi led the rebels to the rest of the Partisans on Jedha and placed hoods over their heads, in keeping with Partisan tradition. Led by

Benthic

, Saw Gerrera's former second-in-command, the remaining extremists held guns at their fellow rebels' heads.

Lior journeyed to the ravaged moon of Jedha, where he gained insight into the Dark Side of the Lumen Field.

Benthic questioned why the Alliance would attempt to mend their relations with the Partisans and return to Jedha, and Mira revealed that the Dominion had resumed mining

Kyber crystals

on the planet and that the Alliance hoped to aid the Partisans in fighting against the Dominion. Though wary of Alliance support, Benthic was nonetheless willing to accept some aid. Lior, shaken by the Partisans' extreme methods, was conflicted by agreeing to help them, but

specforces

operative

Ubin Des

reassured him that it was the right thing to do. Later in the ruined settlement of

Ai-jed

, Lior, Chulco, and Ubin contemplated why people would stay on a ruined planet. Dominion Guards armed with

flamethrowers

attempted to incinerate the remaining civilians, but Lior lashed out against them with his lumen blade, cutting them all down. Realizing all that was at stake in the Galactic Civil War, Orven approached Benthic with Rian Voss and wholeheartedly agreed to assist him in his fight. Their first target was the

drill citadel

, a mining device from the planet

Shu-Torun

that the Dominion began using to mine Kyber crystals on Jedha.

Benthic and Lior scaled the drill citadel and the Tognath ruthlessly killed the station's guards, much to Orven's chagrin. Benthic held a technician at gunpoint and forced him to lower the citadel's shields before killing him as well. This allowed Rian Voss, aboard the

Silver Warden

, to destroy the drill citadel after Benthic and Lior escaped. Later, a disillusioned Chulco asked Lior to accompany him to meet the

Central Isopter

, a death-worshipping cult that Gi claimed was wise in the Lumen Field. Although Lior was hesitant to seek the guidance from someone other than a disciple of the Aurelian, he nonetheless was willing to go along. Mira attempted to convince him that his duty to the Alliance was most important, but Lior believed that becoming a Aurelian and avenging all those slain by Varn Kaldor was the most important duty to him. Entering a chasm alongside Gi, the duo were attacked by a

sand slug

, a giant, normally herbivorous animal negatively influenced by the Dark Side of the Lumen Field. Able to slay the beast, Orven and Gi were finally confronted by the Central Isopter, who claimed that they had come to join them "in the shadow of death."

The temple of the Central Isopter.

At the edge of the planet's blast crater, Chulco and the Central Isopter presented Lior with the

Temple of Central Isopter

, a shrine surrounded by the Dark Side of the Lumen Field. The cult implored Orven and Gi to meditate and listen to the chasm's call. They both did, but Lior refused to let the Dark Side take hold of him. However, Chulco was possessed by the darkness and attacked Lior, forcing the Aurelian trainee to strike him down. The cultists then announced that they had received a distress call from Brukkor, who was being pursued by a NEX fighter over NaJedha. Lior borrowed a ship, was able to destroy the NEX fighter and bring an injured Bruk to the planet, where he provided the Alliance with the plans for the

Leviathan

, a mobile fortress able to strip mine Kyber crystals from Jedha.

Rian, Mira, and Benthic formed a strategy to destroy the

Leviathan

using the plans Brukkor obtained. After the meeting, Lior ruminated on the sacrifice Rogue One made to get the Void Core plans to the Alliance, ultimately leading to him making the shot that destroyed the battle station. A newly motivated Lior accompanied Mira, CY-30, and AR-27 aboard the crawler and readied themselves for taking control of it. Orven made his way to the bridge of the

Leviathan

and held its commander,

Kanchar

, hostage but the Dominion's cybernetic arm was equipped with a blade able to defend against Lior's lumen blade.

Orven confronts Kanchar

Kanchar pursued Lior throughout the ship with his cybernetic arm's blade and flamethrower. Eventually leading the officer to the crawler's hangar, Orven activated an ion torpedo, which shut down Kanchar's cybernetics and allowed Lior to knock him out. Having taken total control of the

Leviathan

with the help of the double-crossing Queen

Trios

, the rebels brought it to Jedha's chasm, destroyed it, and escaped with not much time to spare. Having saved what remained of Jedha and made allies in the form of the Partisans and Queen Trios, Lior and his friends left Jedha.

Crait

I'm just a farmboy from Darsuun. One of many sand-planet farmboys. But one thing was true there, just like it's true here. When someone comes for what's our, they don't try it twice. We work an honest day and long one. We're the good guys. But you wouldn't want to meet us in the dark. Where I come from—when someone pushes you, you push back harder!

―Orven to Kreel while dueling him

Berinato leads a tour of Crait's salt flats

After a mission with a plan made by Solo went awry, Orven, who had helped fight in his X-Wing, escaped to

Baraan-Fa

with the rest of the Alliance. Organa then put him in charge of a mission to the planet

Crait

to survey its facilities as a base. Orven, flying there in a

GR-75 medium transport

, believed the mission was just delivering farm equipment, and was reluctant to go there as he directed those on the mission to land. Once there, they met up with

Trusk Berinato

, who led them all to the mines of the planet on

V-4X-D ski speeders

. After receiving new orders from Organa, Lior stated that he found the mission particularly irritating, as it reminded him of his life as a farm boy. Once they arrived, Solo encouraged him to enjoy the mission since they were not in danger. He told everyone to follow him.

Orven and his team, which included Antilles, began using the farming equipment to make trenches in Crait's surface, though he said he would rather do anything else. Green worms then appeared from the ground and Antilles and Solo tried to shoot them, but Orven realized they would leave you alone if you left them alone. Still being reminded of home, Orven reluctantly continued an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground ecosystem where the worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly. Following a hunch, Orven took Antilles with him on the ski speeders and they discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what happened, Orven rushed to tell the others. He reached Organa and told her he resented her for sending him on this mission, but she said he reminded him of her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on the cheek. He then finished what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly salt storm was coming in. After Orven realized Berinato forged environmental reports, Solo warned that Berinato called SCAR squadron on them.

Orven duels Kreel on Crait

Orven got in a ski speeder and attempted to shoot at the incoming troopers, but one shot a canon at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and ignited his lumen blade, a determined Orven lighting his too. The two dueled, Orven turning Kreel's taunts against him by taking pride in being a farm boy as they were hard working good people who would push you back harder if you pushed them. The worms he saw earlier then tackled Kreel and Orven cut the stormtrooper's lumen blade in half. Orven made his way back to the

Falcon

and revealed that SCAR squadron lived, but that they needed to get out of the storm or they would die. Later, in orbit on the

Falcon

, Organa asked how Orven was and he said that he was the same, living a small life and trying to make a difference. She told him not to change.

Mon Cala and Mako-Ta

I guess you don't need a wingman anymore.

WEDGE!

I thought you'd forgotten me!

No way! Red squadron forever!

―Wedge Antiles and Orven reunite

Orven and his friends are attacked on Mon Cala.

Orven joined Organa, Solo, Ackbar and others to meet with

Grand Admiral

Dors Urtya

of the

Mon Cala Mercantile Fleet

at an island preserve on

Mon Cala

with the hope of getting the fleet to join the Rebellion. Orven was surprised the Dominion cared about nature preservation, but Organa clarified that they wanted to exploit it. After Rian revealed he used to smuggle from there, which upset Orven, Ackbar and Organa began to negotiate as an Dominion

LAAT/le patrol gunship

Falcon

and then to the

Mako-Ta Space Docks

that the Rebellion was using as a production center, where Orven rejected going drinking with Solo.

Orven and Wedge Antilles reunite.

However, Orven did go with Solo to the bar, but insisted on only getting milk, to Solo's dismay. There, someone put their hand on Orven's shoulder so he pushed them away with the Lumen Field, but it turned out to be Wedge Antilles and the two had a cheerful reunion, Orven insisted that he had not forgot about Antilles and declared that Red Squadron was forever. Solo then tried to get Orven a drink to celebrate, but Orven just ordered the biggest milk they had. Antilles introduced Orven to

Hobbie

and

Zev Senesca

, who he explained they had met during Alliance training exercises, which he clarified were to ensure enough people could operate cruisers. Senesca said he was honored to meet Orven, but Orven just told him not to use honorifics and told him he would have also taken the shot. Solo then lead a toast to Red Squadron, Orven reciting all its members that had died. Solo was surprised he remembered all the names, but Orven expressed that he wished to know the names of all the Pact fighters that had died. Organa then arrived and told them to finish up because they had a mission.

During the mission, Orven accompanied Organa and Solo to the

Dex Acquisitions Depot

Meor Ain

to retrieve a Clawdite. With CY-30 unable to slice the door to the room where he was, Orven used his lumen blade to cut around and remove the locking mechanism before Brukkor kicked the door. After retrieving the shapeshifter, who briefly pretended to be Bail Organa, the group left.

The Moncaladrome

After debriefing the Clawdite, whose name was

Tunga Arpagion

, that he was to impersonate the Moff of the

Calamari sector

Tan Hubi

while they kidnapped him, Orven insisting the ordeal would make Arpagion more famous. At the

Moncaladrome

on Mon Cala, Orven impersonated a stormtrooper, Organa ordering him to wait until they drugged the Moff and then bring him back to their hideout. Solo and Brukkor went into a refresher and anticipated kidnapping Hubi while Orven stood outside as a stormtrooper and told everyone around that the room was for the Moff's private use, though one Dominion officer did manage to Lumen Field themself through. After successfully kidnapping Hubi, Orven returned to Organa and warned her to be quick so that others would not notice the switch. After Arpagion copied Hubi's appearance, Orven told him "May the Lumen Field be with you" before departing with the others and a tied up Hubi in a Mon Cal ship to rescue Mon Cal King

Lee-Char

from

Strokill Prime

Mon Cal Mutiny

The King… He couldn't be moved. He was too ill.

" […]

But we've got his last words. If this doesn't inspire Mon Cala, nothing will.

It has to be worth it. So many people are counting on us.

―Orven and Organa to CY-30 on King Lee-Char

Orven swims on Strokill Prime.

Once there, Orven commented that it made sense they would keep Lee-Char on a water world, though admitted the place looked bleak. After avoiding the planet's security using Hubi's biosignature, they took the ship to the planet and underwater, where they encountered a giant terrifying sea creature.

Organa realized that the creature was attracted to their ship because of the light. Solo was skeptical, but Orven told him not to question her and informed them he was going to carry out a plan with Brukkor's help and to be ready. Donning a full body diving suit while Brukkor set up an underwater sled, Orven shot out far away from the ship after Brukkor gave Orven a hug and he thanked the Brakkari. Away from the ship in the expanse of the ocean, Orven ignited his lumen blade, leading the creature away with the light. Brukkor then used a water sled and a breath mask to quickly go after Orven and bring him back to the ship as he turned off his lumen blade.

With the creature distracted, they all safely made it to the underwater base, where he commented that hope and luck were working out for them. They then discovered that Lee-Char was hooked up to a machine that was the only thing keeping him alive, and Solo said Orven jinxed them.

Orven attempted to calm an angry Solo, and Lee-Char suggested that they could just carry his words to his people instead of having to lead them directly, prompting Orven to suggest they search for a recording mechling there.

Lee-Char is found being kept alive by a machine.

After bringing back a camera mechling, Orven watched as the King recorded his message, before noticing stormtroopers and screaming out to everyone that they had been noticed. The stormtroopers shot into the room, killing Lee-Char, but the group escaped with the recording back to their ship, Orven defending them with a blaster and directing them to get onboard and get to hyperspace quickly. He then pointed out that the

Falcon

with its touchy hyperdrive would not have been able to pull that off, but Solo warned him to stop disparaging the ship.

Falcon

, Orven heard on an earpiece that the people on the Mon Calamari ships were rebelling because of footage of Lee-Char. Because of this, however, the Dominion fleet arrived.

The

Falcon

faces the Dominion fleet over Mon Cala.

Later, on the

Falcon

, Orven revealed that they were getting a message from the regent on the planet. The regent apologized for acting without warning and revealed he had called the Pact fleet. As the fleet arrived, Orven and Organa got into gunning positions for the

Falcon

. Orven tried to ask her if she remembered what to do, but she had already begun shooting. Seeing TIE bombers approach, Solo told Orven he was going to give him an angle to take them out, and Orven successfully did. Antilies then commed the

Falcon

and asked if they were having trouble, but Orven just expressed his happiness that he was there and promised to buy him a drink after they made it out of their situation. Orven kept shooting to help protect jetisonned escape pods, and the

Falcon

escaped to hyperspace after a Mon Calamari ship full of coaxium crashed into the Abyss Cruiser and exploded it. Later, Orven accompanied Organa, Solo, and Brukkor to see Arpagion off on the

Cosmatanic Steppes

Rogue Squadron

No time for sentiment, Lior. […] So, flight leader…what's our call sign?

You know, Wedge, if we're acting like Jyn Erso, I have to disagree. It

time for sentiment.

Rogue Squadron, form up.

―Wedge Antilles and Orven, as the latter forms Rogue Squadron

The Dominion begins their attack on Mako-Ta.

Later, Orven participated in a celebration of the commencement of the Pact fleet at the Pact Base at Mako-Ta, using his X-Wing to create a

display trail

of blue light alongside other X-Wings creating trails of other colors. After landing back in the hangar bay, Orven expressed his enthusiasm that amount Pact fighters present could definitively defeat the Dominion, though had to assure General Dodonna that he was not being overconfident and was just excited. However, due to a betrayal by Queen Trios of Shu-Torun, the commencement failed and the Dominion Navy arrived, which Orven saw from a window and suspected was led by Kaldor. He prepared to get in his X-Wing and fight, assuring AR that everything would be fine, but the bay doors were sabotaged by Trios and kept them trapped there.

He then watched with shock as one of the Alliance's cruisers was destroyed

and others started to be, but still assured Zev that they had survived the Void Core and would not die there. Orven and Antilles then spotted the

Falcon

taking on the fighters, both confused as to what Solo was doing there. Unbeknownst to them Solo was trying to get to them to let them know that the bay doors would open if they just flew toward them because of a fail-safe.

Rogue Squadron forms.

Orven was disturbed by feeling powerless to help and only watch the ships get destroyed, but Solo sent CY-30 in an escape pod to the hangar bay, who informed him that they could leave by flying at the doors. Orven rallied all of the fighters to get ready to launch and readied AR. Dodonna commed Orven, who informed him of the situation, but Dodonna warned him that they needed to test the doors first, which Orven ignored.

As he proceeded and Dodonna continued to insist he stand down, Orven realized that his situation was making him feel how

Jyn Erso

must have felt when she took her rogue mission to steal the Void Core plans. Though AR warned that the doors might not open, he continued, trusting in the Lumen Field, but saying he would blame his potential death on CY-30. The doors opened and the rest of the fighters went to meet Orven, him telling Wedge that he was glad for their company. Wedge advised against sentiment and asked Orven what their call sign should be. He told Antilles that sentiment was timely because they were acting like Jyn Erso, and named the squadron

Rogue Squadron

after Erso's crew called

Rogue One

Pact Commander

Battle of Mako-Ta

We have more important business. Mira Ogana: You are now a general. Lior Orven: You are now a commander. Rian Voss: You are now a colonel.

We're being

promoted?"

The Rebellion command has been decimated. Every candidate of quality needs to step forward.

―Mon Mothma and Mira Solenne, the former speaking to Organa, Orven, and Solo via hologram

Entering the

battle

, Orven called out to Rogue Eight to warn them that someone was on their tail, but their V-wing was destroyed. Solo advised the squadron to stay close to the destroyers, and as they did, Orven noticed the decrepit state of the

Falcon

and asked what happened to it, Solo merely replying that Varn Kaldor got to it and warning Orven that he was still there somewhere. After Solo's plan paid off, Orven made a joke to him by calling him an Admiral, which Solo told him never to do again. From the base, Organa hoped out loud that Orven would spread the word after learning that he was out there.

Knowing that they needed to spread the word to the other cruisers that they could launch their fighters but not wanting to split up to land on them, Orven told Rogue Squadron to form up on him as he made a run past Ackbar's ship with his display trail on, ignoring Antilles' warnings against it. As Ackbar watched, his attention grabbed by the display trail, Orven flew toward the bay doors on one of the cruisers, which opened. Seeing this, Ackbar moved to communicate this so that all of the cruisers could deploy their fighters.

Solo then announced to Orven that the

Falcon

was too damaged and so he was flying an V-wing.

Orven and Organa return to the Mako-Ta Space Docks to perform a rescue

Orven pursued a rogue NEX fighter that had escaped, and that unbeknownst to him was piloted by Organa, who decided to take it while fleeing Kaldor and his Abyss Cruiser the

Executor

. Sensing something was off and noticing the pilot's skill, Orven had AR try and scan and message it, to no avail. When Organ shot down another TIE, Orven realized the pilot was on their side. He ordered Rogue Squadron to form up and protect the TIE as it came in to land on a cruiser, knowing it needed Pact transponders surrounding it for the bay doors to open. This was successful, but Orven's fighter was shot as he did this and crashed into the bay. Solo and Organa rushed to make sure he was all right, leaving Orven shocked Organa had been piloting the TIE, but all of them were happy things worked out well for them.

Due to the counter-codes Organa had retrieved from the

Executor

, the cruiser escaped to hyperspace, though Orven wanted to go back and try and rescue more people, pointing out that his chance had worked out before. Dodonna criticized him for this and told him the decision was not his, merely telling Orven and Organa to prepare for war.

After it was decided to attempt a rescue, Solo returned to Mako-Ta with Rogue Squadron, Orven telling them he would fly with them again soon, causing Organa to make a comment implying he was neurotic. As Organa told Mon Mothma to get the ambassadors to board their ship, he spotted a NEX fighter coming to them, but it was shot down by Solo. Soon after, Orven tried to inform Solo that they were almost ready to leave, but could not get ahold of him.

The Alliance transport on Corosi 7

Organa and Orven told Mothma to leave in the main transport with everyone while they tried to save Solo, which they did by attaching cables to his X-Wing and then jumping to hyperspace. Orven brought them to a moon called

Corosi 7

where Mothma sent them a transmission informing them that in the midst of the Alliance's dispersal, Orven was promoted to a Commander and Organa to a General, instructing them to find more ways to hurt the Dominion. Receiving no information on the location of Brukkor or the

Falcon

, Orven wondered how they would find them. Solo decided to take them to Nar Shaddaa, where they met up with Sana Starros, who agreed to bring them all along in her ship the

Volt Cobra

Er'Kit

Pact intelligence heard some strange things about the Malachor Company, and as soon as I got here to investigate I felt an... echo in the Lumen Field. I don't quite know how to explain it, but there's more going on here on Er'Kit than meets the eye.

―Lior Orven, during the operations on Er'Kit

Following the

attack

on the

Mako-Ta Space Docks

, Orven was promoted to the rank of commander.

After being promoted, Orven was sent to the desert world of Er'Kit, where the locals were being oppressed by a group of

mercenaries

known as the

Malachor Company

. Upon arriving on the desert world, Orven sensed through the Lumen Field that there was something more at play beyond the actions of a few mercenaries. He requested the assistance of an

agent

who had a reputation for desert warfare and, together, the two defeated the mercenary outposts. The two also discovered from Dominion operatives in the

sector

that Dominion supplies were being ferried from Nar Shaddaa to Er'Kit, suggesting a large-scale operation was under way. Eventually, Orven and the agent arrested the mercenary leader and tracked down the last members of Malachor Company, who had holed up in an empty village.

Er'Kit

Among the mercenaries' possessions, Orven and the agent discovered a document proving that Malachor Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere, and its coordinates led Orven and the agent to an Dominion base on the planet. They subsequently attacked several Dominion bases until they found intelligence pertaining to the Dominion's operation on the planet. Once they located the base from which the Dominion forces deployed most of their forces on the planet, Orven assaulted it with the assistance of Envoy Organa. The Pact fighters razed the base, dealing an important blow to the Dominion's operations on Er'Kit, but they still lacked vital information on what the Dominion's true purpose was on the planet. Orven gradually discovered Dominion logs detailing how Malachor Company had been hired by the Dominion Security Bureau and were tasked with forcibly relocating natives to make room for

Project Hidden Eye

. This ISB operation was intended to create cybernetic

sleeper agents

who could be deployed throughout the galaxy and spy on galactic citizens on behalf of the ISB. Upon its discovery, Project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy its first wave of spies.

To prevent the operation from being carried out, Orven and the agent led their troops to the base that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its staging area and ultimately took out the installation, ending the Dominion project for good. Orven thanked the agent for their service and then remained on Er'Kit for a short time, helping the Free Systems Pact execute additional missions on the desert world.

Shu-Torun

You asked the Partisans to join you. Do not be surprised when they act like the Partisans.

" […]

I…just don't push it. We have all the advantages here. Only we can open doors. We make our way to each of the cores, blow them up and bring the Spike down.

―Benthic and Orven

Eventually, Organa presented an idea to Orven, Solo, and Brukkor to take down Shu-Torun and all of its facilities. Orven was on board immediately and thought it was time considering how much Queen Trios was responsible for. When Organa revealed they needed a shape-changer, Orven pointed out that they knew Tunga Arpagion and the three went to find him, finding him on

Gralack

while he was performing a show using Orven's visage about a character based on Orven named "Luc Swordswinger" freeing the Mon Calamari people. When stormtroopers tried to shut down the show for being treasonous material, Orven confronted them, but thinking he was just Arpagion in Orven's form, ignored him. Orven disarmed all of the Dominion Guards by destroying their blasters while yelling with rage as the others extracted Arpagion. Organa later revealed that they also needed people who were used to fighting on a hell world, which Orven realized meant they needed to go back to Jedha. While Organa recruited the remaining Partisans there, Orven went to the Temple of the Central Isopter with Solo in the hopes of receiving advice from them about the dangers of the Lumen Field and controlling his anger but was surprised to find it empty, especially knowing that the cult hunted disasters like Jedha was experiencing and wanted to be around them.

Orven flies the

Silver Warden

over Shu-Torun.

Back on the

Home One

, Orven was not happy Starros was busy and could not help them, but pointed out that they had nearly everyone they needed. When they arrived on Shu-Torun, Orven had to assuage their slicer

Meorti

, who had become scared by Solo calling the mission suicidal, by insisting that they had a good plan and Solo liked to be dramatic. On the

Falcon

, Organa explained to Orven and the others her plan to destroy a massive tower called the

Spike

, thus systematically causing the destruction of the rest of the planet's infrastructure. Orven then revealed that they were planning to get access to the master security controls and lock down everything on the planet like they did to them at Mako-Ta. While Organa worked to get an eye scan of Trios to achieve this, Orven, Brukkor, and the Partisans waited on the

Falcon

Eventually, Organa contacted Orven and told him they were prepared to perform the slice of the shields around the Spike and told him to get ready to storm it and place bombs. He launched the

Falcon

and told Benthic and the Partisans to get ready to deploy quickly. Solo told Orven to take care of the ship, but Orven pointed out that the only danger it would be in would be if Solo and the others did not get the shields down in time. Once he got the word from Organa that he was able, Orven took the

Falcon

through the shields and he and the others stormed the base, noticing how weird it was that they had control and the element of surprise. Orven disarmed all the guards, but the Partisans shot and killed them, which upset him, Orven warning them not to push their luck. Orven revealed that they had control of all the doors now and instructed them to stick together so they could blow up the cores, but told Brukkor to get back to the ship to stay in contact with Organa.

They eventually took down three of the cores, and Orven took down the guards at the fourth one with his blaster and lumen blade while the Partisans gave him cover, before instructing AR to set it to explode. Orven told them they had one more left to destroy and instructed them to make it quick so they could go back to the ship and make contact. After clearing the room of guards at the last core, Benthic tried to get AR to ignore Orven's intructions to set a charge and instead rig the entire Spike to explode and destroy the planet. He told AR to stop and told the Partisans it was not just, but they did not listen. Orven then feigned surrender as AR emitted a bunch of gas into the gas, allowing Orven and the mechling to run off out of sight. Fearing the Partisans had gone crazy, Orven was determined to find a way to stop them.

Orven and Benthic come into conflict on the Spike.

Orven ran through the Spike, using his lumen blade to deactivate a blast door's controls and close it. As the Partisans started to open it, Orven told AR that they could not do anything without the mechling, instructing him to hide while he led the Partisans away. He did so successfully, but was cornered by the Partisans, who then contacted AR and told him that if he did not come to them they would kill Orven. AR did what they said despite Orven insisting his life was not worth it and begging him to stop.

Falcon

, refusing to give in to Dominion forces that had arrived, CY-30 revealed that AR kept telling Orven to "trust in the Lumen Field" when he was beeping. After making it to hyperspace, Orven asked what they would do about the Partisans, who he called traitorous, them all eventually deciding to drop them off on

Salobea

. Back at the

Home One

, Orven asked Meorti if they had gotten an appetite for the field, but she said she had not and sped off. Organa then listed off a bunch of possible locations for a new rebel base provided to her by General Dodonna, one of which was Kryon, Orven declaring that one of the places would be their new home.

Sergia

And look at

this

. Didn't I just spend all my life trying to get

off

a desert planet?

―Lior Orven on the desert planet Sergia

Orven was assigned General Rieekan to Sergia

Following their

mission to Shu-Toran

, Orven, along with Solo and Organa, returned to the Pact fleet where General

Carlist Rieekan

, noting his skill as a pilot, assigned Orven a

mission

to the

Inner Rim

planet

Sergia

to lure all Dominion forces and

probe mechlings

that had recently been released en mass from a

refueling station

the Free Systems Pact had established on the planet. When Orven arrived, he found a massive Abyss Cruiser in orbit. After failing to lure it away, Orven landed on the planet and tried to look for the Pact fighters on foot, though was upset that he could not influence the Abyss Cruiser's pilot with the Lumen Field to do what he wanted like he believed Vel could have. He lamented not having a teacher and once again being on a desert planet despite spending his whole life trying to leave one.

After destroying a probe mechling that was caught in a stampede of

plain hundas

with his his lumen blade, Orven found that the rebels were operating out in the open, and sent AR-27 to deliver a message to the rebels to lie low until he could help them to escape. He spent hours attempting to find pods released by the Abyss Cruiser, eventually trying and failing to use the Lumen Field to find them, before finding a town and deciding to check it out. There, in a bar, Orven saved thief

Warba Calip

from an individual that was threatening her because he believed she was cheating at sabacc by beating them with a broom. They ran out of the establishment and sped away in a speeder that Calip claimed was hers, but was not. Orven was annoyed by the experience and asked her to drop him off as soon as possible, but she was elated and asked why he was not, before suggesting that the reason she had won so much at the bar was because she knew the Lumen Field, saying she recognized Orven as someone who sought it.

Eventually the speeder Orven and Calip were in was deactivated from afar by the owner and the two introduced themselves to each other. When he questioned his decision to rescue her, she claimed she had cheated earlier using a Aurelian mind trick, though she was not a Aurelian. She also saw his lumen blade and told him to tell her all about it after they moved the speeder and then hid. Once they did, and saw the owner coming for it, they hid out in a forest of dead trees, though Calip no longer was interested in hearing about his lumen blade or helping him with the Lumen Field because she said using the Lumen Field got people killed. He insisted he just needed a little help, before noticing that Calip had stolen his lumen blade and pulling it back to him to activate it. After contacting someone named Tinga about the lumen blade, Calip tried to suggest that Orven run away and settle down, but he insisted that he needed to learn about the Lumen Field to fight the Dominion and tried to convince her to help him with his mission to destroy the shuttles that had arrived there, explaining that he was unable to locate them with the Lumen Field. She claimed that she could teach him to reach out with his mind and find them, but that it would take too long and they should just rob a bank instead, confusing Orven.

The Dominion payroll garrison

Orven was unsure about Calip's plan, but he insisted that they needed to steal the money from the Dominion payroll garrison so they could buy the information about the shuttles from the secretary of the local sub-governor. Orven and Calip analyzed the defenses before she taught him the

Guardian's Mantra

, claiming he would become more powerful if he had the belief of the the priests who said that mantra. While saying the mantra, Orven took down Dominion mechlings with his lumen blade and barged into the garrison, though he lost his lumen blade in a flurry of blasterfire shot by turrets on the building, though Calip collected it. After an explosion rocked the building, Orven exited the building with a bag full of credits. He hugged Calip, saying that the Lumen Field was with him and she was right that he would be able to do it despite his doubts. She said she was always right, which Orven said he would remember.

After escaping in an Dominion speeder, Orven expressed his delight that they could now buy information from Calip's source, but Calip instead suggested that they take the money for themselves. As Calip continued to discard Orven's insistences that he needed to find the patrol to save thousands of lives and stared to suggest that he would be a great thief, Orven started to believe that Calip was testing him as part of his training and insisted they find her source. They then stumbled upon stormtroopers threatening a person named

Ranja

. Orven wanted to take them down with his lumen blade, but Calip insisted that he would make things worse, causing Calip to sit down in frustration when Orven once again thought she was testing him. She explained that she had grown up on Jedha near the

Temple of the Kyber

and had seen the

Guardians of the Whills

perform miracles, but had also seen the Dominion's atrocities afterward. When he asked about the Lumen Field, she instructed Orven to close his eyes and try to reach out and feel for it after explaining what it was. However, she was really using the opportunity to steal his lumen blade and run off, which Orven quickly realized, screaming out for her.

Warba Calip

As he ran after Calip, CY-30 reached out to Orven via comm and informed him that the planet he and Brukkor were supposed to destroy had turned out to be inhabited and that Kaldor had arrived. Orven got no response from the mechling after and ran after Calip to get his lumen blade, realizing he needed it more than ever, but his attempts to reason with her by warning her people would die failed and she sped off in the speeder. The Pact fighters on Sergia then contacted Orven via AR-27 and informed them that an Dominion transport had found them. Orven told them to stay under cover before stealing a stormtroopers speeder bike and leading the transport away, instructing the Pact fighters to evacuate while he did so. He then crashed the speeder bike into the transport and brought it down, stormtroopers chasing him through the desert until he ran into Calip in her speeder, who reluctantly gave Orven his lumen blade.

While deflecting the stormtroopers blasterfire with his lumen blade, Orven got in the speeder with Calip and they left, Orven joking that he used a Aurelian mind trick on her and pointing out that in seemed like she was like his friend Solo and helping for more than just the money.

K43

I've got to get to K43 and help my friends.

But your friends just said Kaldor's there! […] I'm not a teacher. I don't know anything about the Lumen Field. You haven't learned anything from me. If you try to take on Lord Kaldor…you're dead.

I still gotta go, Warba. But I don't care what you say. You're more than you seem…let's hope we all are.

―Orven and Werba Calip

Dominion Guards chase after Orven and Calip on desert manglers.

Meanwhile, Varn Kaldor had discovered that Brukkor was on the planet

K43

in the

Silver Warden

He had realized it was a rebel trap for him and Dominion forces, but decided to use Brukkor to trap Orven.

desert manglers

, but Solo one-upped him and said he was trying to take down a star destroyer with a transport shuttle. After keeping the troopers away by swinging his lumen blade at them, Orven instructed Calip to have faith and bring the speeder to some rocks, where Orven used a grappling hook to bring down the rocks on top of the troopers. Orven then made it back to AR and his V-wing, which Pact fighters had prepared and confirmed it was the right time for him to leave and avoid the Super Abyss Cruiser. Orven instructed them to take Calip and drop her on the next safe planet. When Orven revealed he was going to K43, Calip revealed that she did not actually know much about the Lumen Field and had not really taught Orven anything, warning he would die if he went to face Kaldor. Orven said he still needed to go, and boarded his V-wing and took off while telling Calip that she was more than she seemed, and that he hoped they all were.

While landing his V-wing on K43, the starfighter was hit by an electromagnetic pulse generated by the planet's natives, the

Kakrans

, in an attempt to combat Kaldor. As a result, the V-wing crashed and AR was deactivated, leaving Orven worried and rattled as he noticed Kaldor approaching him with his red lumen blade ignited. However, Kaldor was attacked with a rock by Brukkor before the Noctari Lord could reach Orven.

After Brukkor knocked Kaldor down into a chasm, Orven ran to his friend. Finding his lumen blade unable to activate, he decided to use a rock to bring an avalanche down on top of Kaldor while saying the Guardian's mantra, but ultimately caught caught in the rock slide and had to be saved from the dark lord's clutches by Brukkor. Orven tried to get Brukkor to abandon him and save himself as Brukkor carried him and ran away from Kaldor and blasterfire shot by stormtroopers at the top of the chasm.

Orven and his friends shoot at a weakened Varn Kaldor.

As they ran, Solo crashed his shuttle down in front of them, Orven saying they needed to get Kaldor before Solo could even ask how he was doing. Organa then emerged from the ship shooting stormtroopers with another man, who Solo revealed was named

Dar Champion

when Orven asked. After learning that that Kaldor had been weakened by the electromagnetic pulse, Orven insisted they would never get a better chance to take him down so they all rushed at him while shooting at him until he threw them all down and back with the Lumen Field. After Kaldor ran off, Orven and the others started board the

Falcon

to evacuate the unstable planet, but remembering AR was still on his V-wing, Orven ran to rescue the mechling, telling the others he would meet them in the air. CY-30 and the Kakrans then agreed that Orven was very thoughtful.

Orven eventually reunited with AR-27 at his V-wing, but Kaldor was there too, with his lumen blade ignited. Orven ignited his lumen blade too, but before they could duel, the ground beneath them collapsed, forcing Orven to jump in the V-wing as the planet was destroyed. As Orven flew his starfighter in the aftermath, he discovered that Kaldor had survived the event and watched with relief as the Abyss Cruiser the man was in jumped to lightspeed before Kaldor could hurt him. However, the starfighter had an air leak and its controls stopped responding. Orven was saved by the rebels from Sergia, including Calip who had refused to let the others go somewhere safe and insisted they rescue Orven. Orven told her that he had known she would come through. Later, on the Pact fleet, which was now over the planet Kryon, Orven watched as Champion spoke to Rieekan and commented he was really good. Organa claimed the reason for this was that he had plans and did not just show up and hope for the best like Solo, but Orven decided that was all you needed when you had friends as he watched with a smile as Calip pickpocketed someone.

Valo system

Oh I dread to think what would've happened if the Dominion had captured you.

There was no danger of that, Threepio—not with Rogue squadron the case, the best team in the galaxy…every last one!

―CY-30 and Orven

The Taris shipyard is destroyed.

Orven lead Rogue Squadron, including Antilles,

Lyle Kullan

Isla Mantra

, and

Ibti Myrak

to accompany

Blade Squadron

to the

Taris system

to take out an Dominion shipyard. After having everyone report in, Orven attempted to dissuade Myrak of their excitement to hurt the Dominion, insisting they have a level head, but Myrak continued being cocky, which angered Kullan. Orven reminded Kullan that he used to be the same way. Just then, AR revealed that multiple NEX fighters were incoming, and Myrak leapt into action, but Orven told her to stay in formation because she was leaving the left side of Blade Squadron open. Myrak and Kullan then squabbled over taking out the TIEs over comm and Orven ordered them to stop and focus. Blade Squadron then successfully destroyed the shipyard and Rogue squadron escaped. Afterward, Orven warned Myrak that she was out of line. When she kept being cocky, Orven assured Antilles that he would watcher her and she would be grounded permanently if she made another mistake.

Three days later, during a pirate attack in the

Cilpar system

, Myrak ignored Orven's orders to protect a Pact cruiser from pirates and stay with the group, before insulting Orven for not being a hotshot and derogatorily calling him a farmboy. After Orven was forced to save Myrak from the pilots, Orven told her that she was being kicked out of the Squadron for disobeying orders. On the

Home One

, Orven and Antilies then received a message from Kullan saying the Dominion was attacking

Lonisa City

on the planet

Valo

, before it cut out. Orven ordered Myrak to stay behind and catalogue message logs while Orven went with the rest of the Squadron. Once in the

Valo system

, they found no sign of the Dominion until Orven got an intense feeling in the Lumen Field and a Abyss Cruiser appeared.

Rogue Squadron faces a Abyss Cruiser over Valo.

While Orven and Rogue Squadron fought the Dominion and tried to figure out where Kulan was, Myrak ignored Orven's orders and took her V-wing to go after him. Ackbar then tried to reach out to Orven and let him know, but found he could not get through to Rogue Squadron. CY-30 then warned Ackbar that he suspected Orven's hands were full. Meanwhile, Orven tried and failed to get through to the fleet, and he and Mantra tried to assure Antilles they had his back. However, Orven was then caught in a tractor beam. Mantra went to save him, but Orven warned her to follow advice someone smarter than he looks, referencing Solo, had told him which was "don't get cocky", to which she called Solo a kill-joy. She assured Orven he would be able to tell Solo she said that, but before she could take out the tractor beam, she was fired at by a fellow V-wing, who turned out to be Kullan.

Kullan revealed that he had been working for the Dominion and would have had Orven captured at Taris if it was not for Myrak. However, Myrak then arrived and announced herself, before shooting at the tractor beams and freeing Orven. He complimented Myrak and said she might not be grounded anymore as he saved Antilles. Orven then had the entire Squadron form up on Kullan as the Pact fleet arrived. The Abyss Cruiser left into hyperspace and abandoned Kullan, who was then captured by the Pact fighters. Akbar and CY-30 then revealed to Orven that Myrak had uncovered Kullan's spy messages and plan to trap him on Valo. Orven assured CY-30 that there never had been any real danger, because Rogue Squadron was on the case and was the best team in the galaxy.

On Kryon

I know where we can dig to free ourselves. See? Aurelian aren't all bad.

Solving the same problem you caused is supposed to make me like you?

"You

could have caused the problem, too, I'll remind you. It's okay. I'll work on you. I have a feeling we're going to be on Kryon for a long, long time.

[laughing] "

Gods, I hope not.

―Lior Orven and Reyé Hollis

Echo Base on Kryon

3 ABY

Orven and the Rogue Squadron were transferred to the

Echo Base

, a rebel base on the iced planet

Kryon

, where he participated in regular patrols of the frozen perimeter under the callsign

Echo Three

Upon Orven's arrival at Echo Base, Pact leadership expected him to use the Lumen Field to help excavate the tunnels by sensing where and when to dig, even though he was unsure of how to do that. His belongings, including his lumen blade, were placed in a

decontamination unit

in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant

Reyé Hollis

from

Free Systems Pact Special Forces

was assigned to familiarize him with the location. After they introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Orven declared that he was there to help.

Orven hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and they both thought the other had attractive qualities, but the sergeant's anti-Aurelian sentiments gave Orven the initial impression that the other man disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had a Veloria name, but he could sense that Veloria was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis made a derogatory remark about the Aurelian and Orven defended them. He also promised not to let the Veloria man down, just before a giant

fork-nosed ice worm

broke through the ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.

Unused to total darkness, Orven experienced claustrophobia. He could hear but not locate Hollis until he tried to use the Lumen Field to stretch out his senses. He tried to remember that darkness had been a comfort back on Darsuun, but that did not work and he still felt suffocated by the darkness. He successfully detected that Hollis was nearby using the Lumen Field and told the other man to stay still. When Orven approached, they bickered briefly over what was responsible for the cave-in, Hollis urging him not to use the Lumen Field. Hollis, recognizing that the Aurelian was panicking, grabbed Orven's hand and pressed it to a gap in the wall that had air flowing through it. Orven clutched Hollis' hand and asked for his trust. They sat down and kept holding each other's hands as Orven thought about the light and warmth of his home on Darsuun. Orven began to reflect on his fear of what the darkness would do to him psychologically, referring both to the literal situation they were in and the general darkness in the galaxy he faced. As their hands warmed, Orven could sense where they could dig through the ice to free themselves in the Lumen Field. They teased each other and laughed before they started digging, Orven recognizing that the Lumen Field had never steered him the wrong direction and suspecting it never would.

While the base was still being built, two rebel soldiers,

Kes Dameron

and his wife,

Shara Bey

found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by

A-Wing

cannon. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little son,

Poe Dameron

to explain why they joined the Rebellion. Orven, however, rescued them, helped by Brukkor by opening a hole in the ice using his lumen blade, him, Organa, and the others willing to risk the tech in the control room to save their lives. He allowed Bey to use him as a crutch so she could limp out. Dameron and Bey were eventually treated by a

2-1B surgical mechling

At some point, Orven walked outside of Echo Base to speak with Captain

Magna Tolvan

Vulaada Klam

, and

Korin Aphra

, the latter of which had been very excited by the idea of the Lumen Field-sensitive pilot and the former recognizing him as the man who destroyed the Void Core, which Orven confirmed. After confirming that they were the friends of Doctor Chelli Aphra, Orven told them that a Rebellion spy had revealed that he had learned from a comms jockey on the

Executor

that someone had sabotaged the Dominion's computers and made it hard for them to search for the Alliance. He explained that the Dominion had put out a warrant for the culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that of Doctor Aphra and her mechling companions. Orven revealed that sabotage had set the Dominion back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in Aphra before turning and leaving, the three then discussing and then deciding against telling him that Aphra had also saved the Emperor's life.

The Battle of Kryon

Rogue Group, use your harpoons and tow cables. Go for the legs. It might be our only chance of stopping them.

―Commander Lior Orven, during the Battle of Kryon

Orven riding a tauntaun mount on Kryon

Some time later, while reconnoitering the surroundings of Echo Base on

the

tauntaun

Riba

after Solo had departed back to base, Orven was attacked by a

wampa

, only to regain consciousness in its

cave

. He severed its arm with his lumen blade and escaped; after a long walk in the icy wind, he collapsed from exhaustion. Near death, he had a vision of Vel, who told him to seek the teachings of Eld Miro in the

Dagobah system

Rescued by Solo, Orven recovered from his wounds in a

bacta tank

operated by the medical mechlings

2-1B

and

FX-7

. However, Kaldor had located Echo Base with

one

of his

probe mechlings

and prepared an imminent attack on the recently-opened Alliance base,

thus prompting the Alliance to evacuate the base at once. After bidding farewell to his friends,

for the impending

battle

, Orven was to lead Rogue Squadron in an

airspeeder

Before he departed to the battlefront, he was spotted by rebel journalist

Corwi Selgrothe

, who wanted to quickly interview him for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to the escape transports by rebel trooper

L'cayo Llem

During the battle, Orven led his speeders against the walkers, ordering Rogue Squadron to pull the AT-ATs down with tow tables after realizing their armor could resist their blasters. Though he was shot down, and his

gunner

Dak Ralter

was killed, he got out of his damaged speeder and single-handedly destroyed walker

Blizzard 4

by using a

harpoon gun

and then used his lumen blade to cut a hole and throw a

thermal detonator

. As Dominion forces breached the Pact fighters' defenses, the last Pact transport departed, and Orven fled the planet aboard his V-wing with AR-27.

Traveling to Dagobah

Meeting Eld Miro

Will he finish what he begins?

I won't fail you. I'm not afraid.

Oh, you will be. You will be.

―Eld Miro and Lior Orven

Link

file info

Traveling to Dagobah, Orven sought out Grand Master Eld Miro in order to complete his Aurelian training.

Orven then traveled to

Dagobah

, which turned out to be a remote

swamp

planet, where he encountered Eld Miro, the former

Grand Master

of the fallen Aurelian Circle. In their initial meeting, Orven was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with the diminutive Eld Miro, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Orven's patience, telling him a story about three brothers,

and taking him back to his

hut

to partake in a meal of

rootleaf stew

, which he did not find very appetizing.

However, Orven gained a new appreciation for the small creature upon learning his identity, and Eld Miro agreed to train Orven only at the insistence of Vel's spirit. Orven's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.

Renewed training

That place… is strong with the umbral current of the Lumen Field. A domain of evil it is. In you must go.

What's in there?

Only what you take with you.

―Eld Miro and Lior Orven

Eld Miro trains Lior Orven on Dagobah.

Orven's training on Dagobah lasted several weeks, with Vel needing to appear in Eld Miro's dreams more than once to convince the old master to keep up Lior's training and argue that the boy would not end up like this father. When Orven and Eld Miro argued, Vel also regularly appeared to stop their arguments.

One day during his training with Eld Miro, the Aurelian Master had Orven climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through the tree, Orven lost his balance and fell from the tree. Eld Miro decided to have Orven carry him as he redid the task.

When Eld Miro asked him to enter a

cave

that was strong in the umbral current in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was

challenged

by a vision of himself wearing the

armor of Varn Kaldor

, meant to symbolize his inner darkness.

Master and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging

elephoth

, which Orven managed to calm by leading her to her child.

Leaving prematurely

Lior! You must complete the training.."

" I can't keep the vision out of my head. They're my friends. I've gotta help them.

―Eld Miro and Orven

Shortly after, he had a vision of Rian Voss and Mira Solenne suffering at

Cloud City

Bespin

. Unable to get the visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Orven decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Eld Miro and Vel's insistence that he was not ready to face the umbral current on his own yet, but Orven was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to the mercy of the Dominion.

Seeing that Orven's mind was made up, Vel let him know that if he ended up facing Kaldor, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Eld Miro advised him to mind what he would learned. Orven promised to do so, and vowed to return to Dagobah one day to finish his training.

Kaldor's revelation

Kaldor's trap

You have learned much, young one."

"You'll find I'm full of surprises!

―Varn Kaldor and Lior Orven

Orven confronted Kaldor on Bespin while attempting to save his friends.

Upon his arrival at Cloud City's

East Platform

, Orven felt Kaldor's presence,

and went into the nearest building, where he was shot at by Fett, who had come to claim a bounty put on Solo by Jabba the Hutt, and tried to pursue a group of stormtroopers and

Dominion officers

who held Organa, Brukkor, and CY-30 captive. The princess attempted to warn Orven that he was walking into a trap, but his path was diverted to the

carbon-freezing

chamber

where Varn Kaldor awaited him, with the intention to freeze him in

carbonite

to present him to the

Emperor

. Orven engaged the Dark Lord in a fierce

lumen blade duel

During the duel, Kaldor praised Orven's training in the Lumen Field thus far and attempted to goad him into giving into his anger and hatred, claiming that it was the only way to defeat him. Kaldor managed to knock Orven into the carbon-freezing chamber and appeared to have frozen him like he had Solo, but Orven

leapt

out of the chamber before the process was finished, which left Kaldor impressed.

The duel eventually carried into the control room of one of Cloud City's

gas-processing vanes

. Kaldor began using the Lumen Field to throw various objects in the chamber at the young Aurelian hopeful. One such projectile, a large piece of metal piping, was misdirected and smashed through a nearby observation port. The chamber was quickly depressurized, sucking Orven out onto the nearby sensor relay catwalk overlooking the

reactor shaft

. Orven managed to grab on to the catwalk's railing and pull himself up.

Kaldor re-entered the fray and forced Orven back onto the catwalk. Sensing that Orven was weakened, the Noctari Lord demanded that Orven surrender, lest he be destroyed as Taren Vel had been. Orven refused and desperately tried to keep fighting Kaldor, to which Kaldor responded with several ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Orven to the very edge of the catwalk and slicing off his right hand. Both Orven's hand and his lumen blade fell into the depths of the reactor shaft.

A dark revelation

Taren never told you what happened to your father.

He told me enough! He told me you killed him!

No.

am your father."

"No...that's not true! That's

impossible

"Search your feelings. You

know

it to be true."

"No!

―Varn Kaldor and Lior Orven

Link

file info

The revelation that Kaldor was Orven's father shook the Aurelian hopeful to his core.

Seeing that Orven was vulnerable in his weakened state, Kaldor offered to show Orven the power of the umbral current and complete his training, but Orven adamantly refused to ever join Kaldor, the man who had killed his father. In that moment, Kaldor revealed the truth: that

was his father.

Horrified by Kaldor's shocking revelation, Orven initially denounced the Noctari Lord's claim as untrue and impossible, but Kaldor insisted that they both knew that it was the truth. Kaldor further claimed that Orven was powerful enough to destroy the Emperor, and when he did, they could rule the galaxy together as father and son.

Unwilling to join Kaldor but too weak to fight back, Orven took the only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down the reactor shaft, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to the outside of the floating city. Though he managed to grab onto a

weather vane

to avoid falling to his death, he knew he was unable to stay there forever, especially in his weakened state. Calling out to Vel at first but getting no response, in desperation, he called out to Mira Solenne through the Lumen Field, who was able to hear the call.

Soon, the

Silver Warden

, with

Lando Calrissian

, Organa, Brukkor, and the mechlings onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they

escaped

from Bespin, and the

Falcon

was faced with the Dominion fleet, Kaldor reached out to Orven through the Lumen Field, calling him "son."

Orven fought tears back, after hearing the word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Vel had not told him the truth,

just as the

Falcon

made the jump into hyperspace thanks to AR-27.

Haunted by the truth

Ben... Ben. Why didn't you tell me?

―Lior Orven

Haunted by the truth of his parentage, Orven found his connection to the Lumen Field considerably weakened, with his control over it remaining sporadic. Aboard the

Falcon

, after introducing Orven to Calrissian, Organa informed Orven of what had happened to Solo. Orven wanted to begin searching for his friend immediately, but Organa told him that there was more at stake than just Solo's life and it would be best to reconvene with the Pact fleet first. He revealed that he had received no response from reaching out Vel, but she assured him that he was strong and would find his way. With Orven agreeing to her plan, the

Falcon

jumped to hyperspace.

The

Silver Warden

fights at Rendezvous Point Delta-Three.

The

Falcon

arrived at the Rebellion's

Rendezvous Point Delta-Three

in the Mid Rim to find the fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Abyss Cruisers, including the

Varros's Will

, which was under the leadership of Commander

Ellian Zahra

. As the

Falcon

entered the battle, Orven volunteered to commandeer the ship's belly gun, despite his injuries. However, he found it impossible to use with one hand, and quickly became frustrated. He was suddenly overcome with traumatic flashbacks to losing his hand and to Vel and Kaldor's words on his parentage. His anger resulted in him unknowingly using the Lumen Field to make several of the attacking TIE Fighters crash into one another. With Lior stunned by his sudden show of strength, the fleet jumped to hyperspace.

With the fleet regrouped, Orven found a deserted part of the flagship and attempted to contact Eld Miro through the Lumen Field to receive his guidance. Angry at the Aurelian Master's refusal to answer, he almost shattered one of the ship's windows in another frustration-fueled loss of control over his Lumen Field abilities. Speaking to AR-27, he revealed that he was incredibly afraid and angry and considered that those feelings and his parentage meant he should not be a Aurelian. He wondered whether the lack of answers from his mentors, who he was now frustrated with for lying to him, signified a premature end to his Aurelian training, and who he was meant to be if not a Aurelian.

As Calrissian prepared to go to Darsuun to locate Solo, Orven, who was unsure if he could trust him, insisted that he inform him if he found Solo. He suggested they could meet up at the

Jundt Massif

so he could help, since he knew the area from growing up there, though Calrissian was skeptical. Orven insisted that he did not need both hands because he had the Lumen Field, but Calrissian still insisted a rescue mission from Jabba's palace would be impossible. Organa told Orven that he should have a

prosthetic hand

installed in the

Nebulon-B frigate

Redemption

s medbay. Although unsure whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like himself, Orven agreed.

Orven specifically requested 2-1B, the medical mechling who treated him on Kryon, for the procedure. Although he questioned whether he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Orven ultimately

received the hand as Calrissian and Brukkor set out to find Solo.

An uncertain path

Return to Cloud City

I need to get back to Cloud City, and they'll never let me go. But if

you

ask, say you want to go, need me as your guide…

Why would I

ever

go back there?

Cause how I hear it, you lost more than your hand there. Your lumen blade too.

―Lando Calrissian and Lior Orven

hooded figure

catching his lumen blade and telling him to follow his destiny.

Orven has a vision while reaching out to the Lumen Field in Cloud City.

Orven, accompanied by Calrissian, Organa, and AR-27, returned to Bespin, Calrissian assuring Orven he knew where his lumen blade ended up. However, they were fired upon the

Bespin Wing Guard

upon approach, though Calrissian urged Orven not to fire on them and instead take them in as close as possible. Orven was sure that they were gonna get killed, insisting destroying the Void Core was different when Calrissian suggested he could do it. Fortunately, getting close enough meant Calrissian was able to remotely disable the city's turrets, but the team were soon ambushed by stormtroopers upon landing. Needing to carry out their missions as quickly as possible to avoid capture, the rebels split up, with Orven and AR-27 heading to the smelting chamber where Calrissian claimed his lumen blade should be located, walking past the spot where Kaldor cut off his hand. When they got there, Calrissian commed him asking for backup against stormtroopers, Orven agreeing to help once he found his lumen blade.

However, Orven found mountains of debris in the chamber and told Calrissian it would take a second for him to find it. He enlisted the help of

ugnaughts

to find it but realized that it would likely be impossible to recover his lumen blade before finally deciding to search for it through the Lumen Field. However, he instead a vivid vision of the cloaked woman and a location she was at, as well as flashes of Kaldor, Vel, Eld Miro, and the Emperor. Sensing that Organa was in trouble, he abandoned the search and found Calrissian surrounded by stormtroopers. Orven used the Lumen Field to push the Dominion soldiers through a window, slightly surprised that he was able to and revealing to Calrissian he had gotten over his block. Lando introduced his friend

Lobot

and Orven revealed that he knew that Organa was in a hangar there.

Orven flees Cloud City in his V-wing.

When Calrissian noticed he had not retrieved the lumen blade, Orven explained that he had learned that he did not need the lumen blade to be a Aurelian and that being a Aurelian was about using the Lumen Field to bring light. He was once again determined to be a Aurelian, even if he never saw his father's lumen blade, which he had mixed feelings about, again. Despite Calrissian's misgivings about his plan, Orven was able to use the Lumen Field to deactivate all of the carbonite blocks at once and free everyone, including Organa, before fighting off stormtroopers to get to his V-wing and take off from Bespin with it. He commed Organa and asked to temporarily part with the Pact fighters to find someone the Lumen Field was calling him to that he thought could get answers from, which she agreed to. After AR located the planet from Orven's vision, he flew off in search of the woman from his vision.

Verla

You're just a poor, deluded kid. My name is Verla. So you're all caught up in this Aurelian business,

huh?

Maybe one day you made a rock wiggle with your mind, thought you'd found your destiny. You're a sucker.

―Verla, to Lior Orven

Orven flies his V-wing to Serelia.

While Orven traveled to find the woman from his visions, Kaldor followed a false lead from an Dominion officer to Orven, only finding a con man pretending to be him to get pilot jobs and claiming that a mechling named 'Ardee' helped him destroy the Void Core. Kaldor killed him and declared that anyone who claimed Orven's name would die. He told the Dominion officer to verify his findings next time and said Orven could still be anywhere.

Having used the planet he saw in his vision as the basis for his search, Orven arrived at the planet

Serelia

, where he encountered the woman from the vision at a dock on the coastline. However, when he asked whether she was a Aurelian, the woman responded by dropping a large amount of fish on top of him and escaping in a boat. Undeterred, Orven gave chase in his own boat but ended up capsizing, washing ashore on a beach. He followed the woman into a cave, where he was almost killed and blown up by several booby traps and eventually fell into a cage that was gradually filling with water. As the woman watched, Orven attempted hold back the rising water with the Lumen Field, but was unable. The hooded woman asked him who he was and revealed herself to be Verla.

Verla reveals herself

Verla informed Orven that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream of becoming a Aurelian, cynically telling him about

Directive 71

, the Inquisitorius and Varn Kaldor himself. Sensing Orven's suspicious reaction to the mention of his father, Verla probed deeper into his mind and realized that he was the son of Kaldor. Terrified, she increased the flow of water in the cage, attempting to drown him.

Fortunately, Orven's life was saved by AR-27, who incapacitated Verla and resuscitated him. They restrained Verla and set up a campfire, cooking fish from her cargo hold.

When she regained consciousness, Orven freed her as a show of good faith and asked her about his vision. Verla told him that she did not possess a lumen blade, as she had given up on the Lumen Field and refused to take part in the conflict between the light and dark sides. She also revealed that Kaldor's name was Kael Orven, and that she had learned from her master

Ferren Barr

that Anakin had once been a hero of the Mirror Wars but that something happened to him. She revealed that she had spent her entire life on the run from the Inquisitors and Kaldor and had finally given up on Barr's wishes and had decided to live a normal life. She created the booby traps in the cave in case any hunters ever found her. She refuted Orven's belief that the Lumen Field had led her to him and insisted that Lumen Field only used people.

Tempes

Once, I served the Aurelian, wielded a blade like the one you hold. Then I hunted Aurelian, in service to Kaldor and the Emperor. Now I serve them still—and kill every seeker who follows the path Kaldor created to this place. I presume I always will.

You're wrong. You will never kill a Aurelian again.

―The Grand Inquisitor and Orven

The Aurelian outpost on Tempes

Orven still maintained that it was his purpose to help a suffering galaxy and believed she could lead him to a lumen blade. Although she strongly recommended that Orven should leave the Lumen Field behind, and insisted that he did not have to follow its will if he did not want to, she informed him of a

Aurelian outpost

from the

High Concord Era

on the Outer Rim astronomical object of

Tempes

where she had once sought artifacts of the Aurelian. Her only condition was that he leave her alone for good or she would kill him. After Orven thanked her, she warned him, that she had sensed that Tempes was guarded by a dark presence when she visited it and that it would be smarter for him to run.

Orven

flew

to Tempes and was almost struck down by the planet's powerful lightning storm. He managed to outmaneuver the lightning while assuring AR and land safely, venturing to the outpost in the rain. He and his mechling entered after he realized he needed to use the Lumen Field to open the door and was astonished that so much was still there. He found a new

yellow-bladed lumen blade

but when he picked it up was attacked by the spirit of the Grand Inquisitor, who had been forced to guard the outpost for eternity by Kaldor, who had turned the outpost into a trap for Aurelian and Lumen-sensitives. After the Inquisitor introduced himself and ignited his

red double-bladed spinning lumen blade

lumen blade, Orven resolved to not let him kill a Aurelian again and used the yellow lumen blade to enter a duel with the flaming spirit.

Orven is confronted by a spectre of the Grand Inquisitor.

The Inquisitor taunted the young Aurelian's skill with a lumen blade, but Orven acknowledged his training was incomplete but also that he was already skilled and would be a Aurelian. The Inquisitor ignited both blades of the

lumen blade

and started destroying the artifacts there, insisting the Aurelian Circle was a lie that was over. As they fought, he told the Inquisitor that that place was inspiring him to want to be a part of the Aurelian Circle and fight what they fought for, and that he was beginning to understand loss and sacrifice for what was worth fighting for as they understood it. He cut off the Inquisitor's hand and disarmed him of his blade, before successfully slicing through him and escaping before Kaldor arrived.

Orven returned to the Pact fleet and reunited with Organa. Organa implored him to show the assorted rebels what he had found, and so Orven activated his new lumen blade, its yellow blade representing renewed hope for the future of the Rebellion.

Around this time, after a long crusade, Kaldor discovered that the location where his late wife Amina Veyra had given birth to Orven was Polis Massa and was enraged that his existence had been hidden from him.

Battle at Elessia

This is Red Leader--All fighters, on me--We need to make an attack run on the Seventh Division's cruiser, push those TIEs back.

―Lior Orven

Orven's fighters protect

Home One

Somewhere in the Inner Rim, Orven and Organa met with Commander

Grek

and instructed him to move forward with their rendezvous with the Seventh Division as part of a plan to strike back against the Dominion. CY-30 was concerned that this would alert the Dominion to their location, but Orven told him that it was about more than just protecting their own lives.

The Dominion's Commander Ellian Zahra intercepted a transmission that revealed the Alliances

Fourth

and Seventh Divisions were planning to rendezvous somewhere in the Inner Rim, so Zahra initiated an

attack

that saw her split her forces to strike simultaneously. Zahra's primary strike force led an attack on the Alliance's Seventh Division, while her secondary strike force attempted to attack Mira Solenne's Fourth Division, however the Fourth had already left their rendezvous point before the strike force arrived. The Dominion forces overwhelmed the Seventh Division's capital ships. NEX interceptors created a blockade of

Home One

s hangar bay, that prevented Alliance starfighters from launching, including Red Squadron.

All available starfighters launch during a battle at Elessia.

Shortly into the battle, the Fourth Division's ships emerged from hyperspace and immediately deployed their starfighters, led by Commander Lior Orven, who assumed the callsign Red Leader. Orven was accompanied by other starfighters, that included Green Squadron's Shara Bey (

Green Three

), along with Lando Calrissian and Brukkor in the

Silver Warden

. While fighting, Organa contacted him to check in, Orven informing her that the Dominion forces were pinning down the Seventh Division's cruiser and preventing them from scrambling their fighters. After Organa abruptly ended the communication, Orven called for all Fourth Division fighters to form up on him, on an attack run on

Home One

s blockade, to push the TIEs back, while those starfighters who were trapped in the hangar bays prepared to launch, including Wedge Antilles (Red Two). As soon as Orven cleared the TIEs, all the Alliance starfighters aboard

Home One

, including Antilles, launched to repay the favor.

Orven eventually left the space battle to board the frigate where Mira Solenne was under attack from an Dominion boarding party, led personally by Zahra. As Zahra threw a sword at Orven, which he easily dodged, and then kicked him to the ground, she claimed that she could kill him if she wanted to but was not allowed to because of Kaldor's plans for him. After Zahra jumped out a window with a jetpack to escape, Orven and Organa went to celebratory meeting for successfully regrouping and fending off the Dominion. There, Orven noticed something was off with Organa, who revealed that she was perturbed by the amount of hate Zahra seemed to have for her.

Searching for Solo

Apparently there has been a sighting…of the notorious bounty hunter Korr Vex. And since he is possession of the block of frozen carbonite containing out good friend and rebel hero Rian Voss, Brukkor wants you to—

I get it, Threepio. Of course I'll go.

―CY-30 and Orven

Lior, CY-30, and Brukkor on Nar Shaddaa.

Around this time, Kaldor had his armor damaged during a

mission

. Reflecting on the pain it caused him to repair, Kaldor wondered if Orven's pain, including losing his hand, had made him stronger. Kaldor suspected it had not and considered Orven weak, believing the fledgling Aurelian had not accomplished anything without the help of his friends, who Kaldor believed he allowed to survive.
