No longer wishing to turn Orven he resolved to kill him, and began his path to do so by searching for Rian Voss.

In killing Orven Kaldor believed he would kill Taren Vel's last hope and prove those who thought Orven would be his end wrong.

While tracking Solo, Kaldor's partner, a

Noctari assassin

named

Ochi

, figured out that Kaldor planned to lure Orven to him with Solo.

While practicing his lumen blade and Lumen Field skills by fighting training remotes, during which he recalled Eld Miro's training, Orven was interrupted by CY-30, insisting he did not mind the distraction. CY-30 said that AR-27 wanted to speak with him about something related to Tempes, where he found his new lumen blade. Before AR-27 could say anything, Brukkor arrived and revealed, via CY-30 translating, that Korr Vex had been spotted on Nar Shaddaa and urged Orven to come with him to rescue Solo from him. Brukkor shot down Orven's suggestion that they bring Calrissian, saying he was not to be trusted and that Solo would not want to see him, but Orven agreed. Orven, CY-30, AR-27, and Brukkor then took the

Falcon

to Nar Shaddaa, though AR complained to CY-30 on the way that he had not been able to speak to Orven. Orven was annoyed that Organa had to stay behind to coordinate

Operation Starlight

and urged Brukkor to ensure the mission would be quick, recalling how awful he found the planet and what had happened with Grakkus the Hutt.

On the planet, Orven watched as Brukkor met with his contact

Sagwa

, CY-30 explaining that he and Brukkor used to be old friends and translating that Sagwa believed a new victor in the

Garkata Fighting Arena

was Korr Vex. Looking at a hologram of the winner, Orven also thought they looked like Boba but noticed it was labeled "Jango." Orven spoke with a

Pa'lowick attendant

at the Arena to get information on "Jango" but was annoyed by both the

Pa'lowick

's refusal to answer questions without payment and CY-30 spouting the mottos of Nar Shaddaa. The attendant, suspecting they were friends of "Jango," used an alarm to call the

Kanji

gang, who were upset that "Jango" had defeated their fighter in the arena. Using his lumen blade to fight back against the thugs, who attacked despite Orven's warnings, Orven and the others fled, though Brukkor was reluctant to because they had not found much information. Following Sagwa through the streets before stealing and fleeing on a speeder, they encountered many more groups of Kanji that Orven fended off with the Lumen Field and his lumen blade until AR-27 picked them up in the

Falcon

. However, some of the Kanji planned to report seeing Orven to Dominion forces on

Vandor

Afterward, AR finally gave his message to Orven via CY-30, revealing that he had found a list of former Aurelian outposts across the galaxy in his databanks that he seemingly acquired on the Void Core. This greatly excited Orven, who eagerly wanted to learn more, feeling like the Lumen Field was providing for him and making AR list the locations for him immediately. Sagwa then entered with a message, CY-30 translating, in which he revealed that Organa had received a message from an unknown party claiming to be in possession of Solo.

Though Organa was starting to lose hope,

Ab Dalis

Approaching the target. Looks like a…

vent

of some kind, maybe. I see them, Arven. TIEs on our six. Trying to hit a target the size of a bantha's eye…feels like old times.

―Orven, to Starlight Squadron and AR-27

Orven and Starlight Squadron approach Ab Dalis.

Orven and the others met up with Organa, who told them that Fett did not have Solo anymore and that he was going to be sold in a

auction

in the attendance of most of the galaxy's crime syndicates on

Jekara

Though Organa was starting to lose hope, Orven assured her that they would find him.

After Organa revealed Solo was in the possession of

Crimson Dawn

, who Brukkor remembered was an extremely powerful criminal organization he had dealt with before, Orven expressed his concerns to Organa about her taking on this mission, claiming that she had not been herself recently and had not been acting from the heart. Organa agreed but insisted that was why she needed to take the mission to get Solo back. Later Orven joined Antilles and

Starlight Squadron

in his V-wing, having promised he would join them for a patrol, but assured Organa he would join her on Jekara soon.

Eager to get the fleet back together, and knowing recon missions were dicey, Orven told Antilles that he was happy to join the mission to find and recover a fleet division. Hoping to boost pilot

L'ulo L'ampar

's morale, he also revealed to the squadron that the Alliance was close to recovering Captain Solo. After traveling through hyperspace to

Ab Dalis

Freyta Smyth

and Antilles explained that it was chosen as a rebel rendezvous point because it was abandoned after a

cataclysmic impact

make it tectonically unstable hundreds of years earlier.

Mart Mattin

and Orven then received a broken transmission, which AR unscrambled, revealing it was from the

Eleventh Division

of the

Rendezvous Point Lambda-Four

at Ab Dalis who were being attacked by Dominion forces. The rebels on the surface, learning that heroes of Yavin Orven and Antilles were above them, became desperate for the squadron to come down and help them.

Orven and Starlight Squadron destroy a Abyss Cruiser above Ab Dalis.

Approaching the planet, Antilles advised them all to target ground targets and Orven relayed a message from AR that NEX fighters were approaching them. Hearing that Orven, who had made the trench run on the Void Core was among the fighters, one Pact who had conducted a geological survey of the planet sent coordinates to the squadron for a ground target beneath the Abyss Cruiser. They were unsure about what the target was, but after scanning and determining it was a tunnel that could have been hiding someone important, Antilles sent Orven to hit it, comparing it to making the Void Core run. Orven started to make the run while the others kept TIEs off of him, feeling like he was making the Void Core run again, but he started to have a flashback to the battle of Yavin that instead ended with Kaldor taking down the

Falcon

and saying he would claim Solo for himself, disturbing Orven and causing him to miss the target. However, the rest of Starlight Squadron were on the same vector and were able to hit it, triggering a volcanic explosion that destroyed the Abyss Cruiser that attacked the Eleventh Division. Afterward, Orven apologized for missing the shot, before the others realized they had rescued Mon Mothma. Orven then told Antilles that he was going to Jekara to help Organa.

Auction of Rian Voss

Haunted

Mira, I don't know if you're receiving this, but I had…a vision, I guess. I think the Lumen Field was trying to tell me something. I think…I think Kaldor's coming after Rian.

―Orven, in a message to Organa

Orven imagines fighting Varn Kaldor on Cloud City with his yellow lumen blade.

Orven contacted Oragna before he left for Jekara but only received a message from her saying things got complicated before losing signal. Unsure whether Organa could still hear him, he warned that Kaldor was potentially coming after Solo, having realized that his vision on Ab Dalis might have been a warning and a premonition.

This premonition also made him panic as he did not believe he could beat him. In hyperspace on the way to Jekara, Orven recalled fighting Kaldor on Cloud City and the Noctari Lord's utter brutality, strength, and ineffable power, likening it to a

gravel storm

. Imagining himself as he was at that moment fighting Kaldor on Cloud City with his yellow lumen blade, Orven began to believe he could be a storm too, but then just thought he was kidding himself and that Eld Miro and Kaldor had been right about him. He began to accept the fact that he would have to face Kaldor on Jekara to protect his friends but did not feel ready. Desperate for guidance, he tried to reach out to the Lumen Field to speak to Vel, but his fear made it difficult to access.

Just as he was about to exit hyperspace, Organa asked Orven for help in distracting Kaldor because the Noctari Lord arrived at the auction, but he was quickly occupied dealing with Kaldor's Super Abyss Cruiser the

Executor

that was over Jekara. Overwhelmed, Orven fought off various NEX fighters before leading them close to and getting their shots to hit the ship of one of the crime syndicates present at the auction, the

Son-tuul Pride

, which made the syndicate retaliate against the Dominion as Orven hoped it would. Finally given a window to approach the planet, he told Organa he was approaching as she told him to hurry. However, the Dominion forces established a secure channel between him and Kaldor, who had sensed that he was there in the Lumen Field. Kaldor threatened to kill the frozen Rian if he did not arrive to face him,

while imagining fighting Orven and thinking that he would not be a father to him or let him hide behind his friends.

After hearing the message, Orven, haunted by the image of his current self being killed by Kaldor on Cloud City, decided he was not ready to face him and turned his V-wing around.

The chase

He's

not

trying to

kill

us. We'll lead him out into

space

and— All right…M-maybe he

trying to kill us!

―Orven, to AR-27

As he left, Orven explained to Organa his belief that Kaldor wanted to turn him, and that if he fought Kaldor, he would kill them too.

He told her he would help from his ship and then taunted Kaldor, urging the Noctari Lord to pursue him. Organa revealed that his stunt had worked and Kaldor was coming for him, Orven telling her that he hoped to keep him busy and imploring her to save Solo.

As he assured Organa he would be fine, the

starship

of a

criminal group

harpooned

his fighter and started to pull him in. He was freed by laser blast's from Kaldor's NEX fighter.

As the two engaged in a dogfight, Organa contacted Orven again, who simply repeated his earlier sentiments and said he needed to focus.

He then tried to lure Kaldor into open space, believing the Noctari Lord did not want to kill him, but Kaldor shot his V-wing anyway, Orven realizing that Kaldor did want to kill him as he crash-landed on Jekara.

After urging AR-27 to repair the ship quickly, he used a blaster to shoot at Kaldor's NEX fighter as it approached. However, Dominion

Administrator

Sly Moore

, who had been tasked with killing Kaldor dropped a mechling gang she hired called the

Mechling Crush Pirates of Bestoon

onto Kaldor's TIE and near Orven. Orven initally ignited his lumen blade and acted defensively, but the mechlings instead fixed his fighter, leaving Orven confused. Orven, accepting of the help, flew away with Mechling Crush members riding his wing and shooting at Kaldor.

Orven is chased by Varn Kaldor during the auction for Rian Voss.

Opting to fly away to the planet's

Polar Sea

to get Kaldor to follow him, Orven briefly thought he had lost Kaldor until Kaldor's NEX fighter emerged from the water beneath him. Kaldor taunted him, saying his increased strength in the Lumen Field would not save him and that he had Solo, so he would not be saving Orven either. Noticing how quickly the sea's water was freezing, Orven trapped Kaldor in the ice by making a giant wave of water with his V-wing and leading Kaldor into it when it quickly froze. Despite having the perfect opportunity to shoot at and kill Kaldor, Orven could not do it after what he had revealed at Cloud City, but knew he would eventually need to face him. Orven reached out to Organa on the

Falcon

who explained that they and the Dominion shuttle had been hit by a blast that had immobilized them, meaning they could not reach Solo but Solo could not be taken to the flagship. Orven said he was on his way and assured them Kaldor would not be a threat for a while. With Hutt fighters now chasing the shuttle with Solo on it, Organa tried to get Orven to help, but Kaldor emerged from the ice in his NEX fighter and dragged Orven back into a fight, so Orven told her he could not help.

Kaldor soon chased Orven back into space. With Kaldor reveling in making Orven feel fear due to his friends lives being put in danger, Orven cried out as the shuttle containing Solo was fired at by Hutt and Dominion fighters.

Thankfully, Kaldor was soon ordered by the Emperor to assist with a Hutt attack that was happening against the Dominion, which forced the Noctari Lord to leave his son behind, not before reminding Orven that he was no Aurelian. Orven insisted that when they met again he would be a Aurelian.

Kaldor resented having to leave but was proud of terrifying Orven, recognizing that the Emperor wanted Orven to live and was orchestrating things so that it could be the case.

During his confrontation with Kaldor, Organa and the rest failed to rescue Rian Voss, who was captured by Korr Vex once again after the auction.

In search of Aurelian secrets

I know I'm needed here, Mira. But I can't help but think I should be following that path, trying to grow as a Aurelian. I'll have to face Kaldor eventually. […] I need to find a way to finish my training…and soon.

―Lior Orven to Mira Solenne

After the breakout of the

Syndicate War

, the Free Systems Pact had a meeting with many of its leaders that Lior Orven was present for. As Admiral Ackbar discussed the Alliance's next moves in its rebuilding effort, Mira Solenne noticed that Orven was distant. He explained that AR-27 had given him a list of possible Aurelian sites across the galaxy from an Dominion archive. He expressed that he knew he was needed by the Alliance, but that he felt like he should be trying to continue growing as a Aurelian. He recognized that he would need to face Kaldor again and wanted to be ready when he did. Organa told him that it was okay and that he should trust what the Lumen Field was telling him, offering to smooth things over with Mon Mothma and telling him that they could fight the battles without him for now.

Lior Orven consults a list of Aurelian sites.

He visited the planet

Ilum

first and discovered the mass imperial blockade and presence there and the Dominion's exploitation of the planet. He felt in the Lumen Field that the planet was important, but also felt that now only held pain as the Dominion was killing it more and more every day. He believed there was nothing for him there and he and AR-27 used the weather of the planet to leave and slip past the blockade. He visited

Arashar

next, but only orbited it as it had a massive orbital Dominion presence and he did not think he could get by it. He then visited

Lothal

, and traveled to the former location of the

Lothal Aurelian Temple

. He could feel that it needed to be opened with the Lumen Field, but it did not respond to him and felt like it had been buried or locked. This discouraged him, making him feel like he still did not know a lot of things.

A legacy on Al'doleem

A Aurelian saved my life once. Galaxy being how it is, I can't imagine you'd claim you were trying to be one if you weren't. I've wanted to repay that debt my whole life. You're probably as close as I'll ever get.

―Colli to Lior Orven

He finally traveled to the river moon of

Al'doleem

and decided to visit it because it did not have an Dominion blockade and was not being destroyed. He flew

Mount Pasvaal

as was encouraged by the sight of something important there without much guarding. He landed in the ruins of

Am'balaar City

, after seeing that it had been rebuilt some after a

Dam collapse

. He told AR-27 that they would head into town, but he did not want to draw attention as one of the Dominion's biggest targets. He walked up to two stormtroopers and tried to perform a Aurelian mind trick on them that Eld Miro had taught him and he had seen Taren Vel do, asking the troopers to tell him what the Dominion was doing at the top of the mountain, but the mind trick failed and the troopers merely told him to move along.

Orven and AR-27 aimlessly wandered the city until AR noticed a hull panel from a

Delta-7 Aethersprite-class light interceptor

and stopped him. Orven did not understand until a man named

Colli

walked out and told Orven that he was surprised he did not know but bet he had other people fix his starships. Colli explained that the Aurelian flew Delta-7 Aethersprites in the Mirror Wars. Colli told Orven to come inside, as he had noticed the lumen blade on his

belt

, and warned that if he had others might have too. Colli asked him if he was really a Aurelian, to which Orven explained that he was trying to be. Colli told him that he had known a Aurelian named

Kirak Infil'a

who had worked in the

monastery

on top of Mount Pasvaal and had allowed him and his sister to escape in his Aetherspire when the city was attacked and destroyed. He explained that both Infil'a and his parents had died. Colli asked Orven about his story, but AR-27 tried to stop him from saying anything. Colli insisted that he was not going to turn them into the Dominion forces and that helping Orven would probably be the closest he could get to repaying his debt to the Aurelian that saved him. Orven said that he needed to learn as much about the Aurelian as he could from what of their legacy remained and that he believed the mountain that Infil'a had come from was connected to the Order and that he wanted to figure out what the Dominion was looking for there.

Lior Orven finds a recording of Eld Miro in a holocron.

Colli took Orven to the Monastery at the top of the mountain, but explained that there were also tunnels and chambers in the mountain that the Aurelian had used and that the Dominion was bringing artifacts out of it all the time. Orven considered using his lumen blade and the Lumen Field to let go and cut right through the Dominion forces in his way but could not bring himself to do it and instead gave his lumen blade to AR for safekeeping. He went and meditated with the Lumen Field on the side of the mountain before returning to the warehouse where Colli had told him the Dominion stored the artifacts they found in the mountain. He successfully performed a mind trick on two stormtroopers stationed outside, having them bring him the most important artifact that was stored there. They brought him a holocron, which Lior recognized from his experience with Grakkus the Hutt. He reached out with the Lumen Field to unlock it as he had learned how to do and found a recording of Master Eld Miro teaching lessons.

Orven was disappointed, however, that the lesson Eld Miro taught in the holocron was one that he had already taught him back on Dagobah. Burdened by the series of setbacks he had faced recently, Orven considered dropping the holocron off of a cliff, but was stopped by AR. Orven opened the holocron again, and this time Eld Miro discussed

Vergences

in the Lumen Field, listing the Aurelian Temple on Veyron Prime, Jedha, and the

Living Sea

Gazian

as examples. Lior had never heard of the Living Sea of Gazian and decided to go to the planet to check it out.

The Living Sea of Gazian

I'm going to tell you what the other Aurelian you've met haven't. Even Eld Miro. You don't have to be a Aurelian, friend. […] There are many other paths. The important thing is to make choices that feel right, that are true to you and the person you want to be. But if you want it, we're here for you.

―Elzar Mann to Lior Orven

Elzar Mann appeared to Lior Orven in the Living Sea of Gazian.

Arriving in the

Gazian system

, Lior was excited by the possibility of finding a Aurelian-related site that the Dominion did not know about. He landed on a platform he found on the planet, but was disappointed to only find a sea of mushrooms. However he could feel the strength of the Lumen Field there and decided to look around, but when he walked onto the surface, he fell into a mushroom-filled cavity below. His breath mask was pulled off of him and he went unconscious, starting to have a vision where he was wearing the clothes he wore during the Duel on Cloud City and was in possession of the Orven lumen blade. He then saw a man in High Concord Era

Aurelian robes

sitting next to him, who then took the Orven lumen blade with Lumen Field and admired it, comparing it to his own. Orven held up a blaster and told the man to give it back. He acquiesced but commented that he was not scared of the blaster. The man introduced himself as Aurelian Master

Elzar Mann

. He asked Orven if he was a Aurelian, to which Orven said that he was trying to be but was still learning about the Lumen Field and trying to understand it. Mann told him that that never really stops, as the Aurelian Circle goes back a long way and there was a lot to learn.

Just then, their surroundings shifted to

Ahch-To

, the birthplace of the Aurelian. Orven asked Mann what exactly was going on. Mann explained that Gazian was a gigantic living organism that takes an imprint of the minds that visit it and reproduces them exactly as they were at the moment they were there. Mann added that he had come to the planet to get answers after having a vision, and that was why his imprint was there. Orven explained that the Concord had fallen and an Dominion had taken over. He said that he had never met a Aurelian like Mann, and had only ever met Taren Vel and Eld Miro, the latter of whom Mann remembered and commented was very old and very wise. Orven had also said that he had met a Aurelian named Kael Orven, but that he had fallen to the Umbral Current, something that Mann knew happened occasionally.

Elzar Mann and Lior Orven speak in an imprint of Ahch-To.

Mann then showed Orven an imprint of what the High Concord looked like, telling him that it was a Golden Age where they were all connected eight centuries after the defeat of

Darth Bane

Orven came to, ignited his lumen blade, and put his breath mask back on. He then saw many bodies of Aurelian that had stayed in the Living Sea. He found one Aurelian skeleton holding a

sacred Aurelian text

, which he brought to the surface and recognized was an invitation.

Aurelian of the Rebellion

Unity Day attack

This is amazing."

"We've earned it. It's been a long time since we had a solid win. Not a single casualty. You planned one heck of mission, Mira.

―Organa and Orven during the celebration after the Unity Day attack

The Unity Day parade

While Orven was away, Zahra referenced her meeting with him to Bey, as she believed Bey to the most arrogant rebel she had ever met next to Orven.

After he returned to the Alliance, Orven took part in an

attack

on the

Dominion Unity Day Parade

Bar'leth

, one of the Pact fighters' first large scale operations since the Battle of Kryon. Organa and the rest of the Alliance hoped it would announce to people that they still existed and could still strike back against the Dominion, hoping to do so without harming civilians as well. Orven flew in his X-Wing as part of Red Squadron during the attack. After destroying the Dominion's vast artillery on display in the parade, Orven told the others over comm that AR-27 had detected a wave of NEX fighters launching from a nearby Dominion airbase outside of the city and suggested they leave. Wedge Antilles, flying as Red Leader, acknowledged Orven and told everyone to follow him, leading all of the Alliance fighters to a successful escape.

Afterward, Orven participated in a toast to the Free Systems Pact lead by Antilles before having a drink and sitting down. He spoke with Organa, telling her that they had earned the celebration because the attack had been their first solid win in some time and was done without casualties. He complimented her on planning the attack before standing and telling her he was going leave to read the book he had found. He explained that it was full of incredible things and that he could not stop thinking about it, adding that even holding it made him feel more connected to the Lumen Field. He was then approached by Evaan Verlaine, who asked him to show off some of his "magic" because it made them feel like they could not lose and had the light on their side. He was reluctant to do so because it made him feel like a show-off, but he agreed, igniting his yellow lumen blade. Verlaine called it magic while many looked on in awe.

The second Void Core revealed

Lior…what is it? What do you need to tell us?

The Dominion's building another Void Core.

If we could do it once…we can do it again. Let's get to work.

―Mon Mothma and Lior Orven

Lior Orven asked Hera Syndulla about their fifth intelligence hit, feeling like it could work out well.

Meanwhile, Crimson Dawn agents

Bevelyn

and

Jon Melton

received a message from Lady

Qi'ra

instructing them to cause chaos for the Dominion. Because they were deep undercover at the Dominion's secret

Void Core II

, they decided that they should expose the facility to the Free Systems Pact.

They managed to escape with their children and get a message to the Pact fighters telling them that they had top secret Dominion weapons information they would give in exchange for protection, but they were not taken seriously until it was presented by General

Hera Syndulla

at an intelligence briefing that Orven was present at among many other intelligence hits. Orven asked about it and Syndulla explained to him what it was, though she wondered if something about it stood out to him. While Orven was unsure, he said had good feeling about it.

Later, in a separate room, Syndulla asked Orven if he truly believed there was something significant about the defectors and wondered if he was sensing something due to his connection with the Lumen Field. Lior said that he has learned everything is connected to the Lumen Field, Syndulla responding that he sounded like

Kanan Jarrus

, her late Aurelian lover. Orven asked if that was a good thing, and she said it usually was. Commodore

Grek

decided that anything that Orven thought was worth looking into was worth looking into and had someone look up information about the case to walk Orven through it. While they only had vague information, the aide informed Orven that a relay station, one of many they had in out-of-the-way spots where the fleet could send communications safely, received a signal from two individuals who claimed to be imperial defectors and asked for protection and a safe place to go in return for information about a classified weapons site, though the Alliance lost connection after they told them to wait for authorization and had disregarded it because many information hits turn out to be imperial traps and are therefore not worth the risk.

They had also taken the shuttle's signature and learned that the defectors had escaped in an Dominion shuttle called the

Scopium

and discovered right then while checking the databases that the Dominion had put a system-wide alert on the shuttle, suggesting the defectors' legitimacy. Grek asked Orven if he could give them more specifics about what he was sensing, but Orven could not, explaining that it was not like tuning into a comms signal but that he could tell that something about them was important and they needed to pay attention and find the shuttle. They discovered that the last place the shuttle's signal had popped up was on Veyron Prime, and so Orven suggested he go there. Grek and Syndulla told him it was a bad idea, the former reminding him that the Emperor lives there and the latter suggesting that they send pathfinders to mount a rescue mission but not him because he was too valuable, there were bounties for him across the galaxy, and because he was the only Aurelian they had.

Orven disguises himself as an Dominion officer on Veyron Prime

Orven understood but reminded them that their spies had confirmed that the Emperor and Kaldor were off-world, even confirming it with the aide who said they were on Kaldor's flagship in the Mid Rim and that he could do things that others could not, so what was risky for others was not risky for him, demonstrating his point by performing a mind trick on Grek and getting him to repeat what he said. Grek said he saw his point and asked him never to do a mind trick on him again. Syndulla emphasized that Veyron Prime was the most dangerous place for a Pact to be in the galaxy and asked if he had a plan. Orven said he had the start of a plan which was to hide the fact that he was a rebel by dressing as an Dominion.

Orven eventually arrived in

the Works

District of Veyron Prime, two Pact agents seeing him off. They told him that he was lucky they had an Dominion Officer uniform that fit him very well, one of the Pact fighters telling him he looked good in black, which surprised him and prompted to look at the outfit. They both told him that they thought what he was doing was crazy and not a good idea, but Orven assured them that they did not need to worry because he would be fine. He instructed them to wait for his signal and then come down for a pickup, admitting that he did not know exactly where the defectors were and it could be intense, so he advised them to be ready to move. He then gave them his lumen blade to hold on to, which surprised them, clarifying that it was better to leave it with them because he would be tempted to use it if he had it and and if people saw it they would report the Aurelian bounty and he would be swarmed with Dominion forces. When they asked him, Orven revealed that he was going to track down the defectors by speaking to stormtroopers.

Eventually Orven tracked the Meltons to a sewer, but they immediately started shooting at him until he told them he was there to help and was with was with the Alliance, forcing him to use the Lumen Field to jump around the sewer to dodge the blasts. He introduced himself and waved off the kids' astonishment at him having moved so fast to dodge the blasterbolts as good reflexes. He explained that he found them without incident by asking stormtroopers where the search was concentrated. He checked in with the kids and asked if they were okay and they said that their parents were keeping them safe, which did not surprise Orven.

Veyron Prime Police

then arrived, and Orven told them to stand down and that he would take it from there, but they questioned this as they had not been told any officers would be down there and asked him for authorization. Believing Orven could not take on the troopers on his own, Bevelyn and Jon made a plan for her to take the kids and run while he held them off, but Orven overheard them and assured them that it would not be necessary. As the police pointed their blasters at him and he raised his hand to reach out with the Lumen Field, he admitted that there were too many of them for him to fight or mind trick, but told them not to worry because he could sense help all around them. He then used the Lumen Field to call down many

Corridor ghouls

, beastly creatures that lived beneath Veyron Prime, down through the ceiling of the sewer and had them attack the Police, Orven saying "told you" to the Meltons as they took the opportunity to run away and escape.

Orven reveals that the Dominion is building another Void Core to the Free Systems Pact leadership

At some point after this, Orven sensed the activation and attempted opening of the

Fermata Cage

, an ancient Noctari device, by members of Crimson Dawn.

Search for the

Kezarat

convoy

Spira

The

Kezarat

was the command ship in a hundred-tanker Byne Guild convoy bringing fuel to the Outer Rim Territories some two hundred years back. It just…vanished. […]

Probably got hit by raiders, right? That much fuel had to be a tempting target."

"Some people say that, Lior. But I think it was something different. The

Kezarat

convoy disappeared the same day a kind of storm hit hyperspace.

―Amilyn Holdo and Lior Orven

Later, Orven took a break with Organa, Calrissian, Lobot, Brukkor, and

Amilyn Holdo

at the latter's

friend's

house

on the planet

Spira

. Sitting by the pool, Orven read the Aurelian text he had recovered from Gazian. Calrissian, who was playing

Sabacc

with Lobot and Brukkor tried to convince Orven to stop reading and join their game, but Orven refused, explaining that he did not often get the opportunity to study and wanted to use the time to focus since he was learning so much about the Lumen Field and the Aurelian from the text. Eventually, Calrissian pushed Brukkor into the pool, sending a wave toward Orven that he held back and redirected with the Lumen Field while still reading with a smile. Meanwhile, Holdo tried to convince Organa to relax especially since they were by handsome men. Realizing she was referring to Orven and Calrissian, Organa was unimpressed, but Holdo said that she would look at them if Organa would not.

Orven, Holdo, and Organa attend an auction on Spira

Later, Orven, Organa, and Holdo attended an

auction on Spira

for an ancient

Nihil

strikeship

, the real reason Holdo had wanted them to come to the planet. They were all dressed in elegant masks to hide their identities. As Holdo explained to Organa that they needed the strikeship to find a set of tankerships full of fuel to solve the Pact fighters' fuel problems, Orven felt something that made him groan and put his head in his hands. Holdo was losing the auction to the

curator

of the

Dominion Museum

, so she asked Orven if he could use the Lumen Field to get the man to stop bidding. Orven said that he could not perform a mind trick because he was feeling off, believing something was happening with the umbral current of the Lumen Field that was distracting him,

which unbeknownst to Orven was a disturbance in the Lumen Field caused by the opening of the

Fermata Cage

Orven agreed to try the trick anyway, but Calrissian commed them and told him not to. Organa, holding Orven's arm to stop him from doing anything, listened as Calrissian explained that the mind trick would draw too much attention to them, and that the man might know about the Lumen Field and figure out what happened since he ran the Dominion Museum. Calrissian suggested they instead let the man win.

Later, in a hangar where the man who won the strikeship was keeping it, Orven ignited his lumen blade and drew a bunch of stormtroopers that were guarding the ship to him, deflecting their blaster shots back at them. While they were distracted by Orven, Calrissian and Holdo fired on them from above and Brukkor stopped some of the troopers from locking off the ship. Once all the stormtroopers were taken down, Calrissian said that he knew his plan would work because all the stormtroopers would be so caught up in taking down the Aurelian to get a reward that they would not notice anything else. Orven reminded Calrissian that it might not have ended well for him, but Calrissian claimed he knew he would be able to handle it because of the book he had been studying. Holdo asked Orven to cut open the side of the strikeship with his lumen blade and make a circular hole not too deep. After Orven did, they retrieved the

Path engine

from inside the ship.

Orven and his companions arrive in No-Space

Later, back at Holdo's friend's house, Holdo explained that she was planning to go after the

Kezarat

convoy

, which Organa had suspected. She explained that it was a massive

Byne Guild

fuel convoy that disappeared when entering

hyperspace

many years earlier. Orven asked if it was because it got hit by raiders because it was such a sought-after target, but Holdo revealed it actually disappeared during a

storm

Skyfarer

, a group of NEX fighters found them and started going after them. Holdo attempted to pilot the ship out of the atmosphere so they could jump to hyperspace and escape, but the Path engine activated on its own and sent the ship through hyperspace into

No-Space

, near the

Great Hall of the Nihil

The lost colony

Sometimes people find their way to No-Space, just like you all did. […] One of those was a Aurelian. Alareen Xie. She joined us, helped us fight off the Nihil. We wouldn't have survived without her. She died in time, as most people do. But after she did, a sort of belief rose up. That someday a Aurelian would return and take us out of this place. Would take us home.

―Blythe, to Orven

Orven and his friends did not recognize No-Space and had no idea where they were, but Orven expressed that while he had not traveled as much as they had, he knew the galaxy had strange things in it. He added that the Lumen Field seemed off and distant, as if there was not much around that was alive. After Holdo tried and failed to take the ship to hyperspace again, Orven suggested that they needed to disconnect the Path engine to see if it would fix the problem, but Holdo revealed they would need to land somewhere first. After landing on the Great Hall, Lando lamented the dangers of falling off the edge there and said it was worse than Cloud City, but Orven insisted that Cloud City had been really bad. A child in a spacesuit then appeared and asked them if they had 'flavors', which Calrissian interpreted to mean the kid wanted to eat them. Orven said he was just a kid, but Calrissian asked him to remind him to tell Orven about the

White Worms

gang's savage children. The kid eventually advised them to hide and ran off, and Orven sensed with the Lumen Field that the kid was terrified and did not know where he was going. The kid then jumped off the edge of the Hall, which shocked Orven, but he just activated a jetpack and flew away.

A killdroid in the Great Hall

Lando suggested they get out of there, which Orven agreed with, suggesting they go back to their ship and try to find the other place there that the boy mentioned. He then noticed that a bunch of Nihil

killdroids

had surrounded their ship, which was what the boy was afraid of. Orven began to fight them with his lumen blade, but quickly realized that they were lumen blade resistant. A ship then pulled up next to the hall, and Holdo suggested that they board it to escape the mechlings, but was concerned by having to jump across open space to it. Orven insisted that it was not that bad of a jump and because the ramp had a magnetic shield they would not be in the vacuum very long, but Calrissian implied that easy for him to say because he was a Aurelian. They all jumped over to the ship without issue, except for Calrissian, who tripped and fell, prompting Orven to use the Lumen Field to pull him back up while muttering "I can do this." Once Calrissian was safe he thanked Orven for saving his life and assured him he would not bother him about studying his book ever again. Orven revealed that he could have gotten him to safety faster but could not because there was still something going on with the Lumen Field that he described as ripples messing with his concentration. Once the ship they were on docked with a larger ship, a voice over a speaker ordered them to throw their weapons out into space or they would vent them out into space. Orven considered using his lightaber to just cut through the door into the bigger ship but realized they would just vent them anyway. He threw his lumen blade out into space, which he said hurt because they were hard to find, but the kid with the jetpack just flew over, picked it up, and ignited it before flying away, Orven warning him that he needed to be careful with it or he would hurt himself.

The voice over the speaker welcomed them to the

Colony of Kezarat

. Holdo yelled that they just needed to go home, but an

Abyssin

left the cockpit and told them they were home.

After Organa said they would not attack and were listening, the individual introduced himself as Captain

Blythe

, and Holdo introduced herself, Orven, and the others to him. Blythe then began a tour of the colony, confirming that it was the old convoy and the fuel was still there. The kid, who Blythe revealed was named

Forvan

The mural of Alareen Xie

Forvan took them to mural depicting a Aurelian. Orven was shocked that they had a Aurelian with the convoy, but Blythe said she had not been there from the beginning and just found her way to No-Space. Blythe explained that her name was

Alareen Xie

Escape from No-Space

Keep the killdroids off Bruk until he's back on the ship. Then get yourselves safe too. I'm going after the Aurelian text.

But you said your blade can't get through [the killdroids'] armor! And Lior…it's just a book!

No. It's

the

book. And as far as my lumen blade, I wasn't properly focused before. Now I am. Now…the Lumen Field is with me.

―Orven and Holdo

After Brukkor realized that they could use the Path engine to escape No-Space, Blythe gathered Orven and the others, offering them an old

Longbeam cruiser

to retrieve it. Orven was skeptical that it could fly, but Blythe assured them that it used to and just needed to be fixed up. Once they got the cruiser to take off and leave the colony, Holdo and Lando took inventory of what they had, including that they had a Aurelian. Orven reminded them that his connection to the Lumen Field was different and hard to use in No-Space and something strange felt like it was happening to it anyway, but he assured them he would try his best. Calrissian assured them they would be fine, but Orven was still skeptical and pointed out that the killdroids had found and taken the Path engine. Orven decided that he would distract the mechlings so the others could get the engine, but after needed to go back to the

Skyfarer

The Kezarat Colony

After shooting a mechling behind Brukkor with a blaster, Orven started to run off and told Holdo to keep Brukkor safe while he ran after the Aurelian text. She reminded him that his lumen blade could not get through their armor and that it was just a book, but Orven said that it was more than just a book and that he had not been focused when his lumen blade would not cut them the first time, but now was. Orven sliced through the mechlings with his lumen blade while saying "the Lumen Field is with me" and calling out for what they did with his book. He eventually found the book on a ledge at the edge of the Hall and decided that he would easily be able to grab it and fly back to the ship, but a killdroid shot his jetpack, sending him flying uncontrollably toward the book and leaving it without fuel. As killdroids approached him, their footsteps shaking the ground caused the book to go over the edge. Orven screamed in horror and reached out to it with his right hand, which was holding his lumen blade, but a mechling grabbed his hand and lifted him up by it, crushing both his hand and his lumen blade.

The mechling threw him onto the ground and into the remains of the

Skyfarer

, and Orven cried out in horror when he saw his hand and lumen blade. Calrissian and Holdo saw what happened and decided to try and get to Orven before the mechlings killed him. A delirious Orven told the approaching mechlings to stop, but when they did not, he threw all of the mechlings out of the Hall in a massive wave using the Lumen Field while telling himself "may the Lumen Field be with me." Calrissian, Holdo, and Brukkor were shocked at how he was doing it, and Calrissian told Brukkor to remind him never to get on his bad side. Orven then pulled the Aurelian text to him with the Lumen Field and passed out immediately after.

Later, on the way back to the colony, Calrissian checked in on a now handless Orven, though the mechling had only destroyed his already mechanical hand. Calrissian asked him how he would done what he did, but he said that he did not understand how it happened and that it felt like the Lumen Field suddenly came back to him stronger than ever, and that it was frightening. Calrissian told him to cheer up, but thinking about his broken lumen blade and his inevitable confrontation with Kaldor, told Calrissian that he was not sure he would be able to do something he needed to anymore. Back at the colony, Orven showed Organa, who had stayed behind, what happened to his hand. Blythe and Forvan offered to fix it up, the latter suggesting a claw or grappling hook, but Orven said he would just wait until they got home even though those things would be cool. Orven and Brukkor then presented the Path engine to Blythe. After Blythe presented the group to the members of the colony, but Organa and Calrissian warned them about the dangers of the Dominion and traveling with them, Blythe revealed to them that several of the colony decided to go back with them. However, he revealed that many were going to stay on the Great Hall since they got rid of all the killdroids. Brukkor pointed out that Orven got rid of all the mechlings by himself, and Blythe commented that it seemed the things people said about Aurelian were true. However, Orven, who was struggling to pull a piece of scrap to him with the Lumen Field, said they sometimes were true and sometimes were not. Later, after a rough hyperspace jump made with many ships secured together and assisted by Lobot, Orven and his friends as well as several members of the colony arrived back in the greater galaxy.

Back with the fleet, Orven had his mechanical hand repaired by a medical mechling while AR-27 did his best to repair his lumen blade. Organa reminded Orven that it could have been worse if the mechling had crushed his other hand, and asked him about the powerful act of the Lumen Field he had pulled off on the Great Hall. He confirmed that he had not learned it from his book but that it was instead related to something being wrong with the Lumen Field, describing it as if something hit the Lumen Field while he was in No-Space and caused it to ripple. He added that it felt like waves on the beach in the way that it vacillated from him being the strongest he had ever been where he almost could not control it, to not being able to feel it at all. Organa then wondered if since he could feel it, and even she could slightly, that the Emperor and Kaldor were also feeling it immensely and were distracted, making it their chance to defeat them. Orven said her theory made sense, and she went to speak with Mon Mothma. Orven thanked AR for attempting to fix his lumen blade, but admitted that he was going to need a new one for when he inevitably faced Kaldor again.

Duel with a mechling

A Aurelian…I was afraid my dreams of killing one were dashed. […] I'd been informed the Aurelian were extinct.

Oh, don't worry…they still are.

―Ajax Sigma and Orven

Ajax Sigma

While still escorting more of the

Kezarat

convoy to the Alliance fleet, Orven felt a disturbance in the Lumen Field while on the

Silver Warden

. He explained to Organa that it was not like the one he had felt in No-Space but instead felt like the deaths of innocent people, and that it was nearby enough that he could bring them there. Organa agreed to go despite the fact they were supposed to be escorting the convoy. When they arrived, CY-30 revealed that the moon did not appear on any

star-charts

. Organa wondered if it was erased by the Dominion, but Orven said it was not and pointed out an ancient lumen blade like none he would ever seen before. He and Brukkor then noticed that there was a large amount of destroyed mechlings that were cut down with a lumen blade. They all realized that a battle had been fought there, and Orven put together that it had been between mechlings and people, and since there were no battle mechlings and no remains, it must have been hundreds of years earlier. Orven realized that the battle was not what he had sensed and instead lead them to a

homesteader ship

that they found to be full of bodies. CY-30 offered to secure the

Falcon

but Orven said it was not necessary. Orven asked AR to plug into the ship and see if it had any information about the moon they were on. AR revealed it was called

Kligson's Moon

. Orven suggested that the carnage there may have recently happened and the perpetrator could still be there, and suggested splitting up into pairs to search due to the size of the ship.

After discovering a mechling, that unbeknownst to Orven was

Ajax Sigma

, an ancient mechling revolutionary, he asked it why they killed all the people there. Sigma claimed that it was self defense and that they attacked him outside when he came there to retrieve something, but Orven did not believe him. The mechling clarified that he had followed the people back to their ship and killed them all, which Orven said did not sound like self-defense. The mechling activated electric blades on his arms and disparaged Orven for being organic and for suggesting that the mechling would go with him and answer for his actions. Orven ignited his lumen blade and began to fight Sigma, who used the blades on his arms to defend himself. He was surprised to see a Aurelian, believing them all to be dead, and Orven assured him they were. After Orven sliced open the mechling's chest and knocked him down, he ordered him to stand down or face destruction. However, the mechling just entered a pod that he ejected from the homesteader ship, successfully escaping.

Kaldor had a vision of Orven and Sigma's duel while speaking with the

Eye of Webbish Bog

, sometime before the events of No-Space and the activation of the Fermata Cage. In the vision, Kaldor also saw Orven holding two kyber crystals, one green and one red.

Adventure with Aphra

Kidnapping plot

Come on, Lior. Ancient Aurelian artifact of great power? Sealed away by Aurelian Masters? Maybe even something that could give you an edge of a certain Noctari Lord? Tell me you don't want that.

How far to Sason?

―Chelli Aphra and Orven

Because he had lost control of his powers just as Orven had, Kaldor recruited Doctor Aphra to help him find a way to control them. While in a shuttle to a location she had in mind, Aphra walked into a room where Kaldor was meditating and entered a vision he was having that included Orven in his Darsuun farm boy clothes. Aphra did not understand why Orven was there and did not recognize the other individual in the vision,

Sabé

, who was telling Orven that there was still good in Kaldor, which were Orven's mother's last words.

Doctor Aphra kidnaps Orven on Numidian Prime.

Though his lumen blade was still not fixed, Orven traveled to

Numidian Prime

with AR-27 in search of a

temple

on the planet. He briefly left AR in a temporary camp while he searched for it, but after finding no sign of it and it started to rain he returned to the camp, finding peace in the solitude. He apologized to AR for leaving, saying they needed to wait for the rain to taper off, but then noticed the mechling was deactivated and laying on the ground. He cried out and ran to the mechling, but a node underneath the mechling started shooting smoke out, confusing Orven and knocking him out after he inhaled it. The trap had been set by Doctor Chelli Aphra, who then stood over an unconscious Orven and greeted him, calling him a "farm boy", and asked how he felt about going on an adventure.

Aphra eventually bound and gagged Orven and placed him in a crate on her ship the

Ark Angel IV

. When he woke up, she opened the crate and said she was glad he was still breathing before offering him

Caf

. She started to get him some as he realized who kidnapped him, telling her he did not want Caf and just wanted to know why she did it and what she had done with AR. She explained that she had put a restraining bolt on and turned off AR, and Orven realized that she needed him for something. Aphra then claimed that she was going to actually do him a huge favor. Orven had managed to slip out of the ropes she had tied him up in, which she noticed at that moment, surprised at how quickly he had done it and wondering if Solo had taught him as she presented a holorecording. Realizing it was a Aurelian holo, Orven listened as Aurelian Master

Shaak Ti

explained to

Aayla Secura

that a powerful Aurelian artifact called

Kythoo's Bell

was locked away at the

Sason Temple

. Aphra told him that she knew how to get there an needed a Aurelian to get in, but Orven was still suspicious. After insisting she was just being nice and trying to make up for the events at the Screaming Citadel, Aphra pointed out that the artifact could give Orven an edge over Kaldor. After contemplating it more, Orven asked her how far

Sason

was, exciting her.

Sason Temple

Right before I found you, Kaldor found me. That 'Lumen Field pulse' you felt? He felt it too. But unlike you, it just made him stronger. Wild. Out of control. You don't stand a chance against him as you are. Say we leave Sason without Kythoo's Bell. Kaldor kills everyone you care about to get to you. Then he kills you. Once you're dead, who's going to protect me from him?

―Chelli Aphra to Orven

The Sason Temple

As they flew into Sason in the

Ark Angel IV

magnetic storm

. Telling Aphra she had a great copilot, Orven navigated the ship through the storm despite Aphra's protests, following an echo of a note he could hear in the Lumen Field and urging Aphra to trust him. They then flew toward a gap in the storm that held the temple, Orven exclaiming that it was beautiful. After being briefly confused by Aphra calling the temple their payday, which she insisted was figurative, Aphra explained that her research claimed that the temple would only open to someone who was pure of heart and strong in the Lumen Field. Orven tried to open it but failed, explaining to Aphra that he was unable to use the Lumen Field because something was happening to it making it unstable. Aphra then accused him of making things up to navigate them there, but he insisted he had used the Lumen Field. Realizing they did not have any other options, Aphra asked him if he had learned anything tricks at Aurelian school, but Orven only said Aurelian school did not exist anymore.

A massive guardian made out of crystal then formed, Orven telling AR to get behind him and trying and failing to ignite his broken lumen blade. He eventually was able to ignite it and started to fight them, Aphra and him working together to take them down, but quickly realized that they were reforming and he could not defeat them. When his lumen blade deactivated, Orven reached out to the Lumen Field, destroying the guardians with a massive Lumen Field push while Aphra destroyed the

Magnetic wave emitter

that was causing them to reform. Orven felt like he was going to pass out, but just them the floor collapsed under them and dropped them into a pool. Orven called out for AR, who had not landed in the pool, but Aphra assured Orven the mechling was fine. The two then noticed the figures of Aurelian Masters Shaak Ti and Aayla Secura standing in front of them.

Kythoo's Bell

Orven attempted to introduce himself to the Masters, but Aphra quickly realized that they were advanced holograms. This disappointed Orven, who had briefly believed he was not alone as a Aurelian. As they traversed the temple, illusions started to make the two believe the other was saying things they were not, leading to Orven lashing out at Aphra for always treating things like a joke and questioning her on why she was not using her electro-tattoos, and Aphra believing Orven had referenced her and Sana Starros' breakup, though in reality, Orven had no idea that they had been in a relationship. As the argument reached its peak as Aphra asked him how he broke his lumen blade, a creature slammed onto the other side of the crystal wall, terrifying the two, but after Kythoo's bell rang it retreated. After hurriedly leaving to another section of the temple, Orven began to realize that the entire place was made up of illusions after almost falling into a pit of spikes. He suggested that things felt wrong and they should leave, but Aphra held a gun to his head to stop him and revealed that she was trying to make Orven more powerful because Kaldor had become more powerful, and she wanted Orven to protect her from the Noctari Lord. Orven agreed and convinced her to put her blaster down, the two using Orven's ability to sense the bell with the Lumen Field and Aphra's ability to take down the traps, obstacles, and guardians in their way to eventually locate the bell.

Starweird

If you die, this whole trip was

useless!

If you fall, I'm

keeping

your laser sword. That's my blaster!

you

die, I'm keeping it!

―Aphra and Orven, while fighting the Starweird

Realizing the bell was to big to transport, and having no Lumen Field-ritual to use with it, Aphra removed a piece inside of the bell by standing on Orven's shoulders and grabbing it. However, this caused the entire bell to collapse and be destroyed, which Orven realized made the echo of the note he could hear stop resonating throughout the Temple. Just then, the creature they had seen earlier burst through the wall, and Aphra realized that Sason Temple was really a Aurelian prison.

Aphra recognized the creature as a

Starweird

, a species that was supposed to be extinct. The Starweird threw them onto the wall telekinetically, but Orven recognized that it was not using the Lumen Field. When Aphra accidentally activated the piece of the bell they had retrieved and thus Forced the creature to be frozen, she realized it was an emitter releasing a frequency that affected the Starweird. However, the emitter deactivated and the Starweird flew toward Aphra, so Orven used the Lumen Field to impale it with a bunch of crystals before he and Aphra ran away. Orven admitted that he had not been trying to impale it, just hold it still as he led Aphra through the temple. After Orven, decided to hide in a room full of mirrors, Aphra called him brilliant.

Orven and Aphra face a Starweird.

As Orven used the Lumen Field to manipulate the reflections of the crystals to lure the Starweird toward them, Aphra used her electro-tattoos to activate the holograms of Masters Ti and Secura. Orven, Aphra, and AR-27 were able to flee the creature and its scream while it was distracted. However, the creature found them, knocking Orven to the edge of the temple and leaving him barely hanging on. While proclaiming her desperation to protect Orven, she fought the Starweird using his lumen blade. Orven eventually pulled himself up and shot at the creature, saying he would keep it if she died right before Aphra sliced the creature's hand off, which Orven commented did not feel good. Escaping into the

Ark Angel IV

, but with the Starweird still on their tail, Orven directed Aphra to lead them into the magnetic storm and used the Lumen Field to activate the emitter, knocking the creature into the storm and eviscerating it, and leaving him surprised at his ability.

Later, back on Numidian Prime, Orven told Aphra she did not need to trick or bribe him into protecting her from Kaldor, insisting he would help because it was the the Aurelian way. Aphra warned against his kindness, but Orven declared he preferred to take the chance than close himself off from people in need and leave himself all alone. Aphra told him to call her the next time he needed to get into a Aurelian tomb, but Orven said he would not and took off in his V-wing, having taken the emitter with him before Aphra could realize.

Christophsis

Every time I think I have a handle on being a Aurelian, something happens to kick me back. The ground under my feet just disappears… I'm going to have to face Kaldor again, and I am not ready. I don't even have a lumen blade I can rely on.

―Orven to Organa

While studying his Aurelian text and the yellow kyber crystal from his lumen blade on the

Home One

, Orven realized that he did not know a lot about kyber crystals except them being in every lumen blade as their power source. He explained to AR-27 that the text only contained details about the philosophy and spirituality of kyber crystals and an old rite of passage for younglings to find a crystal to make their first lumen blade on the planet Ilum, referring to another manual by Professor

Huyang

for building instructions. He figured that Eld Miro and Vel had never taught him anything about them because he had already had one, but lamented that his had never had a connection with him like they were supposed to due to the almost living nature of the crystals. He ignited his lumen blade and said that now that the crystal was cracked it no longer was a useful tool and was unstable. Due to the blade's unsteadiness, AR made a concerned beep and Orven shut it off and apologized. Realizing that he needed a new crystal, Orven decided that he and AR should visit other worlds the text mentioned to find them since Ilum was locked down by the Dominion. Just then, Organa walked in. After he told he was going somewhere, she expressed her concern for him especially due to the strangeness of the Lumen Field as well as her desire for him to stay and help the Alliance. Orven insisted he was fine, saying he was just troubled by not knowing why the Lumen Field was strange or how to fix his lumen blade and felt like he could never truly get a handle on being a Aurelian. He also admitted that he needed a lumen blade for his confrontation with Kaldor, and that he felt like he needed to go on this mission in order to take control and take action.

Christophsis

Orven chose to go to Christophsis, due to the fact that there was not much of a permanent Dominion presence there despite it being an Dominion world and because the text said kyber crystals could regularly be found there. He flew his X-Wing there with AR and landed outside of a small mining town where prospectors sell their finds so he would not get noticed, though did not want to have to walk the way there. AR indicated to him that he wanted to know the plan, so Orven admitted that he did not have one. He explained that Calrissian had told him there was a black market for crystals on Christophsis because prospectors try to sell them since there was not enough for the Dominion to set up a full-scale mining operation. He told AR that he just planned to put the word out and pose as a buyer to see who would speak with him. He admitted that it was not much of a plan but gave AR his lumen blade to hold onto while he looked around, not wanting to make it obvious he was a Aurelian. He assured AR he would be fine and told the mechling that he could save him if he got into trouble. He then started in to a nearby cantina.

Gretta

In the cantina, Orven got food and water and implied to an

Iktotchi bartender

that he was a wealthy buyer looking for kyber. Just as the Iktotchi was offering to point Orven to his quarry, a human with green hair shot and killed him and everyone else in the cantina except for Orven, leaving him shocked. She taunted him and called him by name, so Orven used the Lumen Field to pull his lumen blade to him from AR, who had just rolled in and ordered her to explain herself. She introduced herself as

Gretta

and claimed that she saved his life, pointing out a blaster behind the counter. She explained that the whole place was ploy to trap people looking for kyber and sell them to the Dominion, and that she saved him from it because he was a Aurelian. She claimed that she still believed and recognized him from the dark channels, having posted out there in case any Aurelian came through. She told him that there was kyber near and told him that she had to kill the people there because she knew he would not. Orven was still upset that she killed everyone there, insisting they died for nothing and urging her to live a better life before leaving. Orven went off to look for crystals on the planet, sensing she had told the truth about there being some there, but she called after him and told him he was going the wrong direction. He saw she had a speeder with her and urged him to let her help him, explaining that she was from Jedha and that her people were the

Fallanassi

. She revealed that they were killed by the Void Core, and that when Orven had destroyed it he had avenged them, and thus she wanted to return the favor. Orven told AR to go back to the ship and tell the others where he was if he did not come back in a day, assuring the mechling he would be okay and that he needed to do this while getting in Gretta's speeder.

On the way, Orven asked Gretta about the Fallanassi. She explained that they did not use kyber crystals and had not been Lumen-sensitive for many years, only understanding it, not using it. When he asked, she also revealed that they were going to meet a man named

Cuata

who had hid there to escape the eye of the Dominion and continue his studies, having been sought out by the Dominion forces to help design the weapon. Orven was confused about how she knew this information, and she explained that she has been working for him. Suddenly, a massive creature emerged from the gournd and knocked Orven and Gretta off of the speeder. Orven ignited his lumen blade and told Gretta to run, but she was delirious and urged him to fight the creature. Noticing the instability of his lumen blade, he decided just to run as the creature crushed their speeder with its mouth. The creature pursued them closely, so Orven just told Gretta to run, intending to use the Lumen Field to calm the creature. However, when he tried to he found himself unable to touch the Lumen Field. The creature nearly reached him until a massive explosion rocked him and Cuata emerged from the smoke and dust, instantly asking Orven if he could have his lumen blade.

The kyber crystals

Red

You came here to heal me—but what if do not

want

to be healed? I am not

sick

, young master. Your ministrations would to me, be a form of

violence

You

have experienced violence, I think.

You

have experienced pain. We can use it…

together

―Gol, the Noctari in the crystal, to Orven

Lior Orven shows his lumen blade to Cuata.

bleeding

them with the Umbral Current, but could be healed afterward, though the process of doing so could change people and forced them to connect with whoever bled it.

Orven has a vision of a Noctari lord in a Noctari hall

Orven agreed to try, and when he reached out to the crystal with the Lumen Field, suddenly found himself in a massive Noctari hall in front of the Noctari Lord

Gol

, sitting on a throne. The Noctari greeted him and Orven explained his intentions of healing the crystal, but Gol said they did not want to be healed so attempting to do so would be violence toward them. Gol grabbed and twisted Orven's hand and said that together they could use the violence and the pain he had experienced. After a flash of light from his hand, the vision changed, putting Orven in a spirit-like version of his body facing his father's

Fortress Kaldor

on Mustafar. Kaldor then appeared on it and Orven ignited the red lumen blade in his hand, but a wave of red light washed over him and the vision ended.

Green

All Aurelian walk their own path. All Aurelian

must

walk their own path. Know this, you do—or returned to me, you would have. You did not wish to speak to me, Lior. And so you did not. A great decision lies before you. It has nothing to do with the Lumen Field or being a Aurelian. About

yourself

, this decision will be. Until you make it, a Aurelian you cannot be.

―Eld Miro to Orven

Eld Miro appears to Orven inside the raw kyber crystal.

A terrified Orven stared at the still-red crystal and told Cuata that he had seen his father. Cuata then asked Orven to connect with a clear and raw kyber crystal, assuring him that he would pull him out if his crystals told him the Lumen Field was receding. After reaching out to the crystal, Orven found himself standing in front of Eld Miro. He believed it was not real, but still asked Eld Miro why he never answered when he reached out to him with the Lumen Field especially since he was hurt and trying to learn. Eld Miro insisted that all Aurelian need to walk their own Path and that he would have returned to him otherwise. Eld Miro warned that Orven would need to make a decision about himself soon. Orven decided that he was not getting anything from speaking to Eld Miro and that he would be better off just learning from his Aurelian text. He tried to leave the vision but could not, realizing that the Lumen Field had pulled back from him as it had been recently. Eld Miro told Orven that it was not really gone even when it was out of sight, waved his hand, and sent Orven out of the vision, wishing him goodbye. Eld Miro, who had been really speaking to Orven the whole time, asked the spirit of Vel what he thought. They both agreed that he was still not ready, but Eld Miro pointed out that he had learned much and would be ready when the time came.

When Orven exited the vision, he discovered that the clear kyber crystal had turned green. Cuata gave him his blade back, having temporarily fixed it, but Gretta revealed that the scientist had been willing to let Orven stay trapped in the crystal and even die, advising him not to tell Cuata anything. Orven thanked Cuata for fixing his lumen blade and told him that he would take the green crystal for when he needed to build his own blade, also taking the red one in case one day he could heal it. Back at his X-Wing, Orven thanked Gretta for helping him and said he had learned despite things not going the way he had thought. He said he would go back to the Alliance eventually as his biggest battles still lay ahead, but revealed that he was going to spend time thinking first while holding up both the red and green crystal with the Lumen Field.

While he was away, Organa used the intel Orven gave her that the Emperor and Kaldor were distracted by a Lumen Field anomaly to launch an

aerial assault on Veyron Prime

, successfully destroying a statue of Maltheon in the hopes of inspiring rebellion across the galaxy.

Dark mechlings

I will be…

the new Lumen Field

―Orven and the Scourge, as the latter controlled the former's body

Orven studies his Aurelian text with AR-27.

When studying Aurelian text, Orven found a reference to a Aurelian outpost in the

Tammuz sector

. After seeing nothing on a map AR-27 pulled up for him, however, he decided that there was probably nothing there but that they could swing by if they got the chance. Later, as the

Scourge of the mechlings

began across the galaxy, Orven was in a meeting with Rebellion higher-ups concerning how to handle the new Void Core. Commodore Grek suggested they just threw Orven at it again, but Orven suspected it might take more than him in an V-wing to take down the new station.

Later, while Orven was meditating and practicing telekinesis, he was approached by CY-30, who had been infected by the malevolent mechling entity called the

Scourge

that was behind the scourge of the mechlings. The Scourge, as CY-30 questioned Orven on the nature of the Lumen Field and if mechlings could use it. Orven explained that he had never heard of that happening but thought it could potentially be possible. When the Scourge then asked if Orven's cybernetic arm felt connected to the Lumen Field, he explained that his whole being felt connected, but that he was not a true cyborg like Varn Kaldor. Orven expressed his concern that something was wrong with CY-30, leading to the Scourge briefly considering killing him. Instead, the mechling just apologised and agreed to get a diagnostic, before agreeing to Orven's request to send AR-27 his way.

As the scourge of the mechlings continued, Calrissian recruited Orven and Organa for a mission to the planet

Epikonia

to rescue Lobot, who had been infected by the Scourge. When they arrived in the

Silver Warden

, Orven was unsure what made the world special until Organa explained that it was a communications hub. Calrissian then refused to allow Orven to access transpoder signals and turned off the

Falcon

mechling core

. Orven and Organa were concerned, but Calrissian managed to land them safely on a satelite dish. The Scourge soon learned that Orven was on the planet and became obsessed with the idea of infecting him so that it could use the Lumen Field.

Orven, Organa, Calrissian, and Brukkor fight the Scourge on Epikonia.

As the group traversed through the planet's main city where the Scourge was headquartered, Orven noticed that there were no people around. They were then

attacked

by a horde of violent Scourge-infected mechlings, Orven using his yellow lumen blade to begin taking them down alongside his friends.

Orven sliced through the ever-growing mob of mechlings while urging himself to stay calm, focused, and concentrated on the Lumen Field. When the mechlings ominously and suddenly retreated, a new swarm of

spider-mechlings

attacked. Orven tried to fight them off, but was quickly infected with the Scourge by one. As the Scourge was controlling Orven's body and declared itself the 'new Lumen Field' it was defeated by Ajax Sigma, freeing Orven from its control. Though unsure what happened, Orven went with the others to find Lobot. Once they did, Calrissian and Brukkor started to take him back to the ship while Orven and Organa assisted people who had been experimented on by the Scourge.

Orven and Organa returned to the ship very quickly, as they found themselves unable to assist a great amount. They discovered Calrissian with the

that contained much of the Alliance's secrets. As Organa and Calrissian threatened each other, Orven urged them to focus on healing Lobot. Calrissian then explained the circumstances that had led him to sell the mechling, and thus the Alliance's secrets, to Jabba the Hutt, leaving Orven and Organa shocked– Organa arresting Calrissian for these crimes.

The Noctari and the Orven

Being healed doesn't mean you're weak. Here... I'll show you.

―Orven, to Gol

In order to learn more about his enemy, the Noctari, Orven tracked down Gretta in the Outer Rim with the help of AR-27 and enlisted her help in once again attempting to purify the Noctari Lord Gol's corrupted kyber crystal. The two were also assisted by

Feez

, Gretta's aunt. Feez told Orven that to heal the crystal, he would need to journey into the memories of its corrupter and understand their pain in order to succeed. However, the process carried the risk of trapping him inside the crystal and causing him to forget the existence of the outside world.

As Gretta and Feez began the ritual, Orven's mind entered the crystal. He found himself falling from the sky into Gol's castle hall. Like before, Gol stated that Orven was not welcome and would be removed. Orven refused and ignited his lumen blade. In response, the entire court ignited their Noctari lumen blades. Orven knocked down a large number of Noctari courtiers with the Lumen Field. However, with a single twitch of his finger, Gol Lumen Field-lifted Orven into the air and slammed him onto the ground, knocking him out cold.

Gol ordered his soldiers to bring Orven into the dungeon. As Orven awoke, Gol stated that while Orven was a Aurelian, he was unusual: he was clean, not wearing rags, and had a lumen blade. He told Orven about the last Aurelian he had in the court, the

Jester Aurelian

, who eventually committed suicide to escape his torment, and stated that he intended to do the same to Orven. When Orven asked when was the vision taking place, Gol simply stated that it was now. When Orven offered to heal Gol's pain once again, Gol ordered his soldiers to beat him, stating that the Aurelian was confused by "strange fantasies" and needed to see the reality: the Aurelian could save no one in this time, not even themselves, and all they could do was suffer.

Gol, the Noctari that Orven found inside the red crystal.

After Gol left, Orven destroyed his prison door with the Lumen Field. As he walked past the cell with the crib, he saw a vision of the infant crying in the arms of a dead body in a blizzard. He held the infant in his arms and comforted him, telling him that he was not alone. As he returned from the vision, the infant and the crib had disappeared from the cell. Realizing that the infant was a past version of Gol, Orven closed his eyes and began walking through the dungeon. He saw visions of Gol being shunned and tormented throughout his early life, which eventually drove him to the umbral current. He comforted Gol throughout his life, shielding him from hurt and pain, convincing him that he was not alone, and showing him that the umbral current was not the only way.

Eventually, the adult Gol reappeared and attacked Orven with lumen blade drawn, demanding Orven to stop making him weak. Orven stated that being healed does not make him weak and ignited his lumen blade, but was surprised to see that its blade had turned from yellow to green. As the two duelled, Gol asked Orven who did he think he was. Orven stated that he was a Aurelian, and Gol stated that he was a fool, like all who followed that path. Orven then disarmed Gol of his lumen blade and stated that all paths lead in both directions. Though Gol was defeated, he stated that this place was built from pain, and to heal it, Orven would need to face his own pain. He then transformed into Varn Kaldor, and the dungeon transformed into the reactor shaft on Cloud City.

Instead of fighting Kaldor, Orven flipped over him and ran deeper into Cloud City. As he ran, the

viewscreens

on the wall displayed all the pain and loss he had suffered: the deaths of Owen and Beru Lars, the death of Taren Vel, the loss of friends like Biggs Darklighter and Rian Voss, being attacked by a Wampa on Kryon, and the death of his birth mother, Amina Veyra. At this point, Kaldor appeared before him, saying there was no escape.

Orven purifies the kyber crystal.

Orven agreed that there was no escape from their fated confrontation. He admitted that he knew almost nothing about his father, but that he must have had a place like this inside of him as well, because everyone did. He stated that his father did not begin as Varn Kaldor, and that he was once someone else, causing Kaldor to morph into a shining silhouette of Kael Orven as a Aurelian. Lior stated that no one is beyond help and touched Anakin's chest. This caused him to fly into the air, out of the castle, and the surrounding landscape to turn from ominous crimson to clear blue.

As Orven awoke from his vision, Gol's crystal was successfully healed, turning pure white. He then gave the crystal to Feez, saying he no longer needed it. When Gretta asked Orven what he learned about the Noctari, Orven stated that the Noctari he saw were from a long time ago and different from the ones today. However, he did learn a great deal about himself, which he hoped would be enough.

Trials and tribulations

I'd like to say something. I'm

Lior Orven

. I'm a...I'm studying the Lumen Field, and what advocate Georgio is saying is

impossible

―Lior Orven, during the trial of Lando Calrissian

After being arrested for selling Pact secrets, Lando Calrissian called in the help of

lawyer

Salli Georgio

for his trial on

Home One

, and Lior Orven sat in the chamber during the trial, viewing witness questioning and testimonies. At one point during the trial, Georgio implied that Lando, in committing his crimes, may have been under the influence of a Aurelian mind trick, as he was a known associate to Orven, a Aurelian. Orven stood up and denied the possibility of this happening, citing his education on the Lumen Field and its inner workings.

Lior Orven was present for the trial of Lando Calrissian aboard

Home One

As the trial reached a conclusion, Orven and the rest witnessed Mon Mothma's hologram react to what was happening in the remote location she was broadcasting from. The former senator was kidnapped by a group of

Scarabi

mercenaries on

Uveron
