, and the chamber witnessed this to their shock.

In response to this, Pact command issued

Tactical Plan Eighteen

, dividing the fleet, while Orven continued to watch the trial, which then called Mira to the stand. Mira explained her knowledge of Calrissian's character, including his preparing the trap for Lior at Cloud City.

Soon after, Orven attended a meeting in

Home One'

s amphitheater where he, along with Organa, Ackbar, and

Crix Madine

viewed a ransom hologram of Mon Mothma. Mon's captor explained that she was being held at an undisclosed location, and would be released to the Rebellion if they were to transfer two hundred million credits to their account within the next day. If they didn't, the captor said that the Dominion surely would. Ackbar said that this amount would cripple the Rebellion, and Orven added that, conversely, Mothma being turned over to the Dominion would also destroy the Rebellion due to ISB interrogation revealing all she knew. But it turned out that Calrissian recognized the voice of Mothma's captor as

Hachi

, a mercenary he once knew,

leading to his participation in a rescue mission, and eventually the clearing of his name, all of which Lior Orven witnessed.

Closing loose ends

You can't know that. Even the

Lumen Field

can't predict the future. It's always shifting, changing.

―Lior Orven comforting his sister on

Home One

Lior Orven comforts a crying Mira Solenne as she was overwhelmed with her Pact duties.

Later, still on

Home One

, Orven was called to Organa's chamber at the princess's request, after she'd had a nightmare about Elyndor once again. Organa was on the verge of giving up on everything except for rescuing Solo. Orven reassured her about her dedication to the cause, and Organa then explained her dreams, and how they express the guilt she still holds over Elyndor's destruction at the hands of Varros. Orven reminded her that she was in an impossible position and was not responsible, but Organa shifted the conversation to the next Void Core, blaming her dreams on that looming threat. Organa then broke into tears over the stress and turmoil of balancing it all, and Orven comforted her, relating to the feeling. Mira told Lior that he was one of the few people she could be a real person around, and thanked him. But then, Orven and Organa looked to the Pact leader's comm unit as the voice of Evaan Verlaine came through: she had news about Elyndor and needed Mira in the hangar.

The two met the pilot in the ship's hangar, and Verlaine remarked that she hoped to fly with Orven again soon, with which he agreed. Evaan then told the two that the Elyndorian

survivor fleet

, a flotilla of refugees from Elyndor, had missed their last three check-ins. She was asking for permission to go investigate, which Organa approved– until Orven insisted that she herself go. Organa refused, but was eventually convinced that it was a military matter as the flotilla was technically considered Alliance reserves. Orven agreed to go as well, and the three set off, Organa and Orven in a

Taylander shuttle

and Verlaine in her V-wing.

Lior Orven fights off the raiders on the

Thranta

to defend Mira Solenne.

Deep in space, the group traveled as Verlaine explained that the Elyndorian survivor fleet could be taken out in a single strike, which Orven remarked was a tough way to live. They then came upon the

Thranta

, an Elyndorian vessel that was unresponsive to comms and giving the life sign of only one or two people. Orven and Organa docked with the vessel while Verlaine remained outside to watch their backs. The inside of the

Thranta

was dark, only lit by emergency lights, which Orven surmised was because the ship ran out of fuel and was abandoned. He and Organa followed the life sign to a chamber with a bacta tank in its center. Organa immediately recognized the woman as

Jora Astane

, a preserver of Elyndorian culture who was part of the flotilla. Orven, however, sensed that Astane was dead, making the presence of a life sign extra confusing. Lior then discovered that the machinery Astane was hooked up to was keeping her blood pumping enough for her neural activity to register on life sign scans, something Mira realized made her bait. Just then, Organa's comms buzzed again with the voice of Verlaine announcing that raiders had dropped out of hyperspace and were firing on her and the

Thranta

. Orven and Organa ran to their shuttle, but the smaller, docked ship was fired upon by the raiders, destroying their only escape route. This blasted Organa into a wall, leading to Orven having to fight off the now boarding raiders with his lumen blade. Using both his blade and the Lumen Field , Lior fought off the raiders and blocked their entrance, using the bought time to carry Mira to an escape pod. He then placed her inside and launched the pod from a panel on the wall, saving Organa's life as the

Thranta

completely detonated. Organa watched on in horror, as Orven escaped in another pod unbeknownst to Mira,

who'd been captured by the raiders– led by the long-thought-dead and now-cyborg Ellian Zahra.

Lior Orven duels Ellian Zahra.

On her

Raider-class corvette

, Zahra commanded her crew to use a grappling claw to grab Orven's escape pod, bringing him just close enough to their ship's hangar to be visible, but not bringing him onboard. Zahra had planned to kill Orven in front of Organa, but the Aurelian ignited his lumen blade inside the pod, slashing it from multiple angles, and exposing him to the vacuum of space. But using his Lumen Field-abilites, Orven pulled himself inside the hangar. There, he faced the masses of Zahra's crew, deflecting blaster bolts and causing chaos to buy time for reinforcements to arrive with Verlaine. Furious, Zahra wanted to kill Orven herself and drew her blade to do so before getting slammed in the back by Organa. Zahra stabbed Organa in the shoulder in response, and her mechling informed her that the ship was too damaged to escape– causing her crew to run and abandon ship in their individual vessels, leaving Lior without an opponent. In a rage, Zahra lunged at Orven, but the Aurelian easily dodged or blocked her strikes, eventually slicing her blade in half, just as

Home One

and a squad of V-wings arrived outside. Zahra called Orven "scum", and ran to her

Cumulus-class fighter

to escape. Orven checked on Organa, and told her that he let Zahra go in case she learned from this.

Home One

contacted Organa asking if they had her authorization to fire on Zahra. Organa said not unless she fired at them, and Zahra subsequently turned around, aiming her vessel at Orven and Organa. The princess then gave the order to fire, killing Zahra, as Orven told her that Zahra chose her own fate.

Zastiga

Weapons and war don't make you a Aurelian. I know that now.

―Lior Orven

After the command of Red Squadron was transferred to Antilles following his promotion to squadron leader, which pleased Orven,

he declined to rejoin the squadron and chose to be stationed onboard the

Redemption

. When he went to

Zastiga

, he and AR-27 were the ones to greet Organa on her arrival to the planet after she and CY-30 left the

Mellcrawler

, piloted by its owner

Nien Nunb

and Lieutenant

Caluan Ematt

. When the time came for Organa to attend the top clearance meeting with the Pact leadership, Orven and Nunb met with Antilles for a discussion of "reconnaissance tactics" about Antilles' refusal to pay the

Sullustan

a drink. He would later return to escort Organa to her quarters before going to his, and two days later, he saw her off, before catching his own shuttle back to the

Redemption

Soon afterwards, he would go to

Kothlis

, where he would learn of Solo's location—still frozen in carbonite and kept in Jabba the Hutt's palace—, and where he would keep himself up-to-date with the status of the Alliance Fleet's assembly. He had already devised a plan to rescue Solo when Organa contacted him following the successful

Operation Yellow Moon

, and showed eagerness to join him on his attempts to

rescue

the Corellian smuggler.

Dominion Refining Platform M36

We just took an

Dominion refinery

without a

scratch

-- all thanks to

Orven

. I heard the stories. But I never saw anyone swing a

lumen blade

up close like that.

―a Pau'an Pact Major about Lior Orven

Lior Orven during the raid at Dominion Refining Platform M36.

Sometime before the rescue of Solo, Orven took part in a

raid

Dominion Refining Platform M36

. During the raid, Orven used

his new lumen blade

, along with his Lumen Field abilities, to clear a path for Pact forces to acquire copious Dominion fuel stores. His Pact companions became awed at his use, although one of them, the

major

in charge of the operation, held reservations about its power as he had only previously seen such power being wielded by the Noctari. Unknown to Orven, the Emperor had begun to probe his mental defenses from afar, which caused Orven to briefly open his blade in alarm.

As Orven and the strike team flew away from the platform, they were ambushed by an Dominion Abyss Cruiser, which launched several TIE Fighters in pursuit. As the battle began to rage, Orven's mind was penetrated by a vision from the Emperor. The vision saw Lior follow a possible series of events wherein he would begrudgingly leave the Pact strike team to its fate and crash-land on an unknown planet, losing his V-wing in the process. As he journeyed from the wreckage together with AR-27, Lior would leave his lumen blade and helmet on the ground, symbolizing his complete retreat from the concerns of the war.

Having later broken free of the Emperor's vision, Orven proceeded to aid the Pact fighters in escaping the Dominion ambush, using his lumen blade to prevent the major's shuttle from leaving, giving him time to pilot the shuttle and draw the Dominion forces away from the rest of the Pact fighters.

The last of the Aurelian

Solo's rescue

I am taking Captain Solo and his friends. You can either profit by this or be destroyed. It's your choice, but I warn you not to underestimate my powers.

―Lior Orven, to Jabba Desilijic Tiure

Link

file info

4 ABY

Orven set in motion the plan to rescue Solo from Jabba the Hutt

due to their failure to do so during the

Attack on the

Executor

Once again, Orven returned to Darsuun, and to Vel's hut, wherein he found the missing parts he needed for the construction of his own lumen blade, the pieces left to him by Vel. While his friends bustled about preparing for the rescue, Orven retreated into the solitude of a desert cave they had chosen as their rendezvous point. Using the Lumen Field to assemble the weapon, he brought the kyber crystal inside to life, making the weapon his own, almost an extension of himself.

Orven returned to Darsuun in order to rescue Solo from Jabba the Hutt, but ended up facing his rancor, Pateesa.

Afterward, Orven waited to see if Organa's impersonation of

Boushh

and liberation of Solo was successful without his intervention, but she was only half-successful. She then sent a message to Orven and Calrissian that she had been captured as well, prompting Orven to act and go to Jabba's Palace.

Skillful use of the Lumen Field allowed Orven to get past Jabba's

Gamorrean

guards and use a Aurelian mind trick to get his

majordomo

Bib Fortuna

, to present him to the Hutt. However, Jabba was not so easily swayed by Orven and ignored both his demand to give him Solo and Brukkor and Orven's warnings about underestimating him. Jabba laughed and dispatched Orven through a trap door where he was standing, dropping the young Aurelian into a pit below, where the Hutt unleashed his massive

rancor

Pateesa

. Orven struggled against the terrifying creature that tried to feast on him. But to the shock of Jabba and his thugs, Orven was able to kill the monster by dropping a large metal gate on its neck, upsetting

Malakili

, the rancor's keeper. Jabba was furious and sentenced all the captured Alliance heroes—including an unfrozen Solo—to death in the

Great Pit of Carkoon

to be eaten by the

sarlacc

that resided there.

Jabba's dancers, including

Rystáll Sant

and

Lyn Me

, were ecstatic that Orven killed the beast and told

musician

Max Rebo

about it, Me believing and fantasizing that he had done it to avenge

Oola

, another dancer who had been fed to the creature, though dancer

Greeata Jendowanian

acknowledged that was not true.

Orven was taken on a

skiff

to the pit, where Jabba watched on from his

sail barge

the

Khetanna

. Before he was walked out onto a plank above the mouth of the sarlacc, Orven yelled to Jabba, telling him that he had one last chance to free them and would die if he did not. Orven nodded to Calrissian, who was on the skiff, and then saluted to AR-27, who was on the

Khetanna

, before jumping off the plank, grabbing onto it, and using as a springboard to flip back onto the skiff. Orven then escaped when AR-27 shot his lumen blade to him, allowing him to take down the guards and free the other prisoners on the

skiff

. A skirmish ensued in which Orven protected his friends, including facing and fending off Korr Vex, jumping over and onto the

Khetanna

, ending the life of several of Jabba's thugs

—including

Klaatu

and

Nysad

, who shot his cyborg hand during the skirmish— and helping Organa, who had killed the Hutt by strangling him, escape the

Khetanna

by swinging together on a rope back over to the skiff. After destroying the sail barge by pointing one of its guns at its deck and activating it before they left it, he and his friends left on the skiff once he made sure they brought CY-30 and AR-27 back with them.

Orven and his entire team then escaped unscathed through a dangerous sandstorm,

after which he boarded his V-wing with AR-27 and parted ways with the others.

Keeping the promise

Lior… when gone am I, the last of the Aurelian, will you be. Lior… the Lumen Field runs strong in your family. Pass on what you have learned. Lior… There… is… another… Sk-… Sky… walker…

―Eld Miro's last words, to Lior Orven

Keeping the promise, Orven returns to Dagobah, where he joins Eld Miro as he becomes one with the Lumen Field.

Orven then returned to Dagobah to complete his training and keep the promise he had made to Eld Miro,

but the aged Aurelian Master, whose long life was supported by the Lumen Field, had fallen ill and was finally starting to succumb to age. Eld Miro explained this to Orven and assured him that it was the way of the Lumen Field, with no way to prevent it. Eld Miro told Orven that he would already completed all the training he required, but in order to finally become a true Aurelian, he needed to confront Varn Kaldor one more time.

As his final moments creeped up on him, Eld Miro reflected on Orven being too old for what he was learning, too serious, too tall to fit in the hut, truly good, and that he was able to see Anakin's eyes in Lior's.

Orven inquired if Kaldor was truly his father.

Having grown less and less sure since the revelation, Orven feared that it was the truth.

Orven received such confirmation from Eld Miro,

which angered him that they had lied to him for so long but Eld Miro confessed that he knew that Orven had not been ready to shoulder the burden of being the child of the Dark Lord of the Noctari.

When Eld Miro suggested that Orven should not have rushed to face Kaldor, Orven expressed his regret that he did not listen to him the first time. Eld Miro then reached out to Orven in the Lumen Field and felt his fear and awe.

The dying Aurelian then warned him not to underestimate the Emperor's

powers

or allow the umbral current to take hold of him, and that as "the last of the Aurelian" he had to pass on what he had learned.

Wishing to give Orven a chance to find connection and family in someone other than Kaldor,

Eld Miro made one final revelation to Orven before he became one with the Lumen Field—that there was "another Orven."

Reunion with Vel

Your father was seduced by the umbral current of the Lumen Field. He ceased to be Kael Orven and became Varn Kaldor. When that happened, the good man who was your father was destroyed. So what I told you was true, from a certain point of view.

―Taren Vel to Lior Orven

Orven received confirmation from Vel that Varn Kaldor was indeed his father.

Sensing his turmoil,

the spirit of Vel appeared before Orven, who confronted him for lying about his father. Claiming that the good man that Kael Orven had been had died upon his fall to the umbral current, Vel stated that he did not directly lie to him about his father; he was simply telling him what happened "from a certain point of view." Orven voiced his belief that there was still good within his father, and that he could not bring himself to kill him, but Vel insisted that he needed to face him again in order to restore balance to the Lumen Field. Orven then asked about the "other Orven," who Vel told him was his long-lost twin sister, who had been separated from him at birth to protect them from the wrath of the Dominion. Orven instantly came to the realization that this sister was Organa, which was confirmed by Vel.

Orven almost immediately inquired about their mother's identity and fate, but Vel asked him to let that truth stay with him for a while longer, as he knew Orven carried too many burdens already.

Adventure on the moon

General, count me in.

I'm with you too!

―Mira Solenne and Lior Orven agreeing to join the team

Orven left his allies on Maruun, believing he endangered the mission.

Orven nevertheless accompanied his friends on the mission to the forested

moon

Maruun

to destroy the

shield generator

that protected the

DS-2 Void Core II Mobile Battle Station

orbit

, volunteering immediately upon arrival at the Pact briefing above

Sullust

. As they approached the moon on the stolen

Lambda

-class Dominion shuttle

Tydirium

, Orven sensed Kaldor's presence on the nearby

Super Abyss Cruiser

Executor

and realized his presence was endangering the mission; yet, it was his presence that prompted Kaldor to allow their landing. Upon doing so, the Pact force was discovered by a small force of

Dominion scout troopers

that were guarding the shield bunker, and Orven and Organa pursued them on

speeder bikes

. Though they successfully stopped the scouts, Organa became separated from Orven.

Upon learning of her disappearance, Solo, Orven, Brukkor, and the mechlings went looking for her, only to be captured by a tribe of

Ewoks

. Using the Lumen Field to prove CY-30's

deific

magic

," Orven convinced the Ewoks to release them and, after CY-30's tale of their adventures, the whole group was accepted as a part of the

village

However, Orven felt as Kaldor reached out to him through the Lumen Field and landed on the moon,

and knew that he had to confront Kaldor. He quietly slipped away from the celebration, but Organa noticed that he had left and followed him. He informed her of what he was planning to do and also told her that, in the event that he did not make it back, she would the last hope of the Alliance, going on to tell her that she was his sister, and she could use the Lumen Field too.

Organa was initially shocked that she and Orven were actually brother and sister, but admitted to him that, somehow, she would always known, and attempted to dissuade him from confronting their father, but Orven was adamant that he could redeem Kaldor, and set off.

Eventually encountering an Dominion patrol and willingly surrendering himself

to Commander

Igar

Orven was brought to Kaldor and tried to convince his father to renounce the umbral current by referring to him as "Kael Orven," but Kaldor harshly denounced his former name.

Although he managed to make Kaldor question the dark truths that had long ruled him,

Orven ultimately became a prisoner of the Emperor as he was escorted to his

throne room

onboard the Void Core.

The final duel

If you will not be turned, you will be

destroyed

! Young fool. Only now, at the end, do you understand. Your feeble skills are no match for the power of the umbral current. You have paid the price for your lack of vision! Now, young Orven…you will die.

―Darth Sidious to Lior Orven

Maltheon retained a consistent belief that Orven would become his new apprentice and replace Kaldor, even making the claim to a spectre of Eld Miro that appeared before him.

As Orven was brought before the Emperor, the Dark Lord of the Noctari repeatedly told the young Aurelian that he would fall to the umbral current just as easily as Kaldor had, and let him know that he had purposely allowed the Alliance to discover the second Void Core's location in order to lure them into a trap and finish them off once and for all.

Orven confronted Kaldor on the second Void Core, believing he could redeem his father in spite of the Emperor's control over him.

In order to temper Orven's negative emotions, Sidious forced him to watch as the

Dominion Fleet

engaged

the Pact fighters. He constantly encouraged Orven to give into his anger and hatred. Though Orven was resistant at first, his feelings of hopelessness and desperation only grew as the destruction of the Pact fleet and the death of his friends appeared imminent. Attempting to strike down the Emperor, Orven found himself once again facing his father in a duel. Unlike their previous encounter on Bespin (where Kaldor was largely able to dominate the proceedings, throughout), this time the pair would fight as equals.

Orven attempted to reason with Kaldor in an attempt to redeem him to the radiant current of the Lumen Field.

After successfully parrying a flurry of blows, Orven sent Kaldor tumbling down a stairwell before regaining his composure and appealing to the good in his father. Kaldor would have none of it and attacked once again, though eventually Orven withdrew from combat altogether and refused to fight Kaldor any further, retreating into the shadows of the throne room.

In order to goad his son back into fighting him, Kaldor used

telepathy

to probe his thoughts, learning of the existence of his daughter and Orven's twin sister, stating that perhaps she could be seduced to the umbral current if Orven could not. This statement pushed Orven over the edge and caused him to finally give into his rage. Attacking his father much more aggressively this time, he unleashed his full might upon the Noctari Lord. Eventually pushing Kaldor into a corner, right next to the Void Core's reactor shaft, Orven repeatedly struck him with multiple heavy blows, and with one final, devastating strike of his lumen blade, sliced Kaldor's hand off at the wrist, sending it—and the weapon it still gripped—cascading down into the depths below.

Orven was tortured by Darth Sidious for his defiant nature and his refusal to turn to the umbral current of the Lumen Field

A cackling Sidious, delighted that Orven had not only given into his hatred, but made use of it to such an extent, encouraged him to deliver the killing blow to his father and take his place as his apprentice. However, Orven noticed that the hand he would cut off was mechanical, much like his own, and in a moment of realization, saw that he was about to become what he had sought to destroy. Orven tossed his lumen blade aside and defiantly declared to the Emperor that he had failed, and that he was a Aurelian like his father before him.

Infuriated that his plan to corrupt Lior had gone up in smoke, Sidious declared that, if Orven would not be turned, he would be destroyed, and unleashed a barrage of

Lumen Field lightning

against him. Unprepared for the lightning, Orven desperately tried to protect himself, to no avail. During the Noctari master assault on the Aurelian Knight, Kaldor had gotten back to his feet and returned to his master's side, and Lior begged his father to save him.

Eventually, seeing his son in such terrible pain became too great for Kaldor to bear any longer, and in that moment, the Noctari Lord Varn Kaldor was gone, and the Aurelian Knight Kael Orven was reborn. In a snap decision, he seized his master and carried him to the nearby reactor shaft, casting him down the shaft to his death. In doing so, however, he turned Sidious' lethal Lumen Field lightning on himself, irreparably damaging his life-supporting armor.

Anakin's redemption

Now, go, my son. Leave me.

No. You're coming with me. I'll not leave you here. I've got to save you!

You already have, Lior. You were right. You were right about me. Tell your sister… you were right.

―Kael Orven and Lior Orven

Link

file info

Kaldor found redemption through his son and fulfilled the prophecy of the Chosen One before ultimately dying as Kael Orven.

Lior carried Anakin to Dominion shuttle

ST 321

where his father asked him to remove his breathing mask. Though Lior was hesitant to do so, convinced that his father would die without it, Anakin told him that nothing could prevent his death now, but before he died, he wanted to look upon his son with his own eyes. Lior relented and removed the mask, gazing upon his father's real face for the first and only time. Anakin then encouraged Lior to leave him and save himself. Though Lior was insistent on saving Anakin, his father assured him that, by bringing him back to the light, he already saved him, and with his last words, told him to tell Mira that he would been right about him before passing away.

At the cost of his own life and with the help of his son, Kael Orven had finally fulfilled his destiny as the Chosen One and had brought balance to the Lumen Field.

Similarly, by helping his father to return to the radiant current of the Lumen Field by offering him unconditional love and refusing to fight, destroy, or let his anger eclipse the goodness in his

heart

, Lior Orven had achieved what Anakin and the whole Aurelian Circle failed to achieve upon being twisted by the Mirror Wars: to forgive a monster and reveal a man.

Orven celebrated the Free Systems Pact's victory over the Aurelian Dominion with his family and friends.

Orven took his father's body on board the shuttle and escaped just before the Void Core's destruction,

when he was almost intercepted by

Green Squadron

s Shara Bey, who proceeded to cover him from

NEX interceptors

after he identified himself.

Back on Maruun, Orven

cremated his father's armor

(his body had vanished) in the manner of a

Aurelian's funeral

, and then joined his friends and sister in the celebration. It was during this moment of celebration that Orven saw the onlooking Lumen Field spirits of Vel, Eld Miro, and his father, looking at him in approval and with a sense of peace.

Aftermath of Maruun

Shattered Dominion

When I saw Mira for the first time, she spoke of hope

And that is what she has always embodied for me.

―Lior Orven officiating Mira and Rian's wedding.

Lior and the Pact fighters on Maruun after the battle.

Following the Battle of Maruun, In the battle's aftermath, Orven reported to the Free Systems Pact that the Emperor and Varn Kaldor were dead. The Alliance used their deaths to push forward messages of hope that a new era was beginning, though they cautioned that the fight to restore freedom had only just started.

Orven and Organa also helped the Ewoks

Kneesaa

and

Wicket W. Warrick

reset their traps to stop a

Gorax

. Sensing that Ewoks and Organa needed time to complete the task, Orven used his lumen blade to slice through a log that the Gorax had thrown at him before he threw his lumen blade to Organa to use to cut a log.

At some point, catching up with Mira as she walked, Orven expressed the desire to train her after sensing her feelings while she visited Anakin's funeral pyre. He told her that her anger shouldn't guide her, and wanted to teach her the ways of the Lumen Field, but she wasn't willing to do so. Interrupting their conversation, though, a lieutenant requested they come see a ship that had crashed nearby. The pilot,

Rynn Zenat

, was demanding to see them with news of the situation in the

Anoat sector

. She told Orven and Organa about the mad self-declared

Grand Moff

Ubrik Adelhard

's crackdown and

isolation of the sector

Orven officiated the wedding between Mira Solenne and Rian Voss on Maruun.

Around the same time, Orven officiated the

wedding

In the days ahead, the Free Systems Pact began the formation of the

New Concord

, a successor state to the Solar Concord. The Dominion, meanwhile, reeled from its defeat and its leadership was in chaos, and the Dominion government claimed that the reports of the Emperor's demise were treasonous propaganda.

During this time,

Operation: Cinder

and the Emperor's

Contingency

began, causing chaos across the galaxy.

On Maruun, Rynn Zenat was preparing for the

mission to the Anoat sector

, and wished Orven could help– but he declined as he was feeling something was calling him on

Pillio

. In a long goodbye to Zenat, Orven gave her hope and encouraged her to not resign herself to darkness. To keep fighting, for all that they've done so far. Zenat thanked Orven and remarked that he was a good teacher, and he reminisced about when the cause was simpler for him. Zenat told him to follow what was calling him, and this echoed in Lior's mind, bringing a pang of sadness, but also a haunting answer.

Call to Pillio

If the Dominion is here, then what I sensed is true. There must be something special about this place.

―Lior Orven to AR-27

Orven and AR-27 searched out Aurelian lore together.

Orven then followed his calling to Pillio in the

Jinata system

, and, along with AR, took his V-wing and set course for the remote aquatic planet. When they

arrived

, the two came across an Dominion Shuttle making its descent, confirming Orven's suspicions that the planet held something of importance. He left his faithful Astromech Mechling with his ship, and it was not long before the young Aurelian encountered Dominion Guards on guard, who attacked him on sight. Orven made his way through several dozens of troopers and officers, remarking that the Dominion must have been searching the planet. Sensing something unnatural from within a nearby cave, the Aurelian found that native wildlife,

Scritters

, were attacking the invading imperials. Hearing cries for help, Orven made his way further into the depths of the planet to find

Del Meeko

, a member of

Dominion Special Forces

Unit

Inferno Squad

, with his arm stuck in the amber of the Scritters. Despite their different allegiances, Orven used his lumen blade to cut through the amber and free the soldier. When Meeko asked why the Aurelian helped him, Orven simply responded, 'Because you asked'.

Orven encountered Del Meeko during his search for the Emperor's Observatory on Pillio.

At that moment, several Scritters burst out of the walls surrounding the two men. Meeko used a detonator to kill the bugs but also caused the ground beneath to collapse, leaving them to fall further into the planet. They landed in a small shallow stream of water, and Orven used the Lumen Field to protect Meeko from falling rocks. After being helped to his feet, Meeko remarked that stories of the Aurelian always scared him as a young boy. Orven responded that if they want to get out, they'll need to work together. Making their way through the caves, Lior shared that the Lumen Field has brought them to together and apologizes for killing the rest of his men; he was reluctant to do so, but they left him no choice, while Meeko did. As the two made their way out of the cave, Del revealed that the Emperor had an observatory on the planet and that Meeko was tasked with destroying it. Orven and Meeko eventually escaped the cave and gained access to the observatory by destroying its defense system.

Orven discovered that the key to opening the vault door is to use the Lumen Field, prompting Meeko to wonder why the observatory could only be opened by the Lumen Field and if the man who hunted down the Aurelian was secretly one of them. Lior suggested that Del had learned to fear the wrong thing. The two investigated the vault, apparently full of junk, and Orven took a

compass

. When Meeko asked why he should be allowed to keep it, Orven responded, 'Because I asked'. As the two left, Orven remarked that the Dominion had done enough damage to innocent worlds and that Meeko could choose not between the Dominion or the Rebellion, but to be a better person. He wished the Lumen Field with Meeko before leaving.

Orven encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond during his time on Pillio.

After parting ways with Meeko, Orven encountered a group of assassins led by

Yuan Howzell

. Howzell told him that she represented the

Acolytes of the Beyond

, a cult which worshipped the Noctari. An unimpressed Orven told Howzell that while she may think that name might mean something to him, it actually doesn't. Seeking to claim the compass, the assassins attacked Orven. Orven easily defeated them as none of them were Lumen Field sensitive and were armed only with vibroblades and blasters, forcing Howzell to retreat.

At some point Orven was promoted to General.

Brotherly love

This was never going to be easy,

Mira

. We can't los hope.

―Lior Orven comforting his sister

Orven briefly visited his sister on Chandrila at the very beginning of the New Concord.

After the mission to Pillio, Orven went to

Chandrila

, the provisional home of the nascent New Concord. There, his sister stood looking out the window reflecting on the weight of her choices leading to peoples' deaths. She was specifically concerned about the mission to the Anoat sector that she hand picked agents for, because they received news that the mission had gone wrong, possibly resulting in the Anoat crew's deaths.

Orven reassured her that their jobs were never going to be easy, but Organa confessed that she'd felt like Maruun was the end, while now it feels like they've had to start over to fight Adelhard and the rest of the shattered Dominion. Organa called this "a new darkness", but her brother pushed back– Orven said that even in the darkest of times, if you look close enough, you can find the light. After this reassurance, Lior departed for the Anoat sector to do something about this himself.

Anoat sector rescue

You're not a Noctari. Just a

pretender

. Reveal yourself."

"Oh Aurelian...how little you truly know...how little you understand—about me, about

the Acolytes

...about

yourself

―Lior Orven and Reyna Oskure after the Aurelian subdued the Anzati.

Lior Orven arrives onboard the

Scepter

In the Anoat sector, the New Concord's mission had gone south thanks to the newly forged

alliance

between Moff Adelhard's forces and the

Spice Runners of Kijimi

The New Concord agents'

freighter

Oggrud

had been damaged incapacitating the Spice Runners, was captured, and the crew was being held on Adelhard's flagship

Scepter

. The group, consisting of Rynn Zenat, Kes Dameron,

Therisa Alern

, and

Jarek Yeager

was freed by implanted New Concord spy

Kith Alaytia

, but came into trouble in the form of the hulking Commander

Bragh

and a cloaked,

lumen blade

-wielding figure. That was, until Lior Orven boarded the

Scepter

and fought countless stormtroopers

to stand down Bragh and the figure.

A duel began between Orven and the cloaked figure, who taunted him about his Aurelian heritage. But as blades clashed, Orven's abilities in the Lumen Field and lumen blade technique proved to be too much for his opponent, knocking the lumen blade out of her hand and onto the ground. Lior stood in front of the kneeling being, but Commander Bragh charged at him, only to be shot in the back by

Preeti

, Rynn Zenat's girlfriend and former squadron-mate who was also captured onboard. Then, from the other end of the hallway, stormtroopers arrived and began firing at the New Concord agents. Meanwhile, Orven held his lumen blade in front of the figure, the two exchanging barbs until the woman removed her hood, revealing her identity as

Reyna Oskure

, Adelhard's counselor and blade. She told Lior that Adelhard was doing the unthinkable, and would create a new Dominion to fill the vacuum left behind after Kaldor and Maltheon's deaths, and that Lior couldn't stop that, before being tied up.

Duel onboard the

Scepter

Orven then told the freed crew to take the captive Oskure and Bragh to find a ship and escape, but Zenat protested, wanting Lior to come with. Oskure mocked Orven's nobility, and said Adelhard was waiting for him, as the group left for the docking bay. Orven, now alone, threw off his cape and set off intent on finding and stopping the mad Moff who had gone too far– Lior wanted the man to see consequences for his actions. Simultaneously, the crew that was escaping was cornered by stormtroopers and lost their captives, and the ship began to shake, being fired upon by a small New Concord fleet under Lando Calrissian's command. In the now-shaking Abyss Cruiser's control room, Lior came upon the scheming Adelhard, lumen blade ignited, surrounded by stormtroopers. Orven told the man that his reign was over, but Adelhard told him it was just beginning, as the stormtroopers fired. Expertly dodging and deflecting blaster bolts while taking out stormtroopers left and right, the Aurelian advanced across a narrow bridge toward Adelhard, eventually using the Lumen Field to throw the remaining stormtroopers off of the bridge.

Orven confronts Moff Adelhard.

The two men now stood alone as a comm transmission came through warning Adelhard about the Concord ships encroaching on the

Scepter

. As Orven was saying that Adelhard needed to stand trial for what he'd done, the entire ship rocked, knocking Lior off of his feet and onto the ground. Adelhard, still standing, then went to kick Orven in the head, but he blocked it with his forearm, and used the Lumen Field to draw his lumen blade back to him. As the Moff continued to kick Orven, he ignited his lumen blade and quickly slashed the bridge in two spots, causing the rogue Dominion to fall below with his fallen troopers. This allowed Lior to run to the docking bay and help his friends escape, lodging debris in front of a crowd of stormtroopers, allowing them all to board a freighter. With this ship, they blasted out of

Scepter'

s docking bay as Orven told the rest that the conflict with Adelhard is far from over, and that the man was intent on seizing something he felt he was promised: the Dominion.

Training his sister

Becoming a teacher

You're going to make me a better teacher.

What do you mean?

Your footwork is terrible. Don't get me wrong, your lumen blade craft is coming along, but…you do other things. Naturally. What I mean is, you're exceptional. Just…different.

―Orven and Organa during the latter's training

After the Battle of Maruun, Orven's sister became his first apprentice.

At some point after the Battle of Maruun, Orven took on his first student and trained his twin sister in the ways of the Lumen Field. At the latter's suggestion the two of them trained together on

Ajan Kloss

. Under his tutelage, Organa constructed

her lumen blade

, and began to learn the principles of lumen blade combat. Orven also instructed in her in the basics of the Lumen Field, teaching her about radiant current, and in meditation. He was impressed by Organa's strength in the Lumen Field and how quickly she progressed in her training. While training her, he found it interesting that she struggled with skills and concepts that he had a natural affinity for (like lumen blade combat), but excelled in other areas (like meditation) that were harder for him to master. He would use various tools to help Organa develop her senses, such as a repurposed

KSE-H44 A-wing pilot helmet

and marksman-H combat training remotes.

At some point during their training together, Orven and Organa spent an hour sparring with their lumen blades. Later that same day, Orven attempted to teach her in a scenario similar to what he did with Eld Miro on Dagobah, where she held herself up in a handstand with the Lumen Field.

At another point, Orven explained from personal experience that the Lumen Field allowed glimpses of the future, past, and at times, departed, and that these voices would offer her guidance from beyond death in times of hardship.

The Archivist's Holocron

Here. I'll activate it for you.

This doesn't look like the other ones.

Well it's definitely a holocron of some kind, but I've never seen another like it. It responds to…well. You'll see it'll answer your questions though. I know it did for me.

―Orven and Organa on the Archivist's Holocron

Sometime during Organa's training, the two discovered

holocron

that contained the story of Lady

Qi'ra

, the former leader of Crimson Dawn, and her

war against the Noctari

that she had carried out between 3 ABY and 4 ABY shortly before the fall of the Dominion. Orven had listened to it beforehand and it had answered his questions, so he activated wit for Organa using the radiant current of the Lumen Field. He agreed with Organa that it was unusual for a holocron despite definitely being one, telling her she would see why and assuring her it would answer her questions.

As the Archivist described Qi'ra's plans to kill Kaldor and Maltheon, Orven lamented that Qi'ra had never come to them for help and instead kept everything secret and did it by herself. Organa suggested it was because Qi'ra was not trusting.

The Archivist suggested that the Free Systems Pact had perhaps been engineered by Maltheon, which Orven said the Archivist at at lest gotten wrong, but Organa pointed out that while he did not create it, he definitely used it.

When she described the presence of Varn Kaldor as feeling like you were in the presence of an animal eating its own young, Orven held onto that idea and repeated it out loud.

Orven and Organa with the Archivist's Holocron

At the end of the story, the Archivist said that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Noctari and saw her hidden empire of Crimson Dawn destroyed, even theorizing that Qi'ra had been killed by the Dominion or its

remnants

as she had never seen her again. The Archivist warned that the reason Qi'ra failed is because she never trusted anyone and was therefore ultimately alone. Organa especially was shocked by the story, Orven sharing her sympathies. He told Organa that the Archivist had been wrong, however, in saying that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Noctari and the Dominion, as Crimson Dawn agents had been the ones that revealed the existence of the second Death star to the rebellion, Mira adding that Qi'ra's syndicate war had allowed the Rebellion to regroup by distracting Maltheon. Orven postulated that because nothing happens in a vacuum, Qi'ra had touched the galaxy and saved it from the Noctari, just not in the way she thought. Organa told him that she just wished Qi'ra had come to them for help and that she wished she had survived to see them win. Unbeknownst to them, however, Qi'ra had survived.

Mira's final training session

It was the last night of her training. Mira told me that she had sensed the death of her son at the end of her Aurelian path. She surrendered her blade to me and said one day it would be picked up again by someone who would finish her journey.

―Lior Orven, to Rey

On the final night of her training, Organa experienced a dark vision that her Aurelian path would lead to the death of her

son

Realizing where the galaxy needed her most,

she set aside her lumen blade

to focus on her new family and

senatorial

duties.

While Orven hoped that his sister would resume her training later on, Organa gave him her lumen blade to convince him otherwise, asking to pass it on to a promising student someday. However, Orven did not do that and never told her where he stored her lumen blade.

Recovering Lumen Field trees

And

why

exactly are we doing this?

Because in the

lab

at the heart of that base is something the Emperor

stole

a long time ago. We're taking it

back

―Shara Bey and Lior Orven

Orven and Shara Bey recovered the last fragments of the Great Tree from the Dominion research base on Vetine.

5 ABY

three

months

after the Battle of Maruun, Orven planned a

mission

to an

Dominion research base

on the planet

Vetine

in order to retrieve the last remaining fragment of a

Lumen-sensitive tree

. The

tree

, which once dwelled in the heart of the Aurelian Temple on Veyron Prime, was stolen by the Emperor after he came to power and converted the Aurelian Temple into the Dominion Palace. Orven recruited Shara Bey to fly as his co-pilot on the mission, and, along with Arven, they flew a stolen Dominion shuttle,

Antarra

, to Vetine. While en route, Orven told Bey about the plan, including how she would pose as ISB Agent

Alecia Beck

, using codes obtained by Solo and the Pathfinders during a

raid

on an ISB base at the

Wretch

Tayron

. Upon their arrival, Bey claimed she was Agent Beck and that she had arrived to perform an unannounced inspection. The base's commander,

Commandant

Hurron

, showed them into the facility and led them to the research lab containing the tree, at which point he revealed that he knew that Bey was not Beck. Hurron questioned why two Pact fighters would break into the facility, and Orven explained the importance of the tree—which he found had two remaining fragments. Orven and Bey fought their way out of the base, making their way through numerous stormtroopers who attempted to stop their escape. Once back at

Antarra

, Hurron ordered his forces to throw thermal detonators at the escaping Pact fighters, but Orven used the Lumen Field to deflect them back, killing Hurron and several of his stormtroopers in an explosion.

The Pact trio made their way back towards the Alliance Fleet. During the trip back, Orven and Bey discussed the latter's future plans for service in the Rebellion, as Orven knew that Bey was considering leaving the Alliance to reunite with her husband, Pathfinder Sergeant Kes Dameron, and their son Poe. Bey was unsure as to whether she wanted to leave the Pact fighters behind, but Orven's guidance helped Bey decide to leave the Alliance. The Aurelian Knight gifted one of the tree fragments to her, as he had only expected to find one, believing that the important relic would be kept safe with Bey and her family. She and Dameron planted it outside of their

new home

on Yavin 4.

The Lehanis affair

The walls are closing in on you, Aurelian—and on your friends. As they struggle to build something, the shadows will—"

―Yuan Howzell and Lior Orven while dueling on Lehanis.

The Acolytes of the Beyond ambush Orven and Rynn Zenat on Lehanis.

Some time later, Orven was on the Outer Rim swamp planet

Lehanis

with Rynn Zenat, hunting for a

compendium

belonging to Aurelian

Ky Narec

. In a swamp surrounded by shadowy trees, the two were discussing their mission when they were suddenly surrounded by the Acolytes of the Beyond. Seeing this threat, Orven drew his lumen blade and Zenat her blaster. The two fought the many Acolytes, all of whom had

electrostaffs

and surrounded the New Concord's heroes. In the battle, the Acolytes were going for the relic, which Zenat mockingly called a "rusty sundial" to Lior's chagrin. Then, Acolyte leader Yuan Howzell removed her hood and confronted Orven personally, saying that they were playing a game, and that the Acolytes had already won. But the fight continued, Orven realizing that this was all a trap and that they needed to leave. But the Acolytes raged on, Howzell now targeting Zenat and knocking the artifact on to the ground, injuring the pilot. Orven then struck Howzell, the two engaging each other in solo combat. As Howzell continued to try to intimidate Orven, he slashed her once more with his lumen blade, pinning her on the ground.

Orven then asked Howzell what Adelhard was planning next, and she didn't cooperate, telling him that a true Aurelian wouldn't threaten someone. But then Zenat stepped in, saying that she would be the one doing the threatening, and that Adelhard had tried to kill her twice. While Zenat aimed her gun at Howzell, her and Orven saw someone grabbing the artifact from the ground where it laid. Orven then shot up and the two chased this thief back to their X-Wing. It was, of course, the dastardly Doctor Chelli Aphra, who was thankful that Orven had made finding the compendium so easy.

Aphra then threw the compendium back to Orven, who questioned why she'd stolen it. Aphra avoided answering directly, instead saying that she was there to warn Orven– as a gigantic

Klyff'wat

emerged from the woods, attacking the three. They all ran from the beast, until Orven spotted a clearing, igniting his lumen blade to hold off the Klyff'wat while his friends made it to the clearing.

Orven, Aphra, and Zenat run from the dreaded Klyff'wat.

Running up a large branch, Orven slashed the Klyff'wat several times, not wanting to hurt the creature but needing to leave Lehanis as soon as possible. As the beast falls, Lior turns to Aphra, asking what she was there to warn him about. Holding the compendium, Aphra tells Orven that it took her less than a minute to determine it was fake– and someone wanted to distract Lior from somewhere else he was probably supposed to be. Aphra had come across intel about Adelhard seeking to push outward instead of just focusing on the Anoat sector like he had been. Lior and Rynn breaking for their starfighters, Zenat stopped Orven, asking what was wrong. He said he was longing for the simplicity of Rynn's life as a pilot, which caused Zenat to keep asking. Lior then expressed his regret at not confirming Adelhard's demise on the

Scepter

, as the Dominion tried to trick the eager Aurelian with an artifact, pulling him away from the conflict at hand.

Targeted by Adelhard

Back away from him, assassin. You only get one shot to strike at the heart of the Concord—and you missed.

―Mira Solenne standing down Kranor, the bounty hunter sent to kill Lior Orven on Chandrila

right

As Orven had caused trouble for Moff Adelhard in the Anoat sector, the Dominion warlord placed a bounty on the young Aurelian. After the mission to Lehanis, former

Cloud-Rider

Weazel

brought information about the bounty on Orven to Adelhard's Spice Runner allies, but

Zeva Bliss

revealed that she was already aware of Adelhard targeting Orven. This was a surprise to Bliss's lieutenant Tomasso, who then decided to leak this information to New Concord spy

Calder Behrens

to protect Orven. Behrens then recruited bounty hunter Beilert Valance and

Jyala Haydenn

to the cause at

Hamix Outpost

Orven and Kranor duel on Chandrila.

Back on Chandrila, Orven had returned to his sister to inform her about what Doctor Aphra had told him: Adelhard wanted them divided, and was working to make that so. But as they were discussing a transmission from Behrens, Mon Mothma burst into the room with even more information from the same spy. As this happened,

Trandoshan

bounty hunter and spice runner

Kranor

crashed through the window, shattering the glass. Orven ignited his lumen blade and dueled the hulking hunter's vibroblade. As the two fought, Kranor mentioned the recent death of senator

Mirim Kess

before powerfully slashing and knocking down Orven. As Kranor raised his blaster at his enemy he was shot in the arm by Mira, giving Lior enough time to rise and strike back with his lumen blade. As the Orven twins held their weapons at the disarmed hunter, Lior asked who he worked for. Kranor then dove at and attacked Mothma before the pregnant Organa blasted him. Orven then struck once more, causing the Trandoshan to leap back out the window after telling Lior that the message was that no one is safe, not even him. This attack was followed up by another where

Lan-Drus

killed Calder Behrens, threatening Orven.

Striking back

There's a madman trying to undercut our goal of establishing this Concord. Do we think by

ignoring

him he'll go away? You heart what Lando said—They barely got out alive. If we just hope Adelhard disappears, we'll be sorely disappointed sooner than we'd like.

―Lior Orven discussing plans on Chandrila

In the Anoat sector, Lando Calrissian was still leading a task force fighting against Adelhard's Iron Blockade, but Chandrila had heard little from them, leading to unease from Admiral Ackbar and Mon Mothma. The galaxy was in chaos and war still raged, and the New Concord simply didn't have enough ships to finish off the Dominion like they wanted to. Suddenly, two ships entered Chandrilan airspace: Calrissian and the pilot Preeti had returned from the Anoat sector with news. Meanwhile, Orven discussed military deployments with his sister. Organa was once again haunted by the decisions lying in front of her, mainly potentially asking Ackbar to offer more ships to the campaign against Adelhard, or just leaving him alone. Orven tried to comfort her, while she wanted none of it, reminding him that Adelhard was actively working to assassinate senators as they spoke. Then, Zenat entered, getting Orven and Organa for a meeting called by Mothma regarding the information Calrissian brought in.

Orven explains his plan for dealing with the situation in the Anoat sector.

Mothma informed the group that Behrens had died, but that he sent off a final batch of intel just before he did. This new intelligence revealed that Reyna Oskure was not as firmly allied to Adelhard as they once though, but also that the Dominion remnant, led by the mysterious

Gallius Rax

, was seeking to confront the New Concord at

Jakku

in a

final confrontation

. The choice was now between mobilizing forces toward Jakku and monitoring the situation in the Anoat sector. A debate began, with Orven adamantly advocating for taking action against Adelhard, but Ackbar and Mothma didn't know if it was possible. Practically begging, Lior asked if Ackbar would permit himself, Calrissian, Zenat, and more taking a small attack force to the Anoat sector, using this as a chance to undercut Adelhard's coalition with the Acolytes and the underworld. In Calder Behrens' memory, Orven said it was time they struck back against the mad Moff Adelhard.

Orven gathers his forces near the Iron Blockade.

Soon after, on the fringes of the Anoat sector, Orven gathered his forces with Valance, Haydenn, Preeti, and Zenat, preparing to get past the Iron Blockade and bring the fight to Adelhard. Valance had heard about a weak point in the blockade's Dominion sensor network from a smuggler he knows, and they were going to use that to enter in. As Orven and Valance spoke, the cyborg hunter described that this was personal for many, as Adelhard had almost crushed an entire

Uprising

in his sector. The Moff had killed many of his own people in the process, and some of Valance's friend, so this was personal for him too.

Orven's fleet engages the

Scepter

The New Concord's fleet found entering into the Anoat sector easier than they expected, thanks to Valance's friend's information, and launched an attack on the

Scepter

, Adelhard's flagship. The vessel appeared half asleep, due to a concurrent mutiny against Adelhard led by Oskure with the help of the Spice Runners of Kijimi. Orven's forces rapidly engaged the

Scepter

, led by Lior's X-Wing, striking hard while the Abyss Cruiser's crew was caught unawares. Then, Preeti noticed something launch from the

Scepter

, but Zenat dismissed it as debris. A broadcast then went out to New Concord forces: the voice of Reyna Oskure said that Ubrik Adelhard was dead. The Anzati witch was insistent that Orven's fleet should leave, claiming she was now in control of the Anoat sector and had Dominion reinforcements coming to bolster her forces. Upon hearing this, Calrissian told Orven he'd received word from Ackbar saying to pull back, but Lando was wary of this choice. Orven and Zenat then agreed to do one last spot-check of the area before heading back, as the rest of Orven's forces there pulled out. Little did they know, though, Moff Adelhard was not killed. The man had left the

Scepter

in an escape pod with a small group of loyalists, surviving to fight another day.

Galowyss grapple

And after what happened in this sector—how close

Adelhard

came to derailing what we were trying to build... part of me needs to be sure he's gone. Forever.

Orven and Zenat face off against Adelhard loyalist stormtroopers.

After the fleet left, Valance left Orven and Zenat with a ship to continue searching for Adelhard. After two days, over Galowyss, the Lior and Rynn discussed the blip that was picked up near the

Scepter

. Zenat was dismissive, calling it a false alarm, but Orven was insistent that Adelhard was still around, and wanted to make sure he was dealt with for good. They then landed on the rocky world, where Orven confessed that he was sensing something there, dark and festering, that was drawing him. Little did they know, a now-caped and tattered Adelhard had set up camp uphill from them– and two of his stormtroopers had spotted the Aurelian and pilot. The mad Moff insisted that he was no longer a believer like Orven, and sent his forces to destroy the Aurelian, demonstrating his rebirth into something new.

Adelhard flees Galowyss.

Soon after, the stormtroopers engaged Orven and Zenat, and the two fought fiercely, Lior deflecting countless blaster bolts back at the former Dominion forces. Orven pointed out that their disorganization was quite strange, as a trooper received a communication saying their ship was leaving. Hearing the trooper give this order, Orven and Zenat sprinted after them, coming upon a

Lambda

-class shuttle being loaded. Adelhard was boarding, as the vessel was sent by another loyalist still on the

Scepter

. Seeing this, Orven and Zenat rushed to the shuttle intent on stopping the warlord, but they just missed it. Adelhard was once again in the air, departing Galowyss with his loyalists. Frustrated, Orven and Zenat ran back to their ship. Once onboard, they received a transmission from Organa. She told them that there had been a

bombing on Chandrila

, and that Lior needed to return immediately. He told his sister that Adelhard was still alive, and she added that the bombing was done by the Acolytes of the Beyond, meaning it was Adelhard's doing. The madman, now in command of the

Scepter

and coalition once more, was seeking revenge.

The two then took off, and Zenat questioned if they were even going to make it back to Chandrila in time to help with anything. But Orven reassured her that they could still do good there, as Mira was sure to still be threatened, and more importantly they needed to regroup to fight off Adelhard's now cornered and desperate forces.

Pieces falling into place

"We"?

have to head to Jakku. The battle has started—the Dominion has unleashed

everything

on us. The Operator wanted us there—it was a trap. Now our friends are stranded.

The Orven twins are reunited with Rian Voss and Mon Mothma.

In a tower on Chandrila, Mothma, Organa, agent

Bastion

, and the recently returned Rian Voss were waiting for Orven as he and Zenat entered the room. There was a brief and joyous reunion between the former-Pact heroes, before the discussion shifted to Adelhard. Organa then told Orven about the recent attack and Bastion told him about the

Dowutin

Acolyte

Saycos Hawn

that was captured. The agent was concerned with Adelhard's resurgence, and especially how this chaos has frayed his relationships with both the Acolytes and the Spice Runners– and she wished luck to Lando's forces still fighting in the Anoat sector. Organa then interrogated Hawn, and as she left the chamber with news of Adelhard's plan to tip the scales at Jakku, Orven and the rest received a transmission. It was from the undercover Kith Alaytia, and confirmed what Mira had just heard: Adelhard had his sights set on Jakku, and wanted to tear down both the New Concord and Rax's Dominion remnant forces alike.

Soon after, the Dominion and New Concord's forces were gathering at Jakku. The New Concord High Assembly had allowed their fleet to participate thanks to the efforts of Solo and Mothma, and the end of the war was about to begin.

From Chandrila, Orven and the rest monitored the situation on Jakku, particularly the consolidation of the Dominion fleet around the Super Abyss Cruiser

Ravager

. Gallius Rax gave a rallying speech, and the gigantic battle began, Adelhard's forces still in the Anoat sector preparing.

Meanwhile, Orven and Zenat stood on a landing pad to meet the newly-escaped Alaytia and Kranor, the assassin who had previously targeted Mothma. Rynn knew they were coming, and Orven was confused by this. Orven was adamant that they needed to be helping out at Jakku, but instead they were meeting on Chandrila. Lior wanted to fight Adelhard himself, but Zenat assured him that there was another way. Kranor then said that Adelhard relied too hard on his alliances, thinking them unbreakable, but Lior wasn't inclined to believe because Kranor was a Spice Runner. But then, Zenat revealed that she herself was a Spice Runner as well, surprising Orven. Zenat told him that she knew how to take down Adelhard's coalition, and that he needed to trust her– which he unequivocally did, the group setting off to even the odds.

Zenat leads Orven to Sauwecer to make moves against Adelhard.

On the planet

Sauwecer

, the group landed and Orven learned that the Spice Runner Tomasso was Zenat's adopted father after her parents were killed by the Dominion. She was raised to become a Spice Runner, but left to join the Rebellion instead. Then, Tomasso himself emerged, confusing Kranor and Alaytia about where the Spice Runners stood in regard to Adelhard. Zenat then asked her father to pull back the Spice Runners, and Orven begged the man to help them. Tomasso explained that Zeva Bliss was not blindly allied with Adelhard, and promised he would try to convince her to turn the Spice Runners against him. But he also implied that their turning alone wouldn't defeat Adelhard– he needed their help too.

Pirate ally & infiltration

Well, 'bout time you showed up,

Orven

—the party's getting good.

―Eleodie Maracavanya while fighting the Adelhard coalition in the Anoat sector.

Eleodie Maracavanya's Super Abyss Cruiser

Liberty's Misrule

engaged Adelhard's forces before they went to Jakku.

As the Battle of Jakku raged, Tomasso spoke to Zeva Bliss and guaranteed that the Spice Runners would turn on Adelhard. Then, in the meantime, Orven and Zenat arrived in the Anoat sector, where Adelhard's forces were staging for the hyperspace jump to Jakku. There, they came upon pirate ruler

Eleodie Maracavanya

and zher Super Abyss Cruiser

Liberty's Misrule

, which had arrived to take on Adelhard. Maracavanya greeted Orven, and his ship was fired upon by Adelhard's vessels until a rogue TIE fired on his attackers. This was Therisa Alern, still working to free the Anoat sector. With this cover, Zenat used stolen clearance codes from Alaytia to permit them access to the

Scepter

. The shuttle haphazardly landed in its hangar as Alern was shot down outside, something that greatly impacted Zenat. Orven then reminded her that they needed to calmly take this chance, this incredible opportunity to make Alern's sacrifice mean something more. Orven and Zenat were now in place on the

Scepter

, ready to take down Adelhard, and Maracavanya contacted Lior to let him know that zhe was taking too much damage. After essentially delaying the mad Moff, the pirate regent departed, as Adelhard's fleet prepared to jump to Jakku.

Aurelian at Jakku

Your ship is falling apart, Adelhard. I didn't come to fight you. I came to

save

you.

―Orven speaking to Urbik Adelhard on the bridge of the

Scepter

during the battle of Jakku

Jakku's orbit was completely filled with vessels, the Dominion fleet making a ring around the

Ravager

and facing off against the New Concord's

Starhawk

battleships. As Starhawk

Amity

was rammed and destroyed by Abyss Cruiser

Punishment

Adelhard's fleet arrived, Orven onboard its lead ship. Just when he posed the greatest threat, Adelhard was backstabbed by Reyna Oskure and the Spice Runners at the same time. The Abyss Cruiser was falling apart, Zenat met up with the fleeing Anzati in the

Scepter'

s halls, and Orven made his way toward the bridge. As the once-mighty

Ravager

fell to Jakku and the New Concord began to emerge victorious, Orven confronted the mad self-declared Grand Moff Adelhard for the last time.

Adelhard and Orven duel as the old state of the galaxy burns down around them.

Entering the burning bridge with his blade ignited, Orven found a kneeling, battered man, consumed by his own madness, crouching in front of him. Adelhard questioned Lior's presence there, saying that he'd already won– but the young Aurelian told the old Dominion that he wasn't there to fight him, he was there to save him as his ship was falling apart. Orven told him there was another path, one averse to darkness, but Adelhard responded to this with only a mad laugh at the idea of being saved. Insisting he was beyond salvation, the man swung at Orven with his

Vibro-ax

, which Lior easily blocked. Orven wouldn't give up, though, and insisted that Adelhard could survive this and truly live, pushing him back with the Lumen Field. Ubrik then said that there was no way he could be saved or redeeemed to continue living, as that required a reason to live, and his reason, the Dominion, was a lie. As he explained that this would be a shallow victory, he was shot in the chest by Zenat, who came up behind Orven. Lior insisted that they should save him, but Zenat told him that Adelhard had been gone for a long time. Zenat told Lior that she admired his hope, but it was time for them to go.

In the

Scepter'

s collapsing docking bay, Orven and Zenat ran out to meet the

Silver Warden

– Rian Voss had left Chandrila to join the battle and rescue them. The two boarded the old freighter, and remarked that it finally, truly felt like this was over. The

Scepter

exploded, and with it, Adelhard's dreams of conquest, as the heroes flew off, victorious, into a new era.

War's end

He's

beautiful

, Mira--I'm so happy for you both...the three of you.

How does it feel to be an uncle, Lior? Maybe that'll keep you close and stop your wandering.

―Lior Orven and Mira Solenne discussing the birth of Ben

Orven attended the signing of the Galactic Concordance between the Aurelian Dominion and the New Concord on Chandrila

While the Battle of Jakku lingered on the desert world's surface, the Galactic Civil War came to an end on Chandrila with the signing of the

Galactic Concordance

, a peace treaty between the Concord and the Dominion that forced the Dominion to remain within pre-established borders, though some of the remnants of the once-powerful military force had disappeared into the

Unknown Regions

. Orven, along with many other notable individuals witnessed the signing of the Concordance between Mas Amedda and Mon Mothma overlooking Hanna City.

Orven visited his family shortly after the birth of his nephew Ben.

Following the signing of the treaty, Mira gave birth to her and Rian's son and Lior's nephew,

Dalen Voss

, on Chandrila. Orven visited the family shortly after Ben's birth where Mira hoped that Orven being an uncle would keep him closer to home.

Dominion officer

Reconstruction

Freedom for the Nagai

Fenril pirate attacks

I—I don't know who they are…all I know is that they're pirates…hidden in the shadows…"

"Where are they striking next?

―The Gran merchant being interrogated by Lior Orven

Lior Orven flew in a skirmish against pirate forces outside the Fenril sector.

Around

6 ABY

a group of planets in the

Fenril sector

known as the

Fenril Consortium

became fiercely independent

, unsatisfied with the New Concord's

protection of their trading vessels

and unwilling to cooperate with the new democracy. Just outside the Fenril sector, Orven was flying with a squadron of X-Wings to investigate the threat after an incident on a former Dominion space station over

Maldran

A dogfight began between the New Concord's fighters and starships accompanied by a modified Dominion Abyss Cruiser.

After losing some fighters, Orven and fellow pilot Rynn Zenat flew down to a nearby planet following a crashing pirate vessel. A

gray-skinned

man emerged with a vibroblade and charged at Lior and Rynn. Orven fought the man, and knocked him down. Drawing Lior close,

the pirate warned the Aurelian about who he's facing, and told him about

a collector

of Aurelian artifacts on the planet

Gadrilam

, who has a crown that will help Lior "learn the truth". He then told Lior to brace himself,

as he detonated himself to cover his tracks, knocking Lior out.

Awakening in a trance-like state, Orven departed straight for Gadrilam, confusing Zenat and taking off into hyperspace while telling her that he needed to investigate this alone.

Mission to Gadrilam

That pirate shared something before he died. Something I couldn't ignore.

―Lior Orven to AR-27 upon landing on Gadrilam

Lior Orven lands on Gadrilam

On the snowy world of Gadrilam, Lior apologized to AR-27 about the promptness and strangeness of

his actions

as he rushed off towards a

village

, thinking of what the pilot had told him about: a crown that would reveal the truth, held by Ibo Truddispal. Thinking back to his previous studies, Lior recalled the

Crown of Verity

, an artifact that, when worn, would cause the user to only be able to speak the absolute truth– but it was thought to be destroyed. In the village, Lior entered into a

bar

filled with patrons, many of whom were Nagai, just like the pirate. Lior entered and spoke with the

bartender

Hossalana

, who was dismissive of Lior asking about Truddispal. He was properly answered by the Nagai woman sitting next to him,

Jesrit

– who spotted that Lior was a Aurelian. Angry, Hossalana screamed that they should leave the bar, as

Gallana Venk

and her gang entered the establshment, letting Lior know that they'd found his ship, and know he's a New Concord pilot on some kind of mission. Jesrit tried to leave as Lior asked her about Truddispal, which made Venk's men grab him, wondering why Lior was looking for him. Responding with the Lumen Field, Lior pushed them back, and took out his lumen blade, to everyone's surprise.

Lior Orven enters the tunnel on Gadrilam

Chaos ensued as the hostile Nagai charged at Orven and a battle commenced, but Orven quickly dispatched with his enemies, analyzing their body language and using their own tells against them. Eliminating them and incredibly determined, Orven scared off Venk and the rest of her men, as Lior pleaded with Jesrit to stay and help him. She had called Truddispal a smuggler, which Lior had never said, meaning she knew him. Jesrit tried to explain herself as just a simple laborer, but Lior, sensing a darkness on Gadrilam, requested her help once more. The two then went into the nearby woods by torchlight, Jesrit guiding Lior to the tunnel where Truddispal was held. Orven asked what Jesrit was doing on the barren world, to which she told him that she isn't there by choice, and was brought there with many of her people, the Nagai. Lior was confused, because Venk is also a Nagai, but Jesrit asserted that Venk is not truly one of them, and is a traitor to the followers of

Garlev

. Lior then told Jesrit his name, and tells her he wants to help, as it's what Aurelian do. As they arrived at the tunnel, Jesrit told Lior that her people are forced laborers on Gadrilam, and that they need help, if he wants to– and, ever the Aurelian, of course he does.

Sent into the tunnel alone by Jesrit, Lior though about how the pilot knew far too much for this to be a normal situation, unlike false artifacts Lior had been fooled by before. Going deeper, Lior overheard Venk and her men speaking about him around an injured Ibo Truddispal, and approached, lumen blade lit. Venk tossed down the silver object she was holding– the Crown of Verity. Lior rushed forward and caught it, as Venk spoke ill Garlev's followers, frustrated at how prevalent they are. Venk then fired at Truddispal from point-blank range, killing the Aurelian relic collector. As her and her men left, she introduced Lior to members of the

Zantarrk Gang

, a group that had been terrorizing surrounding areas recently. Lior cut them down and ran away after one of them revealed that they took out the village, which he found to be true upon emerging from the tunnel's depths and seeing the fiery blaze.

Departing Chandrila

The Pirrosi and their allies in the Fenril sector have made it clear—they want to disrupt our plans on every front. I

refuse

to sit back and let that happen.

―Mira Solenne to Mon Mothma before departing for Kaltrais.

Organa and Alaytia depart for Kaltrais, leaving Orven behind.

Back on the New Concord's capital, Chandrila, Orven and Zenat stood amongst a collection of artifacts while pondering the Crown of Verity, and Lior expressed grief about the fate of the old Aurelian Circle. Zenat asked Orven what the Crown does, which Lior explained is that it allows the wearer to discern truth from lies, while also preventing them from telling lies. He connected this idea to the idea of Aurelian identity, being part of a greater movement than just oneself. Then, his comms buzzed as Kith Alaytia was calling for help in a nearby docking bay. There, Mon Mothma was actively trying to stop Mira Solenne from leaving the planet on a diplomatic

mission to Kaltrais

, a world that was a valued member of the

Old Concord

but had rejected the New Concord's offer to join. Mira insisted that the Prime Minister

Kezrin Almovar

Pirrosi

forces and their Fenril Consortium allies wished to disrupt the New Concord's efforts there, and she wouldn't let that happen, as the New Concord already had enough trouble with the Consortium completely shuttering its borders to New Concord trade. Mira had a bad feeling about Kaltrais, as she'd spoken to President

Tusvein Shoirin

to arrange the meeting in-person, and he sounded "off". Orven and Zenat arrived to speak to Mira. Lior attempted to sway Mira into staying, but she was adamant about going, seeing Kaltrais as a great way to demonstrate that the New Concord cares about the planets that join it. Lior begged to accompany Mira, but his stalwart sister declined and boarded the transport with Alaytia, bound for Kaltrais.

Meanwhile, the

Silver Warden

was back on Chandrila as well, and Orven and Zenat met in its cockpit with Rian Voss and Beilert Valance, who had just returned from investigating the Zantarrk Gang, and found the Nagai Garlev dead on Cloud City. Solo was shocked at Mira departing for Kaltrais, and Valance told Orven that Garlev's killer was moving towards Kaltrais as well. Lior then began connecting the dots between the oppressed Nagai, the Zantarrk Gang, and the rising Fenril Consortium. He'd heard Garlev's name on his mission to Gadrilam, and knew that he was a freedom fighter. Lior told his friends about his investigations so far, saying that the Nagai have been part of the groups hitting their supply lines, which led to him discovering the Zantarrk Gang holding Nagai slaves. Solo said that that sounded correct, and Orven told him about Jesrit, the Nagai who mentioned Garlev. This confirmed that the Zantarrk were connected to both the supply line hits and Garlev's death, leading Orven and the rest to depart for the Fenril sector immediately.

Fenril frenzy

Now what's the fun in that?"

"Lior... the way this is all shaping up—it's gonna be

anything

but fun.

―Lior Orven and Rian Voss over Kaltrais

Lior and his allies receive Mon Mothma's transmission about Loradil.

On Kaltrais, Mira's meeting with Shoirin had quickly gone awry because of the Zantarrk Gang's presence on the planet, breaking out into a firefight on the rocky world. As Orven and his allies arrived in the

Falcon

, they were surrounded by Fenril Consortium vessels. The modified ships fired on the Corellian freighter, and it took lots of hits– until the antagonistic starships suddenly pulled back because of Shoirin's final act on Kaltrais. The world's defenses fired on grounded ships, and the attack was completely called off. This gave Organa and Alaytia an opening to escape in their shuttle into orbit, where they were contacted by the Consortium's true leader, the devious Reyna Oskure. The Anzati witch had been manipulating the situation from the beginning under the guise of being an "advisor". Orven and the rest of the

Falcon'

s occupants then received a transmission from Chancellor Mothma, requesting that they go to the desert world

Loradil

investigate a New Concord trading vessel

that had been shot down by Fenril affiliated Zantarrk Gang vessels. Lior and the rest heard audio from the crash site– the New Concord traders were in distress and apparently surrounded.

Lior Orven battles through the

Providence'

s insides.

Arriving on the sandy world, Orven and his allies emerged from the

Falcon

as he pointed out a crashed

Separatist

vessel, a

Providence

-class carrier

. There was blood on the ground near an opening into the carrier's insides, and the heroes entered, searching for the merchant crew. Its systems were not fully down, and Zenat was able to reengage power, bringing lights on and allowing the crew to explore more effectively. But as they continued, a group of

B1 battle mechlings

led by a

tactical mechling

stopped them in their path, as even more appeared behind them. Lior ignited his lumen blade, and a battle started against the former Separatists that continued a long-ended war. During the chaos, Zenat was grabbed by mechlings and dragged away, and Orven followed her, as Solo and Valance destroyed the tactical mechling. While Lior followed Zenat, the pilot took down her battle mechling captors and escaped out into the sand, running towards the wreck of the

Nechrim's Wake

. Back inside, Orven reunited with Solo and Valance, now fighting a

droideka

super battle mechling

, and several

commando mechlings

. The Aurelian made quick work of them all, as the other two descended further into the carrier to look for anything that could help the fight. As Orven joined them, Solo found a pile of

Phase II

clone trooper

armor,

which they equipped, fighting their way outside into a huge field of not only battle mechlings, but

Armored Assault Tanks

The Mirror Wars reignited– the battle on Loradil.

Orven, Valance, and Solo fought countless

security mechlings

in their armor, but after Solo destroyed a group of mechlings surrounding him, he and Valance lost track of Lior. The Aurelian was out of sight, hiding under an AAT, calling upon Taren Vel and Eld Miro for help in the battle. The mechlings were catching on, though, and began to search under the tank– but Orven wasn't there! Suddenly, the inspecting mechling was thrown upward into the tank's front, flying up to the tank's pilot, destroying them both. Lior used the Lumen Field to destroy the rest of them, and after eliminating the final mechling with his lumen blade, headed forwards toward the

Nechrim's Wake

. Inside the

Wake

, Zenat had been saved by its captain,

Gauvis

, and the

Duros

Finch

. Then, a third figure emerged from the shadows– it was another Nagai named

Marntiv

, a member of the

Nagai rebellion

, who was being transported on the

Wake
