. The man revealed that he was the reason the Zantarrk Gang attacked the convoy, the supplies gained just being a bonus. They aligned themselves with Reyna Oskure and the Fenril Consortium for wealth and power, and are trying to squash the Nagai rebellion while using Nagai slaves as troops. Trying to get to Rynn and the

Wake

, Lior lept into battle as his father did before him, destroying countless mechlings, while reminding himself that he must avoid the traps and temptations that got his father. As Solo ran towards the

Falcon

, he was knocked down by an onslaught of mechlings, and as they closed in on him, the entire crew, Orven, Zenat, Valance, Gauvis, Finch, and Marntiv, destroyed the mechlings and rescued him. Rian immediately suggested they head back to Chandrila, but Lior let him know that the Nagai were

rising up on Kaltrais

, a

New Concord agent

was present, and it was the Nagai's only chance at freedom. The group, having survived the Separatist onslaught, then left for Kaltrais.

Uprising at Kaltrais

...You're jere...you can help..."

"I'm a Aurelian, remember?

It's what we do, Jesrit.

―Jesrit and Lior Orven, after meeting again during the Nagai insurrection on Kaltrais

Reunited, Lior Orven and Jesrit fight the Zantarrk Gang.

Soon after, the

Silver Warden

arrived over Kaltrais, but didn't receive any communications from the surface– something was wrong. And as the freighter descended further, towards a central city, blaster fire could be seen from the sky. As the entire New Concord team exited the

Falcon

, Lior Orven ran forward,  shocked to see the Nagai he met on Gadrilam, Jesrit, alive and leading the insurrection in the plaza! Amidst the chaos, and a reunion between Jesrit and Marntiv, Lior called out to Jesrit, and the two fought the pirate troopers together. They took out several as Lior explained that he's a Aurelian, and that means coming back to help. He sadly informed her of Garlev's death, and the two kept fighting the oppressors, many of which were Nagai themselves, enslaved by the Zantarrk or promised reward. They were then interrupted by Solo and the rest, Rian informing Lior that Rynn Zenat was not doing okay, and was feeling weak. Meanwhile, New Concord agent Bastion was in place infiltrating the Zantarrk's base of operations, and entered Prime Minister Almovar's chamber, where the regal woman stood surrounded by holograms. Alarmed, Almovar immediately questioned who Bastion was, as Orven, Zenat, and Jesrit caught up and entered the room as well.

Finally speaking to someone responsible for the continued oppression of the Nagai, Orven tried to rationalize with the fierce leader, as he and Jesrit told her that her actions were putting countless people–including her own–in danger. Almovar said she was doing everything for her home, but Lior and Rynn reminded her that her will doesn't exactly line up with what the people of Pirros really want. Kaltrais is in shambles, the people of Pirros don't want war, and she's been manipulated by Reyna Oskure. Jesrit was furious at the Prime Minister, seeing Nagai blood on her hands as she'd worked with Gallana Venk and used Nagai slaves. A bit resigned, Almovar then admitted that war was already here, that the Fenril Consortium had permanently allied itself with Oskure and the Zantarrk, and that it was too late to stop it all. Bastion refuted this, and Lior extended a hand of grace to the misguided woman, but Almovar was still concerned about Nagai retribution. At this, Jesrit told her that the Nagai rebellion's two goals were the freedom of the homeworld

Nagi

, and the deaths of Venk and Oskure, as Lior rebuked her for this retributive anger and hate.

Orven, Zenat, Bastion, and Jesrit confront Prime Minister Kezrin Almovar on Kaltrais.

Lior then testified of his experience with the Zantarrk, as they'd built a slave army and killed many, and then spoke of Oskure's Dominion past. Accepting that she had indeed failed her people, Prime Minister Almovar then, finally, declared that she would be withdrawing her fleet, as the Nagai insurrection's explosions outside rocked the building. Almovar dismissed the group and said that she had much to repair and ponder, as Rynn promised that the New Concord would be there to help deal with it. As the group left, quietly, Almovar said her fate was sealed, as a pair of white eyes opened in the shadows behind her, Reyna Oskure killing the Pirrosi leader. Outsiude, while Zenat grew weaker, Lior Orven helped her as the group fought their way towards a major hangar. Lior tried asking what was wrong, but Rynn refused to answer. The Nagai were winning, fighting their way onto and stealing ships, as the Zantarrk fell one trooper after the last. Lior and Rynn held the line while more Nagai escaped, until the two were picked up by the

Falcon

, which departed into space towards Nagi to win this revolution once and for all.

Liberation of Nagi

We've got to locate

Venk

and

Oskure

If we take them down, the Fenril Consortium will be finished.

Easier said than done, kid.

―Lior Orven and Rian Voss on the surface of Nagi.

The New Concord's heroes split up on Nagi.

As the

Falcon

and its Nagai allies arrived over Nagi, they were confronted with a Fenril Abyss Cruiser and lots of Zantarrk Gang vessels. The Fenril fleet fired on the freighter and other rebel ships as Rian Voss performed a spin technique to batter away some fighters, while sending the

Falcon

down to the surface. The freighter crashed into a crowd of Zantarrk pirates, taking them out as the smoke rose from the vessel. The heroes emerged, and Rynn's condition was worsening while Orven helped her stand. But then a convoy of Nagai rebels on

landspeeders

and

speeder bikes

appeared, one member saying that it was prophesized that Jesrit would arrive. Jesrit set their goals straight and introduced her new allies to the recently arrived Nagai. The group then decided to split up, Lior, Rynn, Jesrit, and Marntiv going with the Nagai to find medical aid for Zenat, while Solo, Valance, Gauvis, Finch, and Bastion set out to find and eliminate Venk and Oskure as quickly as they could. Orven and his allies were on a Nagai convoy as Zenat's illness quickly became all-consuming, as if her life is being drained. Jesrit promised that they wouldn't let Rynn die, as a group of

Tof

emerged in front of them and blew up their transports. As Orven woke up, he saw that Marntiv was killed in the blast, and that two of the large men were taking Jesrit and Rynn with them. The Tof departed on speeder bikes while claiming the Nagai rebellion was destined for death– with Orven left for dead in the barren wastes of Nagi.

Wandering the wastes injured and reflecting on what and who he'd lost, Lior Orven fainted from exhaustion, but awoke to the Nagai

Tuula

, a friend of Jesrit's and the daughter of Garlev. Tuula says that if Oskure kills Jesrit, the hope of the Nagai rebellion dies with her. She helps Lior up, and the two set off to find their friends,

as Tuula used her tracking skills to guide the two towards Oskure. In the capital city of

Siva

, Oskure was escorting the captured Zenat and Jesrit to a shuttle, guarded by Zantarrk stormtroopers, while her fleet gathered in orbit. Suddenly, a New Concord fleet, led by Alaytia, consisting of a

Defender

-class cruiser

, at least two

escort frigates

, several corvettes, and many starfighters arrived to confront the Consortium. But as the Anzati witch boasted, Orven, Solo, Valance, and Tuula, reunited, fired on Oskure. A new battle had begun as the stormtroopers unleashed a volley of fire at the New Concord heroes, who had the high ground. Lior noticed Oskure draining more of Rynn's life force from above, and slashed stormtroopers with his lumen blade as he dove down to face the Anzati. She'd been waiting for this, and accepted the young Aurelian's challenge. The witch dove in with her claws out, slicing Lior and seeking to feast on his life force. She knocked his lumen blade out of his hand, and asked him if he'd made peace with his past and future. He then said that he'd made peace with who he was, and that his past is the Aurelian's past, and that was also his future. Using the Lumen Field, Lior held Oskure back, pushing her to the wall as he held her still and approached her.

Oskure and Orven finally face off.

Lior then put the Crown of Verity on Reyna's head, causing her to scream as she was now only able to speak the absolute truth. Just before she began to speak, Oskure secretly pressed a button on a remote behind her back, and told Lior that if he wanted the "truth", that she, a creature of lies and shadows, would reveal it. She confessed to forcing the Nagai peasants to haphazardly assemble a fleet made of Dominion scraps, but in a way that would specifically ensure victory for herself. To Lior's horror, Oskure then said that the truth would set her free, and prove to be his final resting place. Then, to the shock of Stannix in orbit, the fleet above Nagi suddenly detonated, causing massive explosions and scaring New Concord pilots. Nagi had now become a death trap.

New Concord pilots in orbit dodged and fought to eliminate debris from the detonated fleet, some of it managing to fall to the surface, threatening the Nagai. There, the standoff between Orven and Oskure ended with the Anzati throwing off the Crown of Verity as wreckage from the fleet rained and Orven used his lumen blade and the Lumen Field to redirect falling debris.

On the wastes of Nagi, the final battle for Nagi had begun as Oskure's forces, consisting of Tof soldiers, pirates, and Zantarrk stormtroopers faced off with Orven and his allies. The heroes fought fiercely, as Lior helped Rynn Zenat to limp away, her condition worsening even more. Orven wanted to help her, but she begged him to stop Oskure and end the fighting first. While Orven deflected a sudden barrage of blaster fire, Zenat crawled on the ground to grab the Crown of Verity as Oskure approached her ship to escape. Then, while the battle raged, a New Concord transport accompanied by two X-Wings landed– and Mon Mothma and Mira Solenne emerged to announce that the New Concord stood with the Nagai– the Pirrosi delegation had surrendered. The fighting ceased upon this declaration, the combatants looking at the arriving dignitaries. Jesrit took this opportunity to address the crowd– saying that they now stood on the precipice of true peace, a free Nagi, and freedom from mainpulators, monsters, and their lackeys. Mira denounced the Fenril Consortium as a shadow government run by Oskure, stated that the New Concord stands with Nagi as a free, independent world they will help rebuild, and the enemy Nagai removed their salvaged Dominion trooper helmets, cheering the phrase "in Garlev's name", freed. Close by, the heroes cornered the escaping Oskure, Orven telling her that she had much to answer for, attacking the vampiric witch. Oskure was able to knock Orven down while her guards were killed, but Zenat emerged from behind, placing the Crown of Verity this time on her own head.

Orven and his allies charge Reyna Oskure as she attempts to escape.

Rynn's eyes glowed, and she explained that the Crown allows the wearer to not only see the truth, but all that they've left behind. Grabbing Oskure, Zenat told the ancient Anzati that she'd taken something from her, a piece of her that's helped her destroy so many lives in the Fenril sector. Oskure mocked Zenat's

spice runner

background, but the pilot continued, telling Oskure that she'd left something behind inside of her, the sorceress's skin growing more and more decrepit as the rapidly aging woman begged to be left alone. This began to restore Zenat, who became somewhat mad with power, taunting the dying witch– to Orven's dismay. He begged her to stop, to not lash out for revenge, and it was Solo's hand on Zenat's shoulder that finally stopped her, as he reminded her that she had won, and shouldn't drag herself down with Oskure. The crumpled, old body of Reyna Oskure then fell to the ground, barely clinging to life, as Rynn removed the Crown.

Some time later, Rynn asked Lior if it was really over, to which the Aurelian said that it was, for now– but there was always a potential challenge waiting around the corner. In the city of Siva, the New Concord heroes celebrated and gathered with the Nagai, as they began clearing debris and rebuilding. Jesrit thanked Lior and his friends, and Lior clarified that he was just keeping his promise from Gadrilam. Zenat saw this moment of reconstruction, celebration, and all the rest of it, and reflected on the moment of happiness with Lior, who told her that these moments were incredibly important. The Aurelian said these moments were what keep one moving through the dark times, even when the fight feels endless: moments of peace and happiness like this give one life.

Foundations of an order

I had great hope for the future of the Aurelian. I came to believe that if a single Aurelian could help bring balance back to the Lumen Field, then perhaps a restored Aurelian Circle could maintain peace and prevent the umbral current from ever regaining its hold on the galaxy. So I scoured worlds for the remaining Aurelian texts and gathered a new class of apprentices under my tutelage.

―Lior Orven

Lior Orven visited many places in search of Aurelian lore, including Elphrona.

Just as he promised Farnay near the start of his own Aurelian studies,

in 5 ABY,

Orven set about rebuilding the Aurelian Circle. Despite his optimism, he had many unanswered questions

and was hesitant to take on another apprentice after his sister Mira, unsure of his grounding in Aurelian lore.

As he traveled the galaxy, he also searched for force-sensitives who could possibly become his students.

During this long and difficult task,

Orven was aided by

Lor San Tekka

, an explorer and member of the

Church of the Lumen Field

who, though not Lumen-sensitive himself, believed in the Aurelian ideals. San Tekka was old enough to remember the Aurelian Knights before the Mirror Wars, and he did not believe the Emperor's lies about how the Aurelian betrayed the Old Concord.

San Tekka, who believed that the return of the Aurelian Circle was essential to bring balance to the Lumen Field,

became a valuable ally for both Orven and Organa.

Gatalenta

was one of the first destinations in Orven's search,

and he also traveled to Moraband,

the ancient home of the Noctari,

to investigate anything he could about the Lumen Field.

He visited the

Deep Core

planet of

Tython

many times and studied its ancient

ruined temple

, impressed by the majesty of the site every time he visited.

He scoured many worlds and assembled a collection of eight ancient bound

books

and scrolls

sacred Aurelian texts

. One of the books was the

Rammahgon

, an influential work that was thought destroyed over 5,000 years ago but retrieved by Orven while he explored the subterranean ruins of

Ossus

. The

Aionomica

was another, a two-volume collection of Aurelian explorations and codifications of the Lumen Field, part codex, correspondence and scrapbook.

He also found a book that was a catalog of the

Eeshaypher Collection

, a forbidden gallery of artifacts related to the umbral current of the Lumen Field.

At one point, Orven journeyed to

Black Spire Outpost

Batuu

, searching for ancient Aurelian texts and artifacts.

Orven uncovered the

Aionomica

and other Aurelian relics on his travels.

Orven dedicated much of these years to finding the site of the

first Aurelian Temple

. He uncovered many tantalizing clues that hinted at the origins of the Aurelian, but the precise location was a mystery. One key to unravelling the mystery was his study of the spread of the Lumen Field-sensitive uneti saplings.

He also made use of the

Aurelian star compass

he had discovered in the Emperor's storehouse on Pillio. With a

plasma

-encased

supraluminite

lodestone

at its center, the compass functioned much like the ancient

wayfinders

and was attuned to specific points in hyperspace. Through studying the compass, Orven uncovered a path to Ahch-To the site of

Temple Island

and the first Aurelian Temple.

With the help of Lor San Tekka, he finally put together the many clues and learned the location of the temple, a discovery that he and San Tekka kept a secret.

With San Tekka, Orven hunted down relics the force across the stars. Following the Aurelian compass he recovered on Pillio, the pair mapped out the network of Aurelian temples scattered across the galaxy, many of which were forgotten after the end of the Aurelian Purge. Their voyages proved fruitful most of the time, Orven amassing a massive collection of tomes, texts, scrolls, data cards, ritual items, sigils, and other antique symbols. Besides the Aurelian star compass, Orven also acquired lumen blades. All of this, the pair of men researched, gaining even deeper understanding of the old Aurelian Circle and its teachings.

Orven discovered the lost cache of Aurelian artifacts belonging to Jocastu Nu.

Around

6 ABY

in his explorations of Aurelian lore, Orven discovered the

cache

of Aurelian

Chief Librarian

Jocasta Nu

He uncovered many lost Aurelian and Lumen Field relics throughout the galaxy, including a

Aurelian Crusader pendant

and a holocron belong to Aurelian Master

Avar Kriss

of the High Concord era.

Orven came to learn about some of the Aurelian Masters that existed before the fall of the Order, such as Nu,

Mace Windu

, and

Qui-Gon Jinn

. He became versed in the

Midi-chlorians

, the

Aurelian Code

, and the various Aurelian temples that existed, like the

one on Lothal

He learned of several Noctari Lords and umbral current warriors: Darth Bane, Darth Maul,

Darth Tyranus

, and the Aurelian hunters of the Aurelian Dominion's

Inquisitorius

among them. Along with

Fortress Kaldor

, his father's castle on Mustafar, he became familiar with several locations and temples strong with the Dark Side.

Another relic Orven recovered was a page of the

Journal of Caleb Dume

, a survivor of the Aurelian Purge and early rebel who fought the Dominion under a new identity, Kanan Jarrus, and eventuallly trained his own apprentice,

Ezra Bridger

He brought this and the other Aurelian artifacts he had uncovered to Ahch-To and compiled his knowledge into a book he called

The Secrets of the Aurelian

. Ahch-To was also where Orven collected the

Rammahgon

, the

Aionomica

, the

Chronicles of Brus-bu

Poetics of a Aurelian

, and the other sacred Aurelian texts into a small

library

housed in an

uneti tree

Rescue of Mogu

He is strong with the Lumen Field, but talent without training is nothing. I will give my life to protect the Child… but he will not be safe until he masters his abilities.

―Lior Orven, to Darin Korr

Lior Orven takes Mogu as his student.

In approximately

9 ABY

Lumen-sensitive

member of

Eld Miro's species

named

Mogu

reached out in the Lumen Field from the

seeing stone

on Tython. Mogu had been raised at the

Aurelian Temple

on Veyron Prime before the

Great Aurelian Purge

but had not yet been fully trained as a Aurelian,

and had since become a

foundling

cared for by the

Ironbound

bounty hunter

Darin Korr

, whom had been tasked with taking Mogu to someone who could train him in the use of the Lumen Field.

Shortly afterwards, Mogu was

taken

by an

Dominion remnant

Feeling a presence in the Lumen Field,

Orven tracked Mogu to a

light cruiser

that Djarin and his allies had boarded in

their own efforts

to rescue him. Orven boarded the vessel with AR-27 and defeated a

platoon

third-generation dark troopers

that had trapped Mogu and Djarin's team on the ship's bridge. Afterwards, Orven offered his hand to Mogu, and, noting the child's strong attachment to Djarin, told Djarin that he would take care of him if Djarin allowed it. Djarin relinquished Mogu to Orven to be trained in the ways of the Lumen Field and Orven left the ship with the child.

Building an academy

You've taught him well.

It's more like he's remembering than I'm actually teaching him anything.

Sometimes the student guides the master.

[...]"

Sometimes I wonder if his heart is in it [...] What should I do about him?

Trust your instincts.

―Lior Orven and Sera Toval on Mogu's training

Orven instructing Mogu on Ossus.

Fulfilling the dying wish of Eld Miro,

Orven set out to begin training a new generation of Aurelian. Having rescued and brought in an original Aurelian Padawan from the twilight years of the Solar Concord, Orven ventured to Ossus with the young Mogu to build the first

Aurelian Academy

after the Aurelian Circle's near extinction following Directive 71. Possessing the wisdom and teachings of his own masters, Orven tried to educate his young student through the ways of the Lumen Field. Telling tales of Master Eld Miro onto Mogu, the young Aurelian Master passed on what he had learned, echoing Eld Miro's words "size matters not" and to do, not try. However, Orven strived to make impression on his small student, having to stop him from using the Lumen Field to eat creatures and let Mogu use the Lumen Field as he did. Knowing that knowledge lied in Mogu's memory, Lior offered to help him remember and placed his hands gently on the alien's head. Seeing the traumatic images of the 501st Legion gunning down three Aurelian Knights protecting the Padawan, everything faded to black when the clones killed the Aurelian and moved towards Mogu. When his student awoke, Lior greeted his return and began to better understand the turmoil and subsequent reluctance of Mogu towards his lessons.

Orven speaks with Tano about Mogu, while watching him train on the riverbank.

While carrying Mogu on his back as he had done with Eld Miro on Dagobah, Orven felt the presence of the Ironbound, Darin Korr. After a time, Mogu seemed to master balance and studied in awe as Lior practiced his skills with the lumen blade. Eventually, he put the young Mogu's reflexes to test on a training remote, which the young Padawan managed to dodge and destroy with ease.

Sera Toval

approached Orven and complimented him on his progress with Mogu. The doubtful Aurelian expressed his concerns that Mogu's progress was due to him gradually remembering his training on Veyron Prime, not necessarily his training so far with him. Ahsoka stated that sometimes the student teaches the master. Orven noted Djarin's presence and Tano gave him a present meant for Mogu. Not sure whether Mogu had heart for the way of the Aurelian, Orven shared his thoughts. Fortunately, Tano knew the same mind of Orven's father, seeing that same compassionate heart which she felt Anakin had for her and mentioned Lior's alikeness. Orven asked what should be done and Tano told him to trust his instincts, wisdom she learned from her master. She then departed the planet and said the two would perhaps meet again and told the Lumen Field be with him.

Orven showing Mogu Master Eld Miro's old lumen blade

Heeding the advice of Tano, Orven decided to offer Mogu a final choice. In the stone hut, Orven presented Mogu with Darin Korr's gift as well as the lumen blade of Master Eld Miro, which Orven had kept. The first path was to accept the present from Djarin and be returned to him, however, by choosing attachment he would forsake the Aurelian way. The second was to pick up

Eld Miro's lumen blade

and pledge himself to Lior's teachings to become his first Aurelian Padawan. He told Mogu that to become a Aurelian would take years and hard work, though at the end he could be a great Aurelian. He then let Mogu choose.

Mogu would choose the armor, thus leading Orven to put him in his V-wing interceptor and instruct AR-27 to pilot him to Darsuun to be reunited with Djarin.

Despite their time together being brief, Orven's guidance had a significant impact on Mogu. After being asked about Mogu's impressive use of the Lumen Field by fellow Ironbound

Bo-Katan Kryze

, Djarin noted that his abilities were a result of Orven's training.

Some time after his meeting with Sera Toval, the

Nightsister

Morgan Elsbeth

recruited former Aurelian Knight of the old Aurelian Circle

Baylan Skoll

, who was aware of the truth behind Orven's father, to help locate Dominion

Grand Admiral

Thrawn

. As Orven had become prominent during the Galactic Civil War, Skoll became aware of his existence, and kept his name, alongside the names of other Pact heroes, listed in a device on his gauntlet during the search for the planet

Peridea

in a

distant galaxy

Recruiting his nephew

He's an amazing teacher. Very strong. I've learned so much from him…

―Dalen Voss, on his uncle

Orven and Solo together

Though reluctant to take on another apprentice at first, when the Lumen Field awakened in Orven's nephew Dalen Voss

and his mother Mira could see the umbral current taking hold of her son, she entrusted the boy to her brother to help him find balance.

He agreed to train the 10-year-old Solo,

15 ABY

Orven took on the responsibilities of a Aurelian Master by teaching his nephew. After Ben's fall to the umbral current however, Lior would say that he was merely playing the role of Aurelian Master as it was a title he had not formally earned.

Orven taught Solo the ways of the Aurelian, in its many forms, under the tutelage and impressed on his pupil the importance of practicing discipline and control. Solo gave his best efforts to gain his Master's approval, although at times their familial bond caused him to address Orven as uncle instead of Master.

Though he loved his nephew,

Orven had committed his life to reestablishing the Aurelian Circle's teachings, which had virtually nothing to do with family ties; therefore, he taught Solo that detachment and distance were necessary to gaining the pure focus that every Aurelian aspired to achieve.

His nephew soon joined in exploring the galaxy for ancient Aurelian lore,

and together they found holocrons from forgotten eras and ancient Aurelian texts with arcane writings.

The two shared more than the same Orven blood, for like him, Ben gained a strong devotion to the ways of the Aurelian Circle.

Orven saw his nephew's raw power and believed he could pass on his strengths to him,

and he attempted to manage his progress through the Lumen Field by having him exercise a level of restraint.

Solo was his strongest and most prized student,

and Orven went on to place his hope for the Aurelian Circle's future within his nephew,

and despite knowing of the darkness within his pupil, the Aurelian Master believed he was strong enough to keep it in check.

Orven was a kind master,

and Solo considered his uncle to be a strong and amazing teacher,

but Orven made mistakes that culminated over time, protecting Ben from the dark truths of their family, much like his sister did before him, and inadvertently isolated Ben over the years of his training.

At the request of his sister, Lior never provided his nephew with the details of Kael Orven's fall to the umbral current or Varn Kaldor's redemption and return to the light.

During his training of his nephew, a powerful umbral current-practitioner known as

Snoke

who was the

Supreme Leader

of a splinter group of the Dominion known as the

First Order

, and also secretly a puppet of the resurrected Emperor Maltheon,

began influencing Ben from afar, clouding him with the umbral current.

At some point Orven had encountered Snoke and supposedly damaged him,

and Snoke was responsible for indirectly inspiring Lior to rebuild the Aurelian Circle, having tried to shatter his resolve.

More apprentices

We all studied together. I was first, but they came not long after.

―Dalen Voss, on Lior's training other apprentices

Orven training a new generation of Aurelian

Around

16 ABY

Orven gathered disciples, young force-sensitive beings adrift with no way to harness their abilities as a result the Aurelian Purge,

who went on to be his first students.

Orven's new students numbered about a dozen besides his nephew,

including students

Voe

Tai

, and

Hennix

and he worked to teach them everything he had learned in his own training and about the radiant current of the Lumen Field per Eld Miro's command.

Orven hoped that teaching the students in a real Aurelian Temple would offer them a more traditional Aurelian path than his own training under Taren Vel and Eld Miro. He kept temple's location on Ossus a secret known only to the members of the Order and his loyal mechling AR-27.

Orven stored the recovered Aurelian knowledge and relics that he and Lor San Tekka had recovered within the Aurelian Temple,

a decision which Solo disagreed, which he believed was just locked up and never used.

With the small gathering of his new students looking to him as a spiritual guide and mentor for the Aurelian arts, Orven believed he could usher in a new era of enlightenment.

With his attention now split between several other apprentices, Orven's nephew Dalen Voss's focus was drawn away to dark Lumen Field knowledge.

Teaching several Aurelian techniques and traditions as telekinesis, meditation, and the use and construction of

lumen blades

Orven's approach for guiding the young minds under his care was steeped in his own master Eld Miro's teachings passed on while he was on Dagobah, so would often be preaching about patience and balance.

While also instilling the belief that all beings were equal within the Lumen Field,

he gave lectures outside his temple, passing on ever more experience from training with Eld Miro on Dagobah as he taught the levitation of rocks through the Lumen Field. During one such event, student Voe asked why Dalen Voss was stronger, where Orven compared the Lumen Field to a door, that it would become easier to use the more she opened herself up to it. The Aurelian Master also tutored Hennix in opening holocrons through the Lumen Field, reminding the young

Quarren

to listen to the unique voices of each holocron, for each one was different based on their teachings.

Orven desired heavily to preserve the teachings of the Aurelian Circle, laboring to pass on the best of what Eld Miro and Taren taught him as Aurelian mentors at his Aurelian temple and to instill the core beliefs they embodied within the next generation. Additionally, Lior understood what Eld Miro experienced with himself on Dagobah as teacher, having the same unique frustrations with his own pupils; a master guiding a young unseasoned student that was convinced they were wasting time on pointless exercises, such as stacking stones.

At some point during his time as a Aurelian Master, Orven would face a group of

IG-RM assassin mechlings

alongside his nephew.

Encountering the Knights of Ren

Well, hello there. Everything here is ours. We're going to take it…but

how

we take it is up to you. Your call, friends.

You're wrong. So, so wrong. This is a

Aurelian outpost

. Everything in it belongs to the Aurelian Circle, which, at the moment, means me. I am the last Aurelian.

―Ren and Orven

Orven, Solo, and San Tekka discover the Aurelian Outpost on Elphrona.

19 ABY

, the year his nephew turned fourteen,

Orven, Ben, and Lor San Tekka

traveled

to a

Aurelian outpost

Elphrona

, a location from the days of the High Concord Era, hoping to find Aurelian artifacts. Lior flew the ship and conversed with San Tekka, while Solo, without his master's knowledge, was communing with Snoke. Orven was initially skeptical of a Aurelian presence so far from the core, but San Tekka assured him that with his expertise he had recognized the description from his contact as a Aurelian Outpost, and explained that the Aurelian's activity was greatly expanded throughout the galaxy during the High Concord Era. Meanwhile, Solo telepathically communicated with Snoke, expressing that he was tired of missions to find artifacts with Orven that he would just lock away in the temple and never use. Solo admitted that Orven was an amazing teacher, but was frustrated that he never wanted Solo to use any of the things he had taught him, which Snoke claimed was because Orven viewed Solo as a child and had not realized his true capabilities. Orven then told Solo that they would possibly find insights into the old Aurelian, and added that they could find interesting weapons, as he knew that Solo was interested in such subjects, which San Tekka affirmed. Orven told Solo he was glad he came along, and Solo said he shared the sentiment.

When they landed, Orven was impressed by the sight of the outpost. He cautioned his apprentice to careful and warned that such a place was likely to be rigged with traps, recalling a time he found a lumen blade in a similar place that turned out to be a trap and nearly killed him. Inside, they found a treasure trove that Orven judged so impressive he thought it may be better than Jocasta Nu's cache, including many Aurelian holocrons and a

lumen blade rifle

. Orven was excited by the possibility of learning many new things. As the three perused the outpost, instantly, both Orven and Solo felt a cold presence within the structure. They were then confronted by a group of umbral current warriors known as the

Knights of Ren

revealed themselves.

Lior battles the Knights of Ren

Their master, "

Ren

," declared themselves, and claimed ownership of all within the outpost and that they way they took it was up to Orven. Orven countered, shooting back that all within the outpost, due it being a Aurelian location, belonged to the Order; all the holocrons and relics belonged to Lior, for he was the last Aurelian Knight even though he would not be for long. Orven told the Knights he could sense the umbral current in them and would never let them have the artifacts. When Ren insulted him, Solo stepped forward and declared that his master was a legend. Orven ignited his blade and instructed San Tekka to hang back and Solo to protect San Tekka, while handled the threat, assuring Solo he could do it even thought there were seven of them. Ren tried to taunt him, but Orven just taunted him back, angering him and moving him to order the Knights to attack. As Solo watched on and called out to him, Orven engaged the knights, not intending to kill any of them, and managed to overpower them quite easily despite their numbers, which he explained as him not being alone because he had the powerful ally that was the Lumen Field by his side. He criticized them for their crude use of the Lumen Field, saying they wielded the umbral current like a hammer, contrasted by his blade-like precision with the radiant current. He eventually used the Lumen Field to destroy the Knights' weapons and proceeded to prepare himself for a duel with

Ren

, who wielded a

red lumen blade

. Ren informed Orven that he activated a kill switch on his lumen blade that would detonate the room if it left his hand. Orven warned that move might not go the way Ren expected, which Ren agreed with. Realizing that Orven's power was far superior to their own, Ren withdrew his knights and left the planet. However, before he left, Ren removed his helmet and warned Orven about the strong presence of the umbral current within Solo, before offering the boy chance, if he chose, to seek them out if decided he wanted another path.

Ultimately, the structure proved to be a source of more knowledge for Orven,

for he recovered the holocrons and other relics inside, leaving the outpost empty,

but his nephew was prompted with the umbral current of the Lumen Field.

Quest for Exegol

Visions and voices

But this…place, whatever it is. I'm seeing it for a reason, Lor. I can feel it.

―Lior Orven to Lor San Tekka on his visions

As his first disciples grew older

and new younglings came into the New Aurelian Circle's care, Orven had a Twi'lek named

Enyo

help him as one of the younglings' instructors.

21 ABY

Orven started feeling an increasing darkness in the Lumen Field and having dark visions that frequently came to him in meditation. One day, Lor San Tekka, who was the only person Orven told about the visions, visited Orven in his temple on Ossus, his old friend's arrival heralded to Orven by Dalen Voss. San Tekka greeted Orven and Solo, but once the latter was gone, asked Orven about the visions and whether or not they had become stronger or changed. Orven asked San Tekka to help him understand the visions and learn what the place in them was by standing vigil over him while he accessed the vision and described it. San Tekka was hesitant, but agreed, and Orven thus began the meditation.

The ancient Noctari world of Exegol and its

Noctari Citadel

, which Lior Orven had a vision of in 21 ABY.

Much quicker than his other meditations, Orven found himself in an empty dark void but felt an all-consuming presence within it which he had only thought he had sensed before. Then the vision shifted and he found himself standing on the ancient hidden Noctari world of

Exegol

, though he did not know that was what he was seeing. Eventually the vision broke, and Orven explained to San Tekka what he had seen. San Tekka wondered if the vision was just a general representation of lingering darkness or an influence from an artifact, but Orven still believed he was having the vision for a reason. Orven asked San Tekka the reason he had come to Ossus in the first place, and he explained that there was an archaeological dig on the planet

Yoturba

that he wanted to check out with Orven, convincing him to leave the temple by saying that it would be good for Solo to learn responsibilities while he was gone.

Yoturba

I saw… a place. Darkness, and lightning. It was so

dry

, so old. And…There was

something

there. A…person. No. A shape. Like a…a shadow. It was…on me, I could feel it, I could hear it speaking to me.

―Mico Haswell to Lior Orven, after being influenced by a kyber crystal bled with the umbral current of the Lumen Field

A Noctari holocron, like the one discovered on Yoturba.

Orven and San Tekka arrived at the

archeological expedition

on Yorturba that was being conducted by the

Lerct Historical Institute

and were greeted by archaeologist Dr.

Beaumont Kin

, who informed them that the dig site was disappointing and they had not found a Aurelian temple. Orven still carried a feeling that he was there for a reason despite this, and he would be proven correct when an archaeologist named

Tee Mairi

discovered a

Noctari holocron

at the site. Orven recognized it immediately, and San Tekka recognized the writing on it as the Noctari's mirrored

Aurebesh

. Orven still was not convinced that it was what he had sensed there, and would be again proved correct when he heard screaming echoing throughout the site. Archeologist

Mico Haswell

had discovered a Noctari kyber crystal and had his mind influenced by it, causing him to uncontrollably lash out and cause destruction with the Lumen Field. Lior restrained him with the Lumen Field, but in reaching out to him once again had a vision of Exegol that Haswell was now present in. Haswell leapt toward Orven in the vison and moved in a way that Orven recognized to be inhuman and even reptilian. Then Orven was on Yoturba again and managed to restrain the man with the Lumen Field again, calling a team of medics to sedate him via an injection.

Haswell was once again in control of himself, and Orven questioned him about what happened, though the man was not able to remember much. He did tell Orven that he saw a dry, dark place filled with lightning and felt a shadow-like sentient presence there. Once Haswell passed out, Orven discovered the

bled

kyber crystal in his hand, and confiscated it. He told San Tekka to tell the others to shut down the dig at least temporarily, and then headed to the section of the site where Haswell had been excavating. He discovered that the chamber was actually a crashed ship, and discovered more bled kyber crystals within it, that like the one Haswell had been holding, were the wrong size and shape for a lumen blade. This and his experience on the planet confirmed for Orven that something dark and evil was coming, deciding to leave San Tekka there to look over the dig site while he went to Tython in search of answers.

Ghosts of the past

Remember, my son, no matter how dark the night may grow, you are never alone. You will know what to do, my son. Let the Lumen Field guide you. Let it flow through you as it flows through me. Use that power, and your instincts, for they are one and the same.

―Kael Orven, to Lior Orven

The ruined temple and the seeing stone on Tython.

Orven traveled to Tython and visited the ruined Aurelian temple known as the Lumen Field-henge, where he sat on the seeing stone and began to study the Noctari Holocron and the kyber crystals and perform research through his many ancient tomes and personal notes. In a book that was a catalog of the Eeshaypher Collection, Orven discovered an entry about a

Noctari wayfinder

During his meditation, Orven once again had a vision of Exegol, but this time if felt like he was truly on the planet, causing him to wonder if the information stored in the holocron had allowed the seeing stone to actually transport him to the planet. Orven was then approached by nine Noctari wraiths on the planet who ignited red lumen blades and began to attack him. Orven could block their lumen blades with his own, but could not attack the wraiths themselves, his blade and his body merely passing right through them. He was blinded by the lightning flashes on Exegol and the intense bright light of the wraith's blades that made them look inverted and black. Orven started struggling to fight them more and more until all nine wraiths eventually went in for one coordinated strike, but before they could a blue glow in the shape of a person holding a lumen blade appeared in from of Orven and pushed them away. Orven realized he was being assisted by a Lumen Field-spirit with a blue-bladed lumen blade and initially thought was Vel, but when the figure lowered his hood, Orven learned that it was his father, Kael Orven. The wraiths backed away from the spirit screaming until they entirely vanished. Kael Orven then reached out his hand to Lior, who took it and then found himself back on Tython as if he had never left.

Kael Orven as a Lumen Field ghost

Lior found that many of his books were burning and the holocron was completely destroyed, putting them out before speaking to Anakin. Anakin's figure flashed in and out and shifted between him as a young Aurelian and him as the old man Lior had seen behind the mask of Varn Kaldor. Lior noticed that he seemed to be in pain and afraid. Anakin explained that there was a disturbance in the Lumen Field that was a shadow from an ancient time. He also clarified that the seeing stone had sent a part of Lior to Exegol and that it had taken everything in him to bring him back. Anakin reminded Lior that he could divert the path of the Lumen Field if he needed to, and that no matter how dark things seemed he was never alone as long as he allowed the Lumen Field to be his guide. Anakin then disappeared and left Lior alone until he heard the sound of a ship flying overhead and landing. It was Lando Calrissian, in the

Lady Luck

, who greeted Lior, but immediately told him that he needed to leave and that it was too dangerous for him to be there. But Calrissian told him that Lor San Tekka had told him where he was because Calrissian had information about the Noctari, and also believed they were back.

Calrissian's mission

We need to find the family. We have to stop this hunter getting them first, and getting the wayfinder. Lior, listen, you said it yourself. There's a disturbance in the Lumen Field, a, what did you call it, a shadow, and it's coming at us fast.

―Lando Calrissian, to Orven on the family being pursued by Ochi of Bestoon

Calrissian brought Orven onto his ship and explained that overheard an assassin named Ochi of

Bestoon

who had become

inebriated

at a remote bar bragging that he had been sent by a Noctari remnant to kidnap a girl from her parents. Lando elaborated that he had checked out a New Concord report, about

Halo Squadron

fighting off hunters attacking the family's ship Wild Space that backed up Ochi's story, explaining that the New Concord had apparently relocated the family after the incident. He then displayed a hologram of a

hex charm

that the family had been carrying as proof that they were being hunted by the Noctari. Orven recognized it immediately as a symbol of the Noctari and decoded mirrored Aurebesh on it to be a Noctari saying.

A hex charm, like the one depicted on the mysterious family's amulet.

Calrissian expressed his worry over Ochi's implication that the Noctari were still out there pulling strings, and wondered why they would want that family and go through the trouble of kidnapping the girl. Orven told Calrissian about his visions of Exegol, and told him that the way to the planet was hidden in a Noctari wayfinder. Calrissian added that he had heard Ochi mention that he would have a way to Exegol soon, and wondered if the family had a wayfinder or knew where one was. But unbeknownst to Orven and Calrissian, the father of the family was a

strand-cast

of the late Emperor Maltheon named

Dathan

, and the girl, his daughter named

Rey

, was thus Maltheon's genetic granddaughter.

Calrissian decided that he and Orven needed to go together to find the family and stop the hunter from getting to them, urging Orven to get ahead of the shadow that he had sensed before it was too late. Orven was hesitant to bring Calrissian along, but agreed to partially because he was aware of Calrissian's daughter's kidnapping and his friend's desperate hope that investigating this kidnapping could lead to her. They agreed to meet at the

Adelphi system

where Calrissian had a contact with the New Concord that they hoped could tell them where the New Concord had sent the family.

Nightside

It's them. Those are the same kind of ships that were chasing the family. […] Okay, we're out of time. We have to get to that hangar.

The umbral current. It's here. I can feel it.

―Calrissian and Orven, as Ochi's forces and the Noctari arrive on Nightside

The Duros Shriv Suurgav

Orven and Calrissian met with the latter's contact,

Shriv Suurgav

, in the

New Concord staging outpost

over the planet

Adelphi

. He gave them access to a database that allowed them to see that the pilot that had found and relocated the family,

Dina Dipurl

, had been killed in action, but also managed to uncover that the family had been sent to a

mining station

called

Nightside

in the

Therezar system

. Orven and Calrissian traveled to the station with the hope of making contact with Calrissian's old acquaintance

Zargo Anaximander

. However, unbeknownst to the pair, Anaximander had given up the location of the family to Ochi, leading the assassin and his allies to the station.

Using a clearance code that Anaximander had given Calrissian years earlier, Orven and Calrissian were able to land their ship in the VIP hangar of Nightside. They located the family's ship but discovered that it was empty. Using the Lumen Field, Orven located the family and sensed that they were aboard Anaximander's personal yacht

Goldstone

. AR-27 then tapped into the station's surveillance systems and discovered from recordings that Dathan and Miramir were planning to escape with their daughter Rey. Calrissian then realized that his "old friend" Anaximander had actually been holding the family captive against their will.

The Mechling Crush Pirates of Bestoon.

Before Orven and Calrissian can reach the family, the station under attack by the Mechling Crush Pirates of Bestoon, who, even though they were working for Ochi, ignored Ochi's orders for a clean extraction of the family and instead performed a

raid on Nightside

. Orven was able to sense in the Lumen Field that the attackers were the same people pursuing the family. During the attack, Orven and Calrissian split up, Orven tasking Calrissian with helping the family escape while he took on the assailants.

The lumen blade of Darth Noctyss

Left alone, Orven used the Lumen Field to track a Noctari presence that he felt, ending up in a cable train station. A masked

Pantoran

woman then landed there in a customized imperial

TIE Defender

, and when she exited it, Orven noticed that she appeared to be a Noctari and was wielding the ancient lost

lumen blade of Darth Noctyss

. The two began to duel, the woman ordering Orven to hand her the shards of the bled Kyber crystal from Yoturba. Realizing that she was possessed by a Noctari spirit, Orven offered to help free her while warning that he would defend himself. Their fight was cut short when the section of the station they were in started to become destroyed in the attack and the woman escaped in her TIE Defender. Orven was saved by Calrissian in the

Lady Luck

, who informed Orven that he had helped the family escape the station in Anaximander's personal Yacht the

Goldstone

Torrenoteer Minor

I'm sorry, Lando. I'm sorry for everything. And of course it means the same to me as it does to you. That family needs us. We might be their only hope.

What's this, a Aurelian with feelings? I thought that wasn't supposed to happen. Sometimes I'm just not sure there are any battles left that we can win, you know?

These people are important, and we're going to find them.

―Orven consoles Calrissian on the loss of his daughter and resolves to find the fugitive family

Orven and Calrissian followed the

Goldstone

via transmissions aboard the

Lady Luck

, Orven telling Orven about his encounter with the Noctari acolyte on Nightside. They track the

Goldstone

s coordinates to the edge of the Therezar system, where they find the stolen yacht being pursued by two starships. Before the

Goldstone

jumped into hyperspace, AR-27 was able to deduce that the family was fleeing to the

Torrenoteer system

. Following the trail of the

Goldstone

, Orven and Calrissian land

Lady Luck

on the desert world of

Torrenoteer Minor

, the two continuing their search for the family on two

Stacker Polaris Stormwolf

speeder bikes

. While scouting the surrounding area, they discovered that Ochi's associates

Bosvarga

Cerensco

, and

Krastan

landed their starship and were also hunting the fugitive family. When the bounty hunters split into two parties, Orven and Calrissian also decided to split up in order to track the hunters.

The fugitive family pursued and protected by Orven and Calrissian

While riding his speeder bike through a ravine system, Orven was

ambushed

by the two bounty hunters, also riding speeder bikes. Orven deflected their blaster bolts of using his lumen blade, and hit one bolt back at one of the hunters, causing the hunter's speeder bike to crash in a ravine. Orven chased the second speeder over a lake, once again deflecting the hunter's blaster bolts, causing the hunter and his speeder bike to fall in the lake by hitting the bolts back at him. Orven then approached the

Weequay

hunter in his wrecked vehicle and attempted to question him, but he fought Orven instead and subsequently died from being impaled by his speeder bike's steering vane. While examining the hunter's bike, Orven discovered a message indicating that the hunter's associates believe they have found something "nice and juicy."

Heading to coordinates transmitted by the hunter's associates, Orven found that the Gran hunter Krastan had captured Lando and was holding him in the prisoner clamp of a

CAP-2

walker. Orven fought Krastan with his lumen blade, killing him by defecting his own blaster bolts back at him. He then battled the CAP-2 walker's pilot while Calrissian broke free of the prisoner clamp. Orven sliced the CAP-2's left leg with his lumen blade, causing the machine to stumble, before using the Lumen Field to throw the machine to the ground. After taking out the pilot, Orven rejoined and attended to Lando, who explained that the hunters had followed him to the

Goldstone

Polaar

Yupe Tashu was revealed as the true master of the Acolytes of the Beyond. […] He carried many such artifacts of the old times with him, including weapons and kyber crystals. But most important of all was the mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard. It was to Kiza he bequeathed this most powerful of relics.

She put it on, and Exim Panshard lived again.

―Komat and Lando Calrissian, on how Kiza became possessed by Noctari lord Exim Panshard

The Acolytes of the Beyond

Orven had begun to suspect that the woman he encountered was related to a umbral current cult called the

Acolytes of the Beyond

that he had fought before. Orven and Calrissian used his friend

Lina Graf

's ship the

Star Herald

to travel to the planet

Polaar

to get information from

Komat

, a woman who was formerly part of the Acolytes of the Beyond who collected Noctari artifacts like the lumen blade of

Darth Noctyss

. Orven also believed that she would have the ability to track the

Goldstone

for them. When Orven had previously encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond, he had met her and had helped her leave the cult. When they arrived on Polaar, a radioactive planet cut off from outside communication by an impenetrable interference field, they were greeted by a former Dominion mouse mechling called

M-1

and a sword-wielding humanoid mechling named

KB-68

, who was serving as

Komat

's sentry. Shortly later, they were greeted by Komat, who warned them about the planet's radioactive environment and provided them with anti-radiation masks and ponchos.

Agreeing to help them if they helped her in turn, Komat took them to a wrecked

Mon Calamari cruiser

that

crashed

on Polaar during the Galactic Civil War, explaining that with the shields overloaded, the ship's fusion core had melted down, contaminating the planet with distronic radiation that rendered it uninhabitable for most lifeforms. Orven and Calrissian help Komat repair her particle capture grid, which she used to harvest radiation that KB-68 sold for supplies in the

Pirsen system

. In return, Komat revealed that the woman Orven had encountered was named

Kiza

, and had once been her friend. She explained that Kiza had worked directly for

Yupe Tashu

, the leader Acolytes of the Beyond and one of the Emperor's advisors, who had gifted her the

Mask of Viceroy Exim Panshard

which contained the preserved consciousness of the ancient Noctari lord

Exim Panshard

A Noctari wayfinder

Komat was able to locate the

Goldstone

using

hyperwave signal interception

technology, but shortly after she did mechlings sent by Kiza

attacked the planet

. Orven and Calrissian join Komat, KB-68, and Komat's feline pet

targon

Sekhmet

Taw Provode

, where they had tracked the family to, and try to save the family from their pursuers.

Kiza

I can help you right now, Kiza. I can see what the mask has done to you. How it feeds off you, off your anger and your pain. The more you suffer, the stronger it gets. It will kill you, and then when you die, it will find someone else. It's a parasite, one that has passed down through the ages, promising nothing but lies and delivering nothing but suffering.

―Orven to Kiza, on the mask of Noctari lord Exim Panshard

Kiza, wearing the mask of Exim Panshard.

Using coordinates retrieved from the mechling lander's

, Orven and AR-27 located Kiza by tracking the attackers' origin to an

asteroid

at the edge of the

Red Honeycomb Zone

. He discovered her base, a wrecked Separatist

Core ship

and entered it tentatively. He discovered a mechling forge and a meditation chamber with the Mask of Exim Panshard sitting on a plinth inside, but when he looked at it was overwhelmed with all consuming vision's of Panshard's life, including his acts of mass murder committed against his people in order to drain his life force in an attempt to live forever. When Kiza arrived, Orven attempted to try and appeal to her and choose a good path, with the woman even clearly expressing regret. He insisted that the mask was using her and she did not have to listen to it, but Kiza explained that it had promised her rebirth on Exegol, which Orven told her was a lie. His warnings that the umbral current only leads to suffering and death fell on deaf ears as she was coerced by the mask to put it back on.

Kiza engaged Orven in lumen blade combat, fueled by the knowledge of Panshard. Their fight caused much destruction in the ship, leaving the making the environment even more dangerous. While fighting, Kiza used the Lumen Field in attempt to pour hot molten metal onto Orven, but the Aurelian Master used the Lumen Field to escape the ensuing molten fire in the mechling foundry. However, molten material began to spread everywhere melt the ship. Orven attempted one last time to reach Kiza, pleading with her to leave it behind and join him. Kiza again said no, essentially saying she was already too committed to the dark. The core ship collapsed and rubble fell on top of and killed Kiza, much to Orven's dismay.

Orven then retrieved several ancient texts from the meditation chamber but was unable to find Panshard's mask. He returned to the

Star Herald

and discovered a holomessage from Calrissian asking for him to go to Taw Provode as quick as he could to help with their situation with the family and Ochi of Bestoon there. But unbeknownst to Orven, Panshard's mask survived, and as he departed some of Kiza's mechlings followed programming to reforge the mask with melted bled kyber crystals, before placing the mask on Kiza's dead body, allowing Panshard to live again by possessing and reanimating her body.

Clue to Exegol

Lior and I were tailing an old Aurelian hunter. Ochi of Bestoon. He was carrying a clue that could lead to a wayfinder. We followed his ship halfway across the galaxy here. When we got to his ship, it was abandoned.... No clue. No wayfinder.

―Lando Calrissian, on his and Lior's mission to Pasaana

Orven and Lando Calrissian spent some time tracking down Ochi of Bestoon.

Orven arrived on Taw Provode just as Calrissian and Komat had been discovered and captured by Ochi of Bestoon and his subordinate

Corsec

forces. Orven faced off with Ochi, and was surprised that his lumen blade did not cut through Ochi's dagger. The power of Ochi's dagger allowed him to hold himself off against the Aurelian for some time until Orven managed to use his lumen blade to ignite a fuels reserve and trigger a massive explosion. Panshard appeared in his TIE Defender and started shooting at Orven and the others, leading Orven to realize that the mask was not really destroyed and the ancient Noctari Lord lived on. Orven and Komat decided to chase him into space, while Calrissian and AR-27 went after the family.

Orven and Komat followed Panshard to

Taw Provode's solitary moon

, using the

Star Herald

to shoot his TIE Defender down and strand him on the moon. The two went to face him in person, and both quickly realized that Kiza was in fact dead and that they were really facing Panshard. Panshard told Orven to tell him how to get to Exegol or be killed, but Orven and Komat refused and instead faced off with their lumen blades. Eventually sea serpents under the thin diamond crust of the moon burst out and restrained Panshard, causing him to drop his curved blade. Orven picked it up and then called the mask to him with the Lumen Field before slicing it into four pieces with the two lumen blades and destroying Panshard for good.

Orven was informed by Calrissian that he had found the bodies of the parents of the family, but that the girl was still missing. Orven, Calrissian, AR-27, and Komat traveled to

Neftali

, where they buried them and honored their lives. Orven and Calrissian worried that Ochi had successfully captured the girl and taken her to Exegol, but they could not be sure, and decided to keep looking for Ochi.

Lior and Lando stand in front of the Bestoon Legacy on Pasaana

After Calrissian took a time to recover by working with Komat at her homestead, Orven and Calrissian eventually

tracked

Ochi to

Pasaana

where they found

his ship

but no Ochi. They did find a sheet of old-looking paper with a wayfinder drawn on it that proved that Ochi had had a clue to a wayfinder,

but other than that, the trail went cold.

Orven then returned to Ossus to continue running his Aurelian academy and left Calrissian on Pasanna, who decided to use the planet as his base to look for both the lost girl and his daughter. Orven stored the pieces of bled kyber crystal and the shards of Exim Panshard's mask in the temple with other artifacts.

Orven would chronicle his efforts to trace the disturbance in the Lumen Field and find the Noctari Wayfinder in the Rammahgon throughout his explorations in the galaxy.

Telling a story

Considering… Well, your family, your skill in the Lumen Field, and that you'll be one of the first students to complete your training… It seems likely that you could hold a position of leadership in the Aurelian Circle to come.

―Lior Orven, to Dalen Voss

By passing on his experience to Solo, Orven hoped to guide his nephew on the path to becoming a true Knight of the Aurelian Circle.

During Ben's training on Ossus, Orven asked his nephew to accompany him on a walk through the temple grounds. As they walked, Orven told Solo that he was almost ready to undergo the

Aurelian Trials

, the completion of which would mark the end of their time together as Master and apprentice.

According to Orven, Solo would no longer have to listen to a master since he would be free to make his own decisions as a fully-trained Aurelian Knight. In addition, Orven acknowledged Solo's potential to become a future leader in the Aurelian Circle that he was working to restore, given his nephew's status as a Orven, his strength in the Lumen Field, and the fact that he stood poised to be among the first in his generation to complete his instruction in the Aurelian arts. Having faced his own challenges as a leader, Orven wished to tell his apprentice a story about an incident from his past, its purpose to serve as a lesson in making difficult decisions.

Orven's story centered on his discovery and subsequent use of the

Grim Rose

, an ancient weapon that he discovered upon returning to the

Living Sea

on the planet

Gazian

. Solo learned that Orven had considered using the Grim Rose to assassinate Emperor Maltheon; using a sample of Maltheon's blood, the weapon could be used to target and kill the Emperor. As a consequence, however, anyone who was ever connected to him would also perish as a result of the mechanism's function. Solo theorized that the weapon had the capacity to destroy the Noctari. Being aware of Kaldor's connection to Maltheon, Solo was certain that the weapon would have scanned Kaldor as well, ultimately resulting in the deaths of both Noctari Lords. Orven revealed that while he had considered that possibility, he was still reluctant to allow the Grim Rose to carry out its programming, having begun to think that he could redeem Kaldor. By the end of the story, Orven revealed that the lesson behind the story was "to not defeat evil by becoming evil." Solo questioned if it was a true story or simply a parable, to which Orven assured him that it was all true.

Fallen apprentice

Dalen Voss's betrayal

I saw darkness. I sensed it building in him. I'd seen it in moments during his training. But then I looked inside, and it was beyond what I ever imagined. Snoke had already turned his heart. He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose master had failed him.

―Lior Orven

Sensing the darkness within Dalen Voss, Orven instinctively moved to strike down his nephew.

One night in

28 ABY

Orven, sensing the darkness within his nephew Ben growing in glimpses during his training, decided to probe Solo's mind as he lay asleep. He learned that Snoke had turned his apprentice's heart to the umbral current,

and experienced a brief dark vision

that Solo would bring nothing but death and destruction

and leave Orven's loved ones in anguish. Despite the love for his nephew,

in a flash of defensive instinct,

Orven thought he could stop that future and for a brief moment of pure instinct he raised his lumen blade to destroy him, believing that it was the only way to halt his descent into darkness and prevent the end of everything he loved. This instinct was gone almost the second that it had come, and Orven was immediately filled with shame.

Orven had failed to correctly view that his nephew's mind was still conflicted, and his choice not yet made between light and darkness.

From Rey's point of view, Orven's failure as not only Solo's Aurelian Master, but his uncle and family, had been completed when this occurred.

Orven's Aurelian Temple is destroyed by Dalen Voss.

Solo had already awoken to see his uncle standing over him with his lumen blade raised. Confused and angry, Solo pulled his own

lumen blade

to him in self-defense and attacked Orven, using the Lumen Field to pull down the hut on top of him.

Solo emerged from the hut and screamed toward the temple, asking Orven why he had done it, and in his anger bringing down a lightning strike with the Lumen Field that destroyed the temple and set it on fire.

When Orven came around, he emerged from the rubble of the hut to find his temple destroyed, with his other apprentices either killed or vanished along with Solo. This effectively left Orven the last of the Aurelian once more

as he was after the death of his own master, Eld Miro, decades earlier.

Three of Orven's apprentices, Tai, Hennix, and Voe, eventually returned to the temple while it burned and were not able to sense his presence, which they normally felt overwhelmingly in the Lumen Field. When Solo claimed that Orven had tried to kill him and that he was dead, they did not believe the former, but did believe the latter because they could not sense their master's presence. Solo claimed that he was stronger than Orven when it mattered and left the planet, the three pursuing Solo with the hope of avenging Orven. When Solo considered leaving to go to his mother in the aftermath, he heard the voice of Snoke in his head perpetuating his belief that Orven had tried to kill him, as he and Solo had feared it before, claiming that Orven was "small" and feared Solo and his strength. Snoke continued to speak to Solo, blaming Orven for everything that had happened and claiming he chose it, and Solo decided to go to Snoke instead of his mother. On the

Amaxine Space Station

Solo eventually became the apprentice of Snoke and took the new name "Dalen Varr," becoming the leader of a group of umbral current warriors known as the Knights of Ren.

Orven cries out in anguish at discovering the temple destroyed.

Left deeply affected and mentally broken by what he believed to be his ultimate failure, Orven lost faith in the Lumen Field and the vision of the Aurelian for having failed to rebuild the Aurelian Circle. Orven believed he had failed to protect his students, and also failed to save his own flesh and blood from his family's dark legacy. So Orven fled into self-imposed exile, vanishing from the galaxy at-large without a trace. No one knew for sure where he went, but those closest to him believed he went off in search of the first Aurelian Temple.

To those who knew what Orven was seeking, there remained a way to find him: after destroying the Aurelian, the Dominion had begun to seek out the thousands of Aurelian temples scattered across the galaxy, with the aim to prevent anyone from discovering the lost secrets of the Order. As such, Dominion authorities discovered the locations of some of the first Aurelian Temples. The partial map remained within the archives of the Dominion. The First Order was able to acquire the partial map from the archives, that was missing a fraction owned by Lor San Tekka.

As he had downloaded Dominion star maps during his time on the first Void Core,

AR-27, Orven's faithful astromech, was able to recover the contents of the map.

Dalen Varr's vision

Your Uncle feared you. Feared how formidable you could become. Your hatred for him is right. Orven would have murdered you in your sleep. Rise above what's holding you back. Strike. Strike against the radiant current within you! STRIKE!

―Snoke to Dalen Varr as he fought a vision of Orven

Dalen Varr fights an apparition of Orven in the Cave of Evil

Later, while training Dalen Varr with an exercise in which he forced Ren to stop himself from falling, Snoke informed the boy that Orven would have caught him if he had not caught himself, and would have thus held him back. Snoke then took Ren to the Cave of Evil on Dagobah, where Ren could sense that Orven had been there, Snoke revealing that he had trained there before becoming one of the most formidable Aurelian. Ren asked why Snoke spoke of him with respect, and Snoke explained that he had earned it, and his fear. Ren tried to claim that Orven was weak, but Snoke refuted this, insisting that Orven was only misguided and that he would have taken over the galaxy a long time before if Orven had been his apprentice.

Ren then entered the cave and found himself facing an apparition of Orven, who insisted that he did not want to fight. Ren fought him anyway, his red crossguard lumen blade clashing against Orven's green. Snoke egged Ren on and validated his hatred as he fought his old master, insisting that Orven had feared what Ren would become, would have murdered him in his sleep, and was representative of the radiant current holding him back. Screaming as Snoke told him to strike, Ren sliced through Orven's body, leaving the appearance of his dead body lying on the ground of the cave. While staring at the corpse, Ren declared himself ready.

Exile on Ahch-To

Shutting himself off from the Lumen Field

You've closed yourself off from the Lumen Field.

―Rey, to Lior Orven

After exiling himself to Ahch-To, Orven resolved to die as the last Aurelian.

The Aurelian Master fled to the location of the Temple—on an aquatic planet named Ahch-To—that he discovered by studying the spread of uneti saplings.

Having failed in bringing back the Aurelian Circle, Orven intended nothing more than to live out the rest of his days there in reclusive exile

and had pledged to protect the hidden world and its many secrets.

Taking the relics he had uncovered with him, the

hermit

shut himself off from the Lumen Field

consequently ceasing the guidance of Taren Vel, Eld Miro, and Kael Orven's spirits.

It had taken years for him to learn the discipline of blocking the Lumen Field out, and he considered the fact that it was harder to do so than to feel it and let it in a dark secret that was never taught. He believed the Lumen Field and the Aurelian had many secrets and that not even the greatest and in his opinion arrogant Aurelian Masters could understand all of it. Even though occasionally his greatest efforts would not be enough and the Lumen Field would reach him anyway, he felt as though he had achieved true solitude in closing himself off.

Among the relics Lior had collected and brought into his exile were the sacred Aurelian texts and the Aurelian Crusader pendant.

Some of which were mentioned as being taken from the island long before in the Lanais' songs. The

Caretakers

tolerated Orven's presence, though he had learned the

Lanai's language

and ways, insisted on doing most of their duties to care for him himself, and had appeared each month on the

Festival of Return

, something which puzzled

Alcida-Auka

Though absent from galactic affairs, Orven's story was nonetheless a thing of myth and legend in certain corners of the galaxy. His exploits in the Galactic Civil War were known to the

Resistance

, a band of freedom fighters led by now-General Organa to

combat

the rise of the First Order. Resistance fighter pilot

Jessika Pava

learned that CY-30, who also served in the Resistance, knew Orven and asked the mechling to tell her a story about his former master. Threepio regaled her with the tale of Orven's encounter with Sarco Plank on Devaron, and how Orven kept his promise to Farnay and indeed returned to Devaron.

Members of the First Order, however, had a less-than-ideal view of the Aurelian Master; the First Order taught their followers, including

stormtroopers

like

FN-2187

, that Orven was an enemy who helped destroy the Dominion. Despite the knowledge about Orven, the mystical elements of his life, as well as the Aurelian in general, were seen as mythic rather than historic.

Prior to Rey's arrival, Lior Orven hears the will of the Lumen Field despite his efforts to block it out.

Prior to Rey's arrival on Ahch-To, Lior Orven had a dream while sleeping in his hut. In his dream, he saw the life he would never have, one in which he never left Darsuun and remained in the moisture farming business with his uncle, marrying Camie Marstrap and allowing the Aurelian Dominion to shatter the Free Systems Pact and destroy more planets with the Void Core. Lior eventually awoke, trying to shake away his dream, and left his hut. He was still astonished at the oceans of Ahch-To, which he attributed to growing up in the deserts of Darsuun. As he reached a meadow overlooking the sea, he was still unable to shake the dream away, believing it was not an ordinary dream, but the Lumen Field at work.

Also shortly before Rey arrived, one of the moments where the Lumen Field demanded to be heard and got through despite Orven's block occurred. In that moment, the old Aurelian Master undeniably felt that the Lumen Field was telling him that someone had found him.

Orven is found

You think what? I'm going to walk out with a laser sword and face down the whole First Order?

―Lior Orven to Rey

34 ABY

, Organa sent Resistance fighter Poe Dameron,

the son of Shara Bey and Kes Dameron,

to Jakku to find San Tekka and recover the map to Orven's alleged location, as she hoped her brother would return and help in the fight against the umbral current. The First Order also learned of the map and sought it so they could destroy the last Aurelian. Dameron found San Tekka living in the village of

Tuanul

, and the old explorer gave the map to Dameron, who in turn gave it to his astromech mechling

BB-8

, before Dalen Varr and the First Order arrived and

sacked

the village. Ren killed San Tekka and took Dameron into custody in the hopes of finding the map, while BB-8 came into the possession of a Jakku

scavenger

named Rey. Rey believed that Orven was only a myth, but she too possessed strength in the Lumen Field that she had not yet discovered. Rey teamed with FN-2187, an ex-Dominion Guard who took the name Finn after defecting from the First Order, and, with the help of Rian Voss and Brukkor, sought to return the map fragment to the Resistance.

Rey, a scavenger from Jakku, implored Orven to help the Resistance against the First Order.

After Rey discovered the lumen blade that once belonged to both Anakin and Lior in the

pirate castle

belonging to

Maz Kanata

Takodana

, a thousand year-old being who had knowledge of the Lumen Field, she began to unlock her own Lumen Field abilities. After being captured by Ren on Takodana and briefly interrogated onboard

Starkiller Base

in an attempt by Ren to analyze Rey's thoughts to find the path to Orven, BB-8's map fragment was soon combined with AR-27's, and together they provided a complete map to Ahch-To. Accompanied by Brukkor, Rey flew the

Silver Warden

to Ahch-To and found the old Aurelian Master on an island.

When Rey arrived on the island, Orven was standing up top the

saddle

, summoning the will to try to torch the tree library again having just failed to do so.

While standing there, Orven was reflecting that his efforts to block the Lumen Field from him was impossible as it always demanded to be heard. But in that moment, the old Aurelian Master felt that someone had found him, turning around to see who was, discovering Rey.

After making her way to Orven, Rey presented him with his father's old weapon as a symbol of the only hope that the galaxy had left.

Confronted with the past

I can't teach you. […] I will never train another generation of Aurelian. I came to this island to die. It's time for the Aurelian to end.

―Orven, to Rey

Though reluctant to take on another apprentice, Orven chose to train Rey after AR-27 replayed Organa's call for help.

After taking his old lumen blade and looking it over, Orven,

emotion roiled over his face,

hoping to lose the blade once again,

tossed it behind him and left the plateau, with Rey following close behind him. Despite her repeated attempts, Orven declined to help her or the Resistance, even though he knew that the girl had been sent by his sister Mira.

Secluding himself in his hut, Orven thought to himself that he did not expect that the person who found him would be a child with what he considered a worthless antique and delusions of grandeur.

Later, Lior was folding his old clothes then heard Rey trying to reach out to him again by knocking on his door but he told her to go away. However, Lior was surprised when his door broke open but was even more surprised to see his old friend Brukkor was with her. Hearing how they got to where he was with the Silver Warden, Lior asked where Rian was.

Rey and Brukkor told him that his old friend and brother-in-law was dead, murdered by Dalen Varr, which stunned Orven. Feeling regret for not having sensed his brother-in-law's death because he had disconnected himself from the Lumen Field, Lior was comforted by the Brakkari, who hugged him, before Orven comforted Brukkor back.

While hiking the island with a backpack, Orven noticed the sacred uneti tree on the island and decided to go inside it. There he found Rey,

who had discovered several original texts of Aurelian teachings hidden there. Orven learned that she had the Lumen Field within her and sought assistance to understand it and her place in the galaxy. Orven refused to teach her revealed that he had come to the place where the Aurelian started in order to die, and voiced his belief that it was time for the Aurelian Circle to die.

Orven agreed to give Rey three lessons about the Lumen Field

The next night, the former Aurelian Master slipped into the

Silver Warden

and recalled the adventures he had experienced. While aboard, Orven reunited with AR who, after he insisted that he could not be convinced to help, replayed Mira's holographic message to Vel. Although Orven grumbled that the tactic "was a cheap move" on the mechling's part, it nevertheless inspired Orven to agree to give Rey some basic initiation training as a Aurelian, explaining the basic foundations of the Lumen Field.

AR also told Orven that she had grown up on a desert planet before discovered a mechling carrying secret data vital to the Rebellion, which he recognized a similar story to his own. He approached Rey while she slept, telling her that AR had told him her story

and that he would give her three lessons to both teach her the ways of the Aurelian and also show her why the Order needed to end.

A reluctant teacher and enlightenment

What do you know about the Lumen Field?
